### [19] [OA-NBV: Occlusion-Aware Next-Best-View Planning for Human-Centered Active Perception on Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11072)
*Boxun Hu,Chang Chang,Jiawei Ge,Man Namgung,Xiaomin Lin,Axel Krieger,Tinoosh Mohsenin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OA-NBV是一种面向移动机器人的遮挡感知最优视点规划方法，专门针对被遮挡人体的观测优化，通过目标中心可见性模型评分可行视点，显著提升观测成功率与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类中心操作（如搜索、分类、灾难响应）中，杂乱环境和部分可见性会降低下游感知性能。现有NBV方法主要优化通用探索或长时程覆盖，未针对实时运动约束下获取被遮挡人员可用观测这一具体目标进行优化。

Method: 提出遮挡感知NBV管道（OA-NBV），集成感知与运动规划，使用目标中心可见性模型对候选视点评分，该模型考虑遮挡、目标尺度、目标完整性，同时将候选限制在可行的机器人位姿。

Result: OA-NBV在仿真和真实世界试验中达到超过90%的成功率，而基线NBV方法在遮挡条件下性能急剧下降。此外，OA-NBV显著提升观测质量：相比最强基线，归一化目标面积至少增加81%，关键点可见性至少提升58%。

Conclusion: OA-NBV是一种即插即用的视点选择模块，适用于多样化的人类中心下游任务，能够有效解决被遮挡人体观测问题，在现实运动约束下实现高质量的主动感知。

Abstract: We naturally step sideways or lean to see around the obstacle when our view is blocked, and recover a more informative observation. Enabling robots to make the same kind of viewpoint choice is critical for human-centered operations, including search, triage, and disaster response, where cluttered environments and partial visibility frequently degrade downstream perception. However, many Next-Best-View (NBV) methods primarily optimize generic exploration or long-horizon coverage, and do not explicitly target the immediate goal of obtaining a single usable observation of a partially occluded person under real motion constraints. We present Occlusion-Aware Next-Best-View Planning for Human-Centered Active Perception on Mobile Robots (OA-NBV), an occlusion-aware NBV pipeline that autonomously selects the next traversable viewpoint to obtain a more complete view of an occluded human. OA-NBV integrates perception and motion planning by scoring candidate viewpoints using a target-centric visibility model that accounts for occlusion, target scale, and target completeness, while restricting candidates to feasible robot poses. OA-NBV achieves over 90% success rate in both simulation and real-world trials, while baseline NBV methods degrade sharply under occlusion. Beyond success rate, OA-NBV improves observation quality: compared to the strongest baseline, it increases normalized target area by at least 81% and keypoint visibility by at least 58% across settings, making it a drop-in view-selection module for diverse human-centered downstream tasks.

</details>


### [20] [DRAFTO: Decoupled Reduced-space and Adaptive Feasibility-repair Trajectory Optimization for Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11074)
*Yichang Feng,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRAFTO是一种新的轨迹优化算法，通过解耦降维空间优化和自适应可行性修复，在保证约束满足的同时提高计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹优化方法在处理关节限制等约束时存在计算效率低或可行性差的问题，需要一种既能高效优化又能可靠处理约束的算法。

Method: 1) 构建包含平滑性、安全性、关节限制和任务要求的约束目标函数；2) 将轨迹参数化为基函数系数的优化；3) 解耦为降维空间高斯牛顿下降（主迭代）和约束二次规划（初始化和终端可行性修复）；4) 采用带非单调策略的两阶段接受规则，使用铰链平方惩罚处理不等式约束以确保全局化能力。

Result: 在基准测试中，DRAFTO相比CHOMP、TrajOpt、GPMP2、FACTO等优化型规划器以及RRT-Connect、RRT*、PRM等采样型规划器，展现出更高的效率和可靠性；从抽屉抓取物体的实验进一步证明了其在复杂操作任务中的实施潜力。

Conclusion: DRAFTO通过解耦优化策略有效平衡了计算效率和约束可行性，为复杂机器人操作任务提供了一种高效可靠的轨迹优化解决方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new algorithm for trajectory optimization, Decoupled Reduced-space and Adaptive Feasibility-repair Trajectory Optimization (DRAFTO). It first constructs a constrained objective that accounts for smoothness, safety, joint limits, and task requirements. Then, it optimizes the coefficients, which are the coordinates of a set of basis functions for trajectory parameterization. To reduce the number of repeated constrained optimizations while handling joint-limit feasibility, the optimization is decoupled into a reduced-space Gauss-Newton (GN) descent for the main iterations and constrained quadratic programming for initialization and terminal feasibility repair. The two-phase acceptance rule with a non-monotone policy is applied to the GN model, which uses a hinge-squared penalty for inequality constraints, to ensure globalizability. The results of our benchmark tests against optimization-based planners, such as CHOMP, TrajOpt, GPMP2, and FACTO, and sampling-based planners, such as RRT-Connect, RRT*, and PRM, validate the high efficiency and reliability across diverse scenarios and tasks. The experiment involving grabbing an object from a drawer further demonstrates the potential for implementation in complex manipulation tasks. The supplemental video is available at https://youtu.be/XisFI37YyTQ.

</details>


### [21] [SELF-VLA: A Skill Enhanced Agentic Vision-Language-Action Framework for Contact-Rich Disassembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11080)
*Chang Liu,Sibo Tian,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SELF-VLA：一个集成显式拆卸技能的智能VLA框架，显著优于现有端到端VLA模型，适用于接触密集的工业拆卸任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人拆卸系统存在以下问题：1) 每个子任务需要大量数据准备、模型训练和系统管理；2) 任务和组件特异性强，难以处理EoL产品的多变性和不确定性；3) 泛化能力有限，高度依赖人工劳动。虽然VLA模型在标准机器人操作任务上表现优异，但在复杂、接触密集、长周期的工业拆卸实践中应用受限。

Method: 提出SELF-VLA框架，将显式拆卸技能集成到智能VLA模型中。该框架通过技能整合增强模型对复杂拆卸任务的处理能力，特别是需要顺序和精确操作的接触密集型任务。

Result: 在两个接触密集型拆卸任务上的实验研究表明，SELF-VLA框架显著优于当前最先进的端到端VLA模型。

Conclusion: SELF-VLA通过集成显式拆卸技能，有效提升了VLA模型在复杂工业拆卸任务中的性能，为解决当前机器人拆卸系统面临的泛化性和实用性挑战提供了新途径。

Abstract: Disassembly automation has long been pursued to address the growing demand for efficient and proper recovery of valuable components from the end-of-life (EoL) electronic products. Existing approaches have demonstrated promising and regimented performance by decomposing the disassembly process into different subtasks. However, each subtask typically requires extensive data preparation, model training, and system management. Moreover, these approaches are often task- and component-specific, making them poorly suited to handle the variability and uncertainty of EoL products and limiting their generalization capabilities. All these factors restrict the practical deployment of current robotic disassembly systems and leave them highly reliant on human labor. With the recent development of foundation models in robotics, vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown impressive performance on standard robotic manipulation tasks, but their applicability to complex, contact-rich, and long-horizon industrial practices like disassembly, which requires sequential and precise manipulation, remains limited. To address this challenge, we propose SELF-VLA, an agentic VLA framework that integrates explicit disassembly skills. Experimental studies demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms current state-of-the-art end-to-end VLA models on two contact-rich disassembly tasks. The video illustration can be found via https://zh.engr.tamu.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/310/2026/03/IROS-VLA-Video.mp4.

</details>


### [22] [Edge-Assisted Multi-Robot Visual-Inertial SLAM with Efficient Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11085)
*Xin Liu,Shuhuan Wen,Jing Zhao,Tony Z. Qiu,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于机器人-边缘-云分层架构的集中式多机器人SLAM系统，采用基于金字塔IMU预测的光流跟踪轻量化SLAM方法，通过特征点和关键帧描述子的无损编码压缩实现有限带宽下的实时远程传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多机器人SLAM系统中终端设备计算、通信和存储能力有限的问题，同时避免云与终端间有限带宽和长通信链路导致的性能下降，实现实时协同SLAM。

Method: 1. 提出基于金字塔IMU预测的光流跟踪轻量化SLAM方法，降低特征跟踪计算成本；2. 设计机器人-边缘-云分层架构的集中式多机器人SLAM系统；3. 仅传输特征点和关键帧描述子并进行无损编码压缩，减少数据传输带宽占用。

Result: 在EuRoC数据集上的实验验证表明：1. 相比当前最先进的局部特征压缩方法，能实现更低数据量的特征传输；2. 相比当前先进的集中式多机器人SLAM方案，在低计算负载下能达到相同或更好的定位精度。

Conclusion: 提出的分层架构和轻量化SLAM方法有效解决了多机器人SLAM系统中的计算资源限制和带宽约束问题，实现了实时协同SLAM，同时保持了定位精度。

Abstract: The integration of cloud computing and edge computing is an effective way to achieve global consistent and real-time multi-robot Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM). Cloud computing effectively solves the problem of limited computing, communication and storage capacity of terminal equipment. However, limited bandwidth and extremely long communication links between terminal devices and the cloud result in serious performance degradation of multi-robot SLAM systems. To reduce the computational cost of feature tracking and improve the real-time performance of the robot, a lightweight SLAM method of optical flow tracking based on pyramid IMU prediction is proposed. On this basis, a centralized multi-robot SLAM system based on a robot-edge-cloud layered architecture is proposed to realize real-time collaborative SLAM. It avoids the problems of limited on-board computing resources and low execution efficiency of single robot. In this framework, only the feature points and keyframe descriptors are transmitted and lossless encoding and compression are carried out to realize real-time remote information transmission with limited bandwidth resources. This design reduces the actual bandwidth occupied in the process of data transmission, and does not cause the loss of SLAM accuracy caused by data compression. Through experimental verification on the EuRoC dataset, compared with the current most advanced local feature compression method, our method can achieve lower data volume feature transmission, and compared with the current advanced centralized multi-robot SLAM scheme, it can achieve the same or better positioning accuracy under low computational load.

</details>


### [23] [ResWM: Residual-Action World Model for Visual RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11110)
*Jseen Zhang,Gabriel Adineera,Jinzhou Tan,Jinoh Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ResWM提出了一种基于残差动作的世界模型框架，将控制变量从绝对动作改为相对于前一步的增量调整，从而稳定优化、减少搜索空间并改善长期规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模型的强化学习框架直接基于绝对动作进行未来预测，这导致优化不稳定：最优动作分布是任务相关的、先验未知的，通常会导致振荡或低效控制。需要一种更稳定、更符合真实世界控制平滑特性的方法。

Method: 提出残差动作世界模型(ResWM)，将控制变量重新表述为残差动作（相对于前一步的增量调整）。引入观测差异编码器显式建模相邻帧之间的变化，生成与残差动作自然耦合的紧凑潜在动态。在Dreamer风格的潜在动态模型基础上进行最小修改，无需额外超参数。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite上的实验表明，ResWM在样本效率、渐进回报和控制平滑性方面均取得一致改进，显著超越Dreamer和TD-MPC等强基线。产生更稳定、更节能的动作轨迹，这对实际机器人系统部署至关重要。

Conclusion: 残差动作建模为连接强化学习算法进展与机器人实际需求提供了一个简单而强大的原则，能够实现更平滑的探索、更低的控制方差和更可靠的规划。

Abstract: Learning predictive world models from raw visual observations is a central challenge in reinforcement learning (RL), especially for robotics and continuous control. Conventional model-based RL frameworks directly condition future predictions on absolute actions, which makes optimization unstable: the optimal action distributions are task-dependent, unknown a priori, and often lead to oscillatory or inefficient control. To address this, we introduce the Residual-Action World Model (ResWM), a new framework that reformulates the control variable from absolute actions to residual actions -- incremental adjustments relative to the previous step. This design aligns with the inherent smoothness of real-world control, reduces the effective search space, and stabilizes long-horizon planning. To further strengthen the representation, we propose an Observation Difference Encoder that explicitly models the changes between adjacent frames, yielding compact latent dynamics that are naturally coupled with residual actions. ResWM is integrated into a Dreamer-style latent dynamics model with minimal modifications and no extra hyperparameters. Both imagination rollouts and policy optimization are conducted in the residual-action space, enabling smoother exploration, lower control variance, and more reliable planning. Empirical results on the DeepMind Control Suite demonstrate that ResWM achieves consistent improvements in sample efficiency, asymptotic returns, and control smoothness, significantly surpassing strong baselines such as Dreamer and TD-MPC. Beyond performance, ResWM produces more stable and energy-efficient action trajectories, a property critical for robotic systems deployed in real-world environments. These findings suggest that residual action modeling provides a simple yet powerful principle for bridging algorithmic advances in RL with the practical requirements of robotics.

</details>


### [24] [Robust Co-design Optimisation for Agile Fixed-Wing UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11130)
*Adrian Andrei Buda,Xavier Chen,Nicolò Botteghi,Urban Fasel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种鲁棒协同设计框架，用于敏捷固定翼无人机，将参数不确定性和风扰动直接集成到并发优化过程中，在三个敏捷飞行任务中优于确定性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同设计框架往往忽视自主系统在非结构化真实环境中所需的鲁棒性，导致设计对扰动和模型失配敏感，特别是对于在飞行包线边缘运行的敏捷无人机。

Method: 采用双层方法：高层循环优化物理设计，通过约束轨迹规划器发现标称解，并使用反馈LQR控制通过随机蒙特卡洛集成评估性能，直接集成参数不确定性和风扰动。

Result: 在三个敏捷飞行任务中验证，该策略始终优于确定性基线，能够自动调整翼面位置和展弦比等气动特征，实现任务性能与扰动抑制之间的最优权衡。

Conclusion: 提出的鲁棒协同设计框架能够为敏捷固定翼无人机生成对不确定性和扰动具有内在鲁棒性的设计，实现物理设计与控制策略的协同优化。

Abstract: Co-design optimisation of autonomous systems has emerged as a powerful alternative to sequential approaches by jointly optimising physical design and control strategies. However, existing frameworks often neglect the robustness required for autonomous systems navigating unstructured, real-world environments. For agile Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) operating at the edge of the flight envelope, this lack of robustness yields designs that are sensitive to perturbations and model mismatch. To address this, we propose a robust co-design framework for agile fixed-wing UAVs that integrates parametric uncertainty and wind disturbances directly into the concurrent optimisation process. Our bi-level approach optimises physical design in a high-level loop while discovering nominal solutions via a constrained trajectory planner and evaluating performance across a stochastic Monte Carlo ensemble using feedback LQR control. Validated across three agile flight missions, our strategy consistently outperforms deterministic baselines. The results demonstrate that our robust co-design strategy inherently tailors aerodynamic features, such as wing placement and aspect ratio, to achieve an optimal trade-off between mission performance and disturbance rejection.

</details>


### [25] [ADMM-based Continuous Trajectory Optimization in Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11335)
*Lukas Pries,Jon Arrizabalaga,Zachary Manchester,Markus Ryll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于ADMM的数值求解器，用于计算非凸环境中的连续轨迹，通过多项式参数化和时空分配图实现联合优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在非凸环境中存在搜索空间受限和需要复杂预热初始化的问题，需要一种能够联合优化离散空间和连续时间域、且对初始条件鲁棒的求解器

Method: 基于定制ADMM实现，包含两个关键组件：1) 将轨迹参数化为多项式，使原始更新可闭式求解为最小控制力问题；2) 基于混合整数规划引入时空分配图概念，将松弛更新转化为最短路径搜索

Result: 该方法相比现有技术具有多个优势：通过联合优化离散空间和连续时间域访问更大的搜索空间，能够发现更优轨迹；结构鲁棒性确保从简单初始化可靠收敛，消除了非凸环境中复杂预热初始化的瓶颈

Conclusion: 提出的ADMM求解器通过多项式参数化和时空分配图的创新组合，有效解决了非凸环境中轨迹规划的挑战，提供了比现有解耦方法更优的解决方案

Abstract: This paper presents a numerical solver for computing continuous trajectories in non-convex environments. Our approach relies on a customized implementation of the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) built upon two key components: First, we parameterize trajectories as polynomials, allowing the primal update to be computed in closed form as a minimum-control-effort problem. Second, we introduce the concept of a spatio-temporal allocation graph based on a mixed-integer formulation and pose the slack update as a shortest-path search. The combination of these ingredients results in a solver with several distinct advantages over the state of the art. By jointly optimizing over both discrete spatial and continuous temporal domains, our method accesses a larger search space than existing decoupled approaches, enabling the discovery of superior trajectories. Additionally, the solver's structural robustness ensures reliable convergence from naive initializations, removing the bottleneck of complex warm starting in non-convex environments.

</details>


### [26] [Novelty Adaptation Through Hybrid Large Language Model (LLM)-Symbolic Planning and LLM-guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11351)
*Hong Lu,Pierrick Lorang,Timothy R. Duggan,Jivko Sinapov,Matthias Scheutz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号架构，结合符号规划、强化学习和大语言模型来处理动态开放世界中的新颖对象


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态开放世界中，自主智能体常遇到新颖对象，导致传统符号规划器因缺乏相应操作符而无法生成有效计划

Method: 集成符号规划、强化学习和LLM：利用LLM的常识推理识别缺失操作符，符号AI规划器生成计划，并编写奖励函数指导RL智能体学习新操作符的控制策略

Result: 在连续机器人领域中，该方法在操作符发现和操作符学习方面均优于现有最先进方法

Conclusion: 神经符号架构能有效处理动态环境中的新颖对象，通过LLM的常识推理弥补符号规划的不足，实现更强大的自主智能体

Abstract: In dynamic open-world environments, autonomous agents often encounter novelties that hinder their ability to find plans to achieve their goals. Specifically, traditional symbolic planners fail to generate plans when the robot's planning domain lacks the operators that enable it to interact appropriately with novel objects in the environment. We propose a neuro-symbolic architecture that integrates symbolic planning, reinforcement learning, and a large language model (LLM) to learn how to handle novel objects. In particular, we leverage the common sense reasoning capability of the LLM to identify missing operators, generate plans with the symbolic AI planner, and write reward functions to guide the reinforcement learning agent in learning control policies for newly identified operators. Our method outperforms the state-of-the-art methods in operator discovery as well as operator learning in continuous robotic domains.

</details>


### [27] [MirrorDrift: Actuated Mirror-Based Attacks on LiDAR SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11364)
*Rokuto Nagata,Kenji Koide,Kazuma Ikeda,Ozora Sako,Shion Horie,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MirrorDrift攻击利用平面镜的镜面反射在LiDAR扫描中产生鬼点，系统性地偏置扫描匹配对应关系，从而破坏LiDAR SLAM系统的定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LiDAR SLAM攻击依赖信号注入，需要传感器特定的时序知识且易被现代防御机制（如时序混淆和注入拒绝）缓解。本研究探索无需注入的替代方案，利用镜面反射实现攻击。

Method: 通过可驱动的平面镜产生LiDAR扫描中的鬼点，系统性地偏置扫描匹配对应关系。优化镜子的放置、对准和驱动参数，最大化定位误差。

Result: 仿真中，攻击使平均姿态误差增加6.1倍，三个SLAM系统的平均APE达到2.29-3.31米。在具有先进干扰缓解功能的现代LiDAR真实实验中，诱导定位误差高达6.03米。

Conclusion: MirrorDrift是首个针对生产级安全LiDAR的SLAM定向攻击，证明了镜面反射作为无注入攻击方法的有效性，揭示了LiDAR SLAM系统的新安全威胁。

Abstract: LiDAR SLAM provides high-accuracy localization but is fragile to point-cloud corruption because scan matching assumes geometric consistency. Prior physical attacks on LiDAR SLAM largely rely on LiDAR spoofing via external signal injection, which requires sensor-specific timing knowledge and is increasingly mitigated by modern defense mechanisms such as timing obfuscation and injection rejection. In this work, we show that specular reflection offers an injection-free alternative and demonstrate an attack, MirrorDrift, that uses an actuated planar mirror to cause ghost points in LiDAR scans and systematically bias scan-matching correspondences. MirrorDrift optimizes mirror placement, alignment, and actuation. In simulation, it increases the average pose error (APE) by 6.1x over random placement, degrading three SLAM systems to 2.29-3.31 m mean APE. In real-world experiments on a modern LiDAR with state-of-the-art interference mitigation, it induces localization errors of up to 6.03 m. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first successful SLAM-targeted attack against production-grade secure LiDARs.

</details>


### [28] [D-SLAMSpoof: An Environment-Agnostic LiDAR Spoofing Attack using Dynamic Point Cloud Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11365)
*Rokuto Nagata,Kenji Koide,Kazuma Ikeda,Ozora Sako,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出D-SLAMSpoof攻击方法，通过时空协调的激光欺骗破坏LiDAR SLAM系统，并在特征丰富环境中显著提高攻击成功率；同时提出ISD-SLAM防御方法，仅使用惯性航位推算检测并缓解此类攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LiDAR欺骗方法在特征丰富的真实环境（如城市和室内）中攻击成功率低，而LiDAR SLAM系统在自动驾驶等关键应用中存在安全漏洞，需要研究更有效的攻击和防御方法。

Method: 1. 攻击方法：D-SLAMSpoof通过外部激光干扰向LiDAR扫描注入虚假测量，设计空间注入形状和基于扫描匹配原理的时间协调动态注入模式；2. 防御方法：ISD-SLAM仅利用自动驾驶系统中常见的惯性航位推算信号检测LiDAR欺骗攻击并缓解位置漂移。

Result: D-SLAMSpoof在特征丰富的真实环境中显著提高了攻击成功率，而ISD-SLAM能够准确检测包括D-SLAMSpoof在内的LiDAR欺骗攻击，并有效缓解由此产生的位置漂移。

Conclusion: 揭示了LiDAR SLAM系统的固有安全漏洞，提出了首个仅使用标准车载传感器的实用LiDAR SLAM欺骗防御方法，为提升自动驾驶系统的安全性和可靠性提供了关键见解。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce Dynamic SLAMSpoof (D-SLAMSpoof), a novel attack that compromises LiDAR SLAM even in feature-rich environments. The attack leverages LiDAR spoofing, which injects spurious measurements into LiDAR scans through external laser interference. By designing both spatial injection shapes and temporally coordinated dynamic injection patterns guided by scan-matching principles, D-SLAMSpoof significantly improves attack success rates in real-world, feature-rich environments such as urban areas and indoor spaces, where conventional LiDAR spoofing methods often fail. Furthermore, we propose a practical defense method, ISD-SLAM, that relies solely on inertial dead reckoning signals commonly available in autonomous systems. We demonstrate that ISD-SLAM accurately detects LiDAR spoofing attacks, including D-SLAMSpoof, and effectively mitigates the resulting position drift. Our findings expose inherent vulnerabilities in LiDAR-based SLAM and introduce the first practical defense against LiDAR-based SLAM spoofing using only standard onboard sensors, providing critical insights for improving the security and reliability of autonomous systems.

</details>


### [29] [Vision-Based Hand Shadowing for Robotic Manipulation via Inverse Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11383)
*Hendrik Chiche,Antoine Jamme,Trevor Rigoberto Martinez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于单目RGB-D相机的离线手部影射与重定向管道，用于低成本机械臂遥操作，通过手部关键点检测、3D重建、坐标变换和逆运动学求解实现机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本机械臂遥操作面临的主要挑战是将复杂的人手关节运动映射到机器人关节指令。现有方法需要昂贵的动捕设备或复杂的训练数据，限制了实际应用。

Method: 使用安装在3D打印眼镜上的单目RGB-D相机采集数据，通过MediaPipe Hands检测每只手21个关键点，结合深度信息进行3D重建，转换到机器人坐标系，在PyBullet中使用阻尼最小二乘法求解逆运动学，生成6自由度SO-ARM101机器人的关节指令。夹爪控制器采用四层回退层次结构将拇指-食指几何映射到抓取开度。

Result: 在结构化拾放基准测试（5个网格，每个网格10次抓取）中达到90%成功率。与四种基于视觉-语言-动作策略（ACT、SmolVLA、pi0.5、GR00T N1.5）相比表现良好。在非结构化真实环境（杂货店、药店）中，由于物体遮挡导致成功率降至9.3%（N=75）。

Conclusion: 无标记分析重定向方法在结构化环境中表现出色，但在非结构化环境中因手部遮挡而受限，显示了该方法的潜力和当前局限性，为未来改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Teleoperation of low-cost robotic manipulators remains challenging due to the complexity of mapping human hand articulations to robot joint commands. We present an offline hand-shadowing and retargeting pipeline from a single egocentric RGB-D camera mounted on 3D-printed glasses. The pipeline detects 21 hand landmarks per hand using MediaPipe Hands, deprojects them into 3D via depth sensing, transforms them into the robot coordinate frame, and solves a damped-least-squares inverse kinematics problem in PyBullet to produce joint commands for the 6-DOF SO-ARM101 robot. A gripper controller maps thumb-index finger geometry to grasp aperture with a four-level fallback hierarchy. Actions are first previewed in a physics simulation before replay on the physical robot through the LeRobot framework. We evaluate the IK retargeting pipeline on a structured pick-and-place benchmark (5-tile grid, 10 grasps per tile) achieving a 90% success rate, and compare it against four vision-language-action policies (ACT, SmolVLA, pi0.5, GR00T N1.5) trained on leader-follower teleoperation data. We also test the IK pipeline in unstructured real-world environments (grocery store, pharmacy), where hand occlusion by surrounding objects reduces success to 9.3% (N=75), highlighting both the promise and current limitations of marker-free analytical retargeting.

</details>


### [30] [Deployment-Time Reliability of Learned Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11400)
*Christopher Agia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出三种部署时机制提升学习型机器人策略的可靠性：运行时监控、基于影响函数的数据中心可解释性框架、以及面向长时程任务执行的策略协调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管学习型机器人策略能力显著提升，但部署可靠性仍是实际应用的根本障碍。分布偏移、误差累积和复杂任务依赖共同影响系统性能，需要部署时机制来提升策略可靠性。

Method: 1. 运行时监控方法：通过识别闭环策略行为不一致和任务进度偏差来检测即将发生的故障，无需故障数据或任务特定监督。
2. 数据中心可解释性框架：使用影响函数将部署时的成功和失败追溯到有影响力的训练演示，实现原则性诊断和数据集管理。
3. 策略协调方法：将策略协调表述为估计和最大化行为序列成功概率的问题，并通过可行性感知任务规划扩展到开放式语言指定任务。

Result: 论文提出了三种互补的部署时机制，为学习型机器人策略在实际应用中的可靠使用奠定了实践基础。这些机制能够在不修改策略本身的情况下提升部署可靠性。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦部署的核心挑战，这些贡献为学习型机器人策略在现实世界中的可靠使用推进了实践基础。在这些基础上持续进步对于实现未来可信赖和可扩展的机器人自主性至关重要。

Abstract: Recent advances in learning-based robot manipulation have produced policies with remarkable capabilities. Yet, reliability at deployment remains a fundamental barrier to real-world use, where distribution shift, compounding errors, and complex task dependencies collectively undermine system performance. This dissertation investigates how the reliability of learned robot policies can be improved at deployment time through mechanisms that operate around them. We develop three complementary classes of deployment-time mechanisms. First, we introduce runtime monitoring methods that detect impending failures by identifying inconsistencies in closed-loop policy behavior and deviations in task progress, without requiring failure data or task-specific supervision. Second, we propose a data-centric framework for policy interpretability that traces deployment-time successes and failures to influential training demonstrations using influence functions, enabling principled diagnosis and dataset curation. Third, we address reliable long-horizon task execution by formulating policy coordination as the problem of estimating and maximizing the success probability of behavior sequences, and we extend this formulation to open-ended, language-specified tasks through feasibility-aware task planning. By centering on core challenges of deployment, these contributions advance practical foundations for the reliable, real-world use of learned robot policies. Continued progress on these foundations will be essential for enabling trustworthy and scalable robot autonomy in the future.

</details>


### [31] [Real-time Rendering-based Surgical Instrument Tracking via Evolutionary Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11404)
*Hanyang Hu,Zekai Liang,Florian Richter,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于CMA-ES进化优化策略的手术器械跟踪框架，通过批量渲染并行评估多个位姿候选，显著提高了跟踪精度和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助微创手术中，手术器械的准确高效跟踪至关重要。现有视觉方法在视觉质量下降和数据稀缺时特征检测不可靠，而基于渲染的方法则面临计算成本高和收敛性差的问题。

Method: 将CMA-ES进化优化策略集成到跟踪流程中，联合估计手术器械位姿和关节配置。采用批量渲染并行评估多个位姿候选，显著减少推理时间并提高收敛鲁棒性。框架可推广到无关节角度和双手跟踪设置。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在精度和运行时间方面显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 提出的基于CMA-ES的跟踪框架为手术器械跟踪提供了一种准确高效的解决方案，适用于视觉反馈控制和在线手术视频校准。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient tracking of surgical instruments is fundamental for Robot-Assisted Minimally Invasive Surgery. Although vision-based robot pose estimation has enabled markerless calibration without tedious physical setups, reliable tool tracking for surgical robots still remains challenging due to partial visibility and specialized articulation design of surgical instruments. Previous works in the field are usually prone to unreliable feature detections under degraded visual quality and data scarcity, whereas rendering-based methods often struggle with computational costs and suboptimal convergence. In this work, we incorporate CMA-ES, an evolutionary optimization strategy, into a versatile tracking pipeline that jointly estimates surgical instrument pose and joint configurations. Using batch rendering to efficiently evaluate multiple pose candidates in parallel, the method significantly reduces inference time and improves convergence robustness. The proposed framework further generalizes to joint angle-free and bi-manual tracking settings, making it suitable for both vision feedback control and online surgery video calibration. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms prior approaches in both accuracy and runtime.

</details>


### [32] [Grounding Robot Generalization in Training Data via Retrieval-Augmented VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11426)
*Jensen Gao,Dorsa Sadigh,Sandy Huang,Dhruv Shah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RADAR是一个用于评估机器人策略泛化能力的可扩展框架，通过检索训练数据并使用视觉语言模型分析测试任务，提供可解释的泛化类型分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人操作研究在策略泛化评估方面存在不足，难以准确描述评估设置如何代表策略从训练分布中的泛化。需要更精确的泛化评估方法来量化测试任务与训练数据之间的关系。

Method: RADAR采用两阶段流水线：1) 使用通用策略嵌入进行检索，识别与给定评估任务相关的训练示例；2) 使用视觉语言模型分析评估任务与检索数据的差异，沿多个维度输出可解释分析，并对所需泛化类型进行分类。

Result: 实验表明：1) VLM能有效分析泛化数据；2) 检索步骤能准确识别分类所需训练示例；3) 在大规模数据集上，RADAR结果与先前工作中人工定义的基准条件一致。

Conclusion: RADAR提供了一个可扩展框架，能够精确评估机器人策略泛化，通过量化测试任务与训练数据的关系来确定所需泛化类型，为机器人泛化评估提供了更系统的方法。

Abstract: Recent work on robot manipulation has advanced policy generalization to novel scenarios. However, it is often difficult to characterize how different evaluation settings actually represent generalization from the training distribution of a given policy. To work towards more precise evaluation of generalization in robotics, we propose RADAR, a scalable framework for directly comparing test-time evaluation tasks to policy training data, to determine what form of policy generalization is required. RADAR consists of a two-stage pipeline: first, retrieval using generalist policy embeddings identifies which training examples are relevant for a given evaluation task. Next, vision-language models (VLMs) analyze the evaluation task against the retrieved data, outputting interpretable analysis on how they compare along a variety of axes, and an overall classification of what type of policy generalization is required. Through controlled experiments, we demonstrate that VLMs are effective at analyzing data for generalization, and that our retrieval step effectively identifies examples needed to make accurate classifications with respect to the training data. Furthermore, we scale RADAR to large-scale datasets, where we observe agreement with human-defined benchmark conditions from prior work. We provide demonstrations at radar-analysis.github.io.

</details>


### [33] [A Generalized Theory of Load Distribution in Redundantly-actuated Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11431)
*Joshua Flight,Clément Gosselin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种广义理论，用于描述冗余驱动机器人系统中刚性体的载荷分布，解决了多指夹持器、腿式机器人等过约束机构的力控制问题，并提供了高效的解析解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理冗余驱动机器人系统（如多指夹持器、腿式机器人、协作机器人等）中刚性体的载荷分布问题时存在不足，需要一种能够完全表征可行操纵力分布的理论和高效计算方法。

Method: 提出广义理论描述冗余驱动机器人系统中刚性体的载荷分布，推导出力合成和力分析问题的显式解析解，计算效率高且线性可扩展，无需数值方法或大矩阵求逆。

Result: 理论完全表征了给定合力下刚性体的可行操纵力分布，识别了现有方法的显著缺陷并提出修正，通过示例展示了改进方法的优势。

Conclusion: 该理论为冗余驱动机器人系统的力控制提供了完整框架，解决了过约束机构的载荷分布问题，提出的高效解析解具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a generalized theory which describes how applied loads are distributed within rigid bodies handled by redundantly-actuated robotic systems composed of multiple independent closed-loop kinematic chains. The theory fully characterizes the feasible set of manipulating wrench distributions for a given resultant wrench applied to the rigid body and has important implications for the force-control of multifingered grippers, legged robots, cooperating robots, and other overconstrained mechanisms. We also derive explicit solutions to the wrench synthesis and wrench analysis problems. These solutions are computationally efficient and scale linearly with the number of applied wrenches, requiring neither numerical methods nor the inversion of large matrices. Finally, we identify significant shortcomings in current state-of-the-art approaches and propose corrections. These are supported by illustrative examples that demonstrate the advantages of the improved methods.

</details>


### [34] [Enhancing Lightweight Vision Language Models through Group Competitive Learning for Socially Compliant Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11447)
*Xinyu Zhang,Atsushi Konno,Toshihiko Yamasaki,Ling Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Group Competitive Learning (GCL)策略，通过Group Competitive Objective (GCO)和Asymmetric Group Optimization (AGO)增强轻量级VLM在社交导航中的推理能力，实现高性能与计算效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人导航需要结合场景语义和人类社交规范。大型视觉语言模型(VLMs)推理能力强但计算开销大，不适合实时部署；轻量级VLMs效率高但推理能力弱。如何在保持高效的同时获得强推理能力是一个开放挑战。

Method: 提出Group Competitive Learning (GCL)策略，包含两个核心组件：1) Group Competitive Objective (GCO)：协调全局语义与分布正则化；2) Asymmetric Group Optimization (AGO)：探索模型性能上限。该策略旨在增强轻量级VLMs的能力。

Result: 在社交导航基准测试中，GCL显著提升VLM性能：Qwen2.5-VL-3B模型F1分数达0.968（比SFT提升40%），Qwen3-VL-4B达0.914（提升12%）。特别地，经过GCL训练的3B模型超越了8B基线模型（提升28%），表明轻量级模型通过GCL能达到甚至超越更大模型的性能。

Conclusion: GCL为在实际部署中同时实现高精度和计算效率提供了有效解决方案，通过竞争性学习策略成功弥合了轻量级VLMs推理能力不足的差距，使小型模型在社交导航任务中达到超越大型模型的性能。

Abstract: Social robot navigation requires a sophisticated integration of scene semantics and human social norms. Scaling up Vision Language Models (VLMs) generally improves reasoning and decision-making capabilities for socially compliant navigation. However, increased model size incurs substantial computational overhead, limiting suitability for real-time robotic deployment. Conversely, lightweight VLMs enable efficient inference but often exhibit weaker reasoning and decision-making performance in socially complex environments. Achieving both strong reasoning ability and efficiency remains an open challenge. To bridge this gap, we propose Group Competitive Learning (GCL), a strategy designed to amplify the capabilities of lightweight VLMs. Our strategy introduces the Group Competitive Objective (GCO) to harmonize global semantics with distributional regularization, alongside Asymmetric Group Optimization (AGO) to explore the upper limits of model performance. Empirical evaluations on social navigation benchmarks demonstrate that GCL significantly elevates VLM performance. Specifically, GCL enables the Qwen2.5-VL-3B learner model and guide Qwen3-VL-4B to achieve an F1 score of 0.968 and 0.914, representing 40\% and 12\% improvement over vanilla supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Notably, under vanilla SFT, the 3B model initially trails the 8B model (F1: 0.692 vs. 0.755). However, through the GCL, the 3B model outperforms (28\%) the 8B baseline model. These results suggest that GCL provides an effective solution for achieving both high accuracy and computational efficiency in real-world deployment.

</details>


### [35] [NFPO: Stabilized Policy Optimization of Normalizing Flow for Robotic Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11470)
*Diyuan Shi,Yiqi Tang,Zifeng Zhuang,Donglin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NFPO：使用归一化流作为策略参数化方法，解决传统高斯策略无法建模多模态分布的问题，通过简单有效的技术解决在线RL训练不稳定性，在机器人任务中表现优异并可迁移到真实机器人


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度强化学习中机器人策略学习的标准参数化方法仍是多元高斯分布（对角协方差矩阵），这种方法缺乏建模多模态分布的能力，限制了策略的表达能力

Method: 采用归一化流作为策略参数化方法，利用其多模态建模能力、对数概率的闭式解以及低计算内存开销；通过分析并解决在线强化学习中NF训练不稳定的问题，提出简单有效的训练技术

Result: 在多个仿真环境中进行广泛实验，NFPO方法在广泛使用的机器人学习任务中获得了鲁棒且强大的性能，并成功迁移到真实世界的机器人上

Conclusion: 归一化流作为策略参数化方法能够有效解决传统高斯策略在多模态建模方面的局限性，通过适当的训练技术可以稳定地在在线强化学习中使用，为机器人策略学习提供了更强大的表达能力和实际应用价值

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) has experienced significant advancements in recent years and has been widely used in many fields. In DRL-based robotic policy learning, however, current de facto policy parameterization is still multivariate Gaussian (with diagonal covariance matrix), which lacks the ability to model multi-modal distribution. In this work, we explore the adoption of a modern network architecture, i.e. Normalizing Flow (NF) as the policy parameterization for its ability of multi-modal modeling, closed form of log probability and low computation and memory overhead. However, naively training NF in online Reinforcement Learning (RL) usually leads to training instability. We provide a detailed analysis for this phenomenon and successfully address it via simple but effective technique. With extensive experiments in multiple simulation environments, we show our method, NFPO could obtain robust and strong performance in widely used robotic learning tasks and successfully transfer into real-world robots.

</details>


### [36] [SPARK: Skeleton-Parameter Aligned Retargeting on Humanoid Robots with Kinodynamic Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11480)
*Hanwen Wang,Qiayuan Liao,Bike Zhang,Kunzhao Ren,Koushil Sreenath,Xiaobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两阶段流程：骨架校准+动力学轨迹优化，从人类运动数据生成机器人可用的高质量运动参考


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类运动数据为训练人形机器人控制策略提供丰富先验，但原始演示与机器人运动学和动力学不兼容，限制了直接使用

Method: 1. 骨架校准：将人类运动转换为URDF骨架表示，校准到目标人形机器人尺寸；2. 渐进式动力学轨迹优化：分三阶段（运动学TO、逆动力学、完整动力学TO）优化轨迹

Result: 显著减少逆运动学误差和调参工作量，生成动态一致的状态轨迹和关节扭矩剖面，为学习型控制器提供高质量参考

Conclusion: 骨架校准和动力学轨迹优化相结合，能够为不同人形平台生成自然、物理一致的运动参考

Abstract: Human motion provides rich priors for training general-purpose humanoid control policies, but raw demonstrations are often incompatible with a robot's kinematics and dynamics, limiting their direct use. We present a two-stage pipeline for generating natural and dynamically feasible motion references from task-space human data. First, we convert human motion into a unified robot description format (URDF)-based skeleton representation and calibrate it to the target humanoid's dimensions. By aligning the underlying skeleton structure rather than heuristically modifying task-space targets, this step significantly reduces inverse kinematics error and tuning effort. Second, we refine the retargeted trajectories through progressive kinodynamic trajectory optimization (TO), solved in three stages: kinematic TO, inverse dynamics, and full kinodynamic TO, each warm-started from the previous solution. The final result yields dynamically consistent state trajectories and joint torque profiles, providing high-quality references for learning-based controllers. Together, skeleton calibration and kinodynamic TO enable the generation of natural, physically consistent motion references across diverse humanoid platforms.

</details>


### [37] [MiNI-Q: A Miniature, Wire-Free Quadruped with Unbounded, Independently Actuated Leg Joints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11537)
*Daniel Koh,Suraj Shah,Yufeng Wu,Dennis Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MiNI-Q²是一款微型无线四足机器人，具有独立驱动、机械无限制的2自由度腿部关节，能够实现多种运动模式并折叠至2.5厘米高度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统腿式机器人的物理关节限制会限制工作空间、约束步态设计并增加硬件损坏风险，因此需要开发具有无限制关节的机器人来克服这些限制。

Method: 提出机械设计、运动学分析和实验验证，采用独立驱动的机械无限制2自由度腿部关节，基于先前Q8bot设计改进无线架构以提高微型尺度下的装配可靠性。

Result: MiNI-Q²最高速度达0.46 m/s，能够实现低间隙爬行、爬楼梯、倒立行走、跳跃和后空翻，可折叠至2.5厘米最小高度，所有设计文件开源。

Conclusion: MiNI-Q²展示了无限制关节在微型四足机器人中的优势，支持多种运动模式，开源设计有助于推动可重复性和进一步研究。

Abstract: Physical joint limits are common in legged robots and can restrict workspace, constrain gait design, and increase the risk of hardware damage. This paper introduces MiNI-Q^2, a miniature, wire-free quadruped robot with independently actuated, mechanically unbounded 2-DOF leg joints. We present the mechanical design, kinematic analysis, and experimental validation of the proposed robot. The leg mechanism enables both oscillatory gaits and rotary locomotion while allowing the robot to fold to a minimum height of 2.5 cm. Experimentally, MiNI-Q achieves speeds up to 0.46 m/s and demonstrates low-clearance crawling, stair climbing, inverted locomotion, jumping, and backflipping. The wire-free architecture extends our previous Q8bot design, improving assembly reliability at miniature scale. All mechanical and electrical design files are released open source to support reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [38] [RoboClaw: An Agentic Framework for Scalable Long-Horizon Robotic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11558)
*Ruiying Li,Yunlang Zhou,YuYao Zhu,Kylin Chen,Jingyuan Wang,Sukai Wang,Kongtao Hu,Minhui Yu,Bowen Jiang,Zhan Su,Jiayao Ma,Xin He,Yongjian Shen,Yangyang,Guanghui Ren,Maoqing Yao,Wenhao Wang,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboClaw：一个统一的VLA机器人框架，通过纠缠动作对实现自主数据收集和策略学习，在长时程任务中提升成功率和减少人工干预


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA系统在长时程任务中存在扩展性挑战，传统流水线分离数据收集、策略学习和部署，导致依赖人工环境重置和多策略执行脆弱。需要统一框架减少人工干预并提升鲁棒性。

Method: 提出RoboClaw框架，统一数据收集、策略学习和任务执行于单一VLM驱动控制器。核心创新是纠缠动作对（EAP），将前向操作行为与逆向恢复动作耦合，形成自主重置循环，实现连续同策略数据采集和迭代策略优化。

Result: 在真实世界操作任务中，相比传统开环流水线，RoboClaw提升了稳定性和可扩展性，长时程任务成功率提高25%，人工时间投入减少53.7%，显著降低机器人生命周期中的人力需求。

Conclusion: RoboClaw通过统一框架和纠缠动作对机制，解决了VLA系统在长时程任务中的扩展性挑战，实现了自主数据收集和鲁棒的多策略执行，为机器人操作提供了更高效、更少人工依赖的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) systems have shown strong potential for language-driven robotic manipulation. However, scaling them to long-horizon tasks remains challenging. Existing pipelines typically separate data collection, policy learning, and deployment, resulting in heavy reliance on manual environment resets and brittle multi-policy execution. We present RoboClaw, an agentic robotics framework that unifies data collection, policy learning, and task execution under a single VLM-driven controller. At the policy level, RoboClaw introduces Entangled Action Pairs (EAP), which couple forward manipulation behaviors with inverse recovery actions to form self-resetting loops for autonomous data collection. This mechanism enables continuous on-policy data acquisition and iterative policy refinement with minimal human intervention. During deployment, the same agent performs high-level reasoning and dynamically orchestrates learned policy primitives to accomplish long-horizon tasks. By maintaining consistent contextual semantics across collection and execution, RoboClaw reduces mismatch between the two phases and improves multi-policy robustness. Experiments in real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improved stability and scalability compared to conventional open-loop pipelines, while significantly reducing human effort throughout the robot lifecycle, achieving a 25% improvement in success rate over baseline methods on long-horizon tasks and reducing human time investment by 53.7%.

</details>


### [39] [Unsupervised LiDAR-Based Multi-UAV Detection and Tracking Under Extreme Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11586)
*Nivand Khosravi,Rodrigo Ventura,Meysam Basiri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无监督LiDAR检测与跟踪流水线，解决固态LiDAR扫描稀疏性导致的无人机检测难题，通过自适应聚类与概率数据关联提升多目标跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态LiDAR非重复扫描导致测量极为稀疏，小型无人机在10-25米距离通常每帧仅产生1-2个点云，远低于现有检测方法假设的点密度，且难以实现鲁棒的多目标数据关联。

Method: 提出无监督、仅LiDAR的流水线：检测器采用距离自适应DBSCAN聚类结合三阶段时间一致性检查；跟踪部分比较确定性匈牙利分配与联合概率数据关联(JPDA)，均与交互多模型滤波结合。采用两环境评估策略：真实世界检测配RTK-GPS地面真值，模拟跟踪配身份标注真值。

Result: 最佳配置达到0.891精度、0.804召回率和0.63米RMSE；minPts参数扫描验证大多数扫描最多包含1-2个目标点。JPDA在模拟场景中将身份切换减少64%，对MOTA影响可忽略，证明在无人机轨迹接近时概率关联具有优势。

Conclusion: 提出的无监督流水线能有效应对固态LiDAR的极端稀疏测量问题，概率数据关联在多目标跟踪中显著优于确定性方法，两环境评估策略克服了GNSS在2米以下距离的局限性。

Abstract: Non-repetitive solid-state LiDAR scanning leads to an extremely sparse measurement regime for detecting airborne UAVs: a small quadrotor at 10-25 m typically produces only 1-2 returns per scan, which is far below the point densities assumed by most existing detection approaches and inadequate for robust multi-target data association. We introduce an unsupervised, LiDAR-only pipeline that addresses both detection and tracking without the need for labeled training data. The detector integrates range-adaptive DBSCAN clustering with a three-stage temporal consistency check and is benchmarked on real-world air-to-air flight data under eight different parameter configurations. The best setup attains 0.891 precision, 0.804 recall, and 0.63 m RMSE, and a systematic minPts sweep verifies that most scans contain at most 1-2 target points, directly quantifying the sparsity regime. For multi-target tracking, we compare deterministic Hungarian assignment with joint probabilistic data association (JPDA), each coupled with Interacting Multiple Model filtering, in four simulated scenarios with increasing levels of ambiguity. JPDA cuts identity switches by 64% with negligible impact on MOTA, demonstrating that probabilistic association is advantageous when UAV trajectories approach one another closely. A two-environment evaluation strategy, combining real-world detection with RTK-GPS ground truth and simulation-based tracking with identity-annotated ground truth, overcomes the limitations of GNSS-only evaluation at inter-UAV distances below 2 m.

</details>


### [40] [Diversity You Can Actually Measure: A Fast, Model-Free Diversity Metric for Robotics Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11634)
*Sreevardhan Sirigiri,Nathan Samuel de Lara,Christopher Agia,Florian Shkurti,Fabio Ramos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FAKTUAL算法，利用轨迹核熵进行机器人模仿学习的数据集多样性度量与筛选，无需模型访问即可提升下游任务成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习数据集通常包含不同长度的长时程轨迹，难以在尊重轨迹结构和几何特性的前提下量化多样性。现有方法缺乏直接操作演示数据集的熵和多样性度量

Method: 将香农熵和冯·诺依曼熵扩展到轨迹设置，基于签名核的Gram矩阵定义签名变换熵。提出FAKTUAL算法，在给定子集大小预算下选择最大化熵的演示子集，该算法完全无模型，无需访问模仿策略或rollout

Result: 在图像和状态基础的RoboMimic、MetaWorld基准以及四个真实世界操作任务上评估。FAKTUAL的多样性感知筛选相比随机选择持续提升下游成功率，计算效率显著高于现有机器人数据筛选方法

Conclusion: 演示数据集的熵是理解和改进机器人模仿学习中数据集多样性的实用工具，FAKTUAL为数据筛选提供了高效无模型的解决方案

Abstract: Robotics datasets for imitation learning typically consist of long-horizon trajectories of different lengths over states, actions, and high-dimensional observations (e.g., RGB video), making it non-trivial to quantify diversity in a way that respects the underlying trajectory structure and geometry. We extend Shannon and von Neumann entropy to this setting by defining signature transform-based entropy on the Gram matrix of a signature kernel over demonstrations, yielding entropy and diversity metrics that operate directly on the demonstration dataset. Building on these metrics, we study how dataset diversity affects generalization performance in robot imitation learning and propose a simple, model-free way to curate diverse demonstrations. We introduce FAKTUAL (FAst trajectory Kernel enTropy cUration for imitation Learning), a data curation algorithm that selects a subset of demonstrations maximizing entropy given a subset-size budget. FAKTUAL is fully model-free, requires no access to the imitation policy or rollouts, and adds negligible overhead relative to policy training. We evaluate our approach on image and state-based RoboMimic and MetaWorld benchmarks, as well as four real-world manipulation tasks. Across tasks and architectures, diversity-aware curation with FAKTUAL consistently improves downstream success rates over random selection, while being substantially more computationally efficient compared to recent robot data curation methods. Our results suggest that the entropy of demonstration datasets is a practical tool for understanding and improving dataset diversity in robot imitation learning.

</details>


### [41] [Learn Structure, Adapt on the Fly: Multi-Scale Residual Learning and Online Adaptation for Aerial Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11638)
*Samaksh Ujjawal,Naveen Sudheer Nair,Shivansh Pratap Singh,Rishabh Dev Yadav,Wei Pan,Spandan Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Factorized Dynamics Transformer (FDT)和Latent Residual Adapter (LRA)的预测-自适应框架，用于自主空中机械臂的非平稳多尺度残差动力学实时建模与补偿，提升未知负载下的控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主空中机械臂(AAMs)是耦合非线性系统，在机械臂重构和突加载荷变化时呈现非平稳多尺度残差动力学。传统解析模型依赖固定参数结构，静态数据驱动模型假设平稳动力学，在配置变化和载荷变化时性能下降。现有学习架构未显式分解跨变量耦合和多尺度时间效应，混淆了瞬时惯性动力学与长时域状态演化。

Method: 提出预测-自适应框架，核心是Factorized Dynamics Transformer (FDT)，将物理变量作为独立token处理，显式实现跨变量注意力，结构上分离短时域惯性依赖和长时域气动效应。为应对部署时分布偏移，Latent Residual Adapter (LRA)通过递归最小二乘法在潜空间进行快速线性自适应，保留离线非线性表示而不产生过高计算开销。自适应残差预测直接集成到残差补偿自适应控制器中。

Result: 在承受未知载荷的空中机械臂上进行真实世界实验，相比最先进的学习基线方法，展示了更高的预测保真度、更快的扰动衰减和更优的闭环跟踪精度，同时保持严格的实时可行性。

Conclusion: 提出的FDT-LRA框架能够有效处理自主空中机械臂的非平稳多尺度残差动力学，通过显式分解跨变量耦合和多尺度时间效应，结合快速潜空间自适应，在未知负载条件下实现了优越的实时控制性能。

Abstract: Autonomous Aerial Manipulators (AAMs) are inherently coupled, nonlinear systems that exhibit nonstationary and multiscale residual dynamics, particularly during manipulator reconfiguration and abrupt payload variations. Conventional analytical dynamic models rely on fixed parametric structures, while static data-driven model assume stationary dynamics and degrade under configuration changes and payload variations. Moreover, existing learning architectures do not explicitly factorize cross-variable coupling and multi-scale temporal effects, conflating instantaneous inertial dynamics with long-horizon regime evolution. We propose a predictive-adaptive framework for real-time residual modeling and compensation in AAMs. The core of this framework is the Factorized Dynamics Transformer (FDT), which treats physical variables as independent tokens. This design enables explicit cross-variable attention while structurally separating short-horizon inertial dependencies from long-horizon aerodynamic effects. To address deployment-time distribution shifts, a Latent Residual Adapter (LRA) performs rapid linear adaptation in the latent space via Recursive Least Squares, preserving the offline nonlinear representation without prohibitive computational overhead. The adapted residual forecast is directly integrated into a residual-compensated adaptive controller. Real-world experiments on an aerial manipulator subjected to unseen payloads demonstrate higher prediction fidelity, accelerated disturbance attenuation, and superior closed-loop tracking precision compared to state-of-the-art learning baselines, all while maintaining strict real-time feasibility.

</details>


### [42] [Chunk-Boundary Artifact in Action-Chunked Generative Policies: A Noise-Sensitive Failure Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11642)
*Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现动作分块策略中的边界伪影是一种噪声敏感故障机制，可通过噪声空间干预调节


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分块已成为生成式视觉运动策略的核心设计选择，但分块边界处产生的执行不连续性尚未得到充分理解。本文旨在深入分析这些边界伪影的机制

Method: 在预训练的动作分块策略中，通过分析边界伪影与任务失败的关系，研究噪声对伪影的影响，识别噪声空间中的伪影相关方向，并应用轨迹级引导来调节伪影幅度

Result: 边界伪影与任务失败显著相关（p < 1e-4），在固定观察上下文下仅改变潜在噪声即可系统调节伪影幅度。通过噪声空间干预可可靠改变所有评估任务的伪影幅度。在困难任务中，成功/失败分布相应变化；在接近上限的任务中，正向增益因策略饱和而压缩，但负向因果效应仍然可见

Conclusion: 将边界不连续性从不可避免的执行问题重新定义为可分析、噪声主导且可干预的故障机制

Abstract: Action chunking has become a central design choice for generative visuomotor policies, yet the execution discontinuities that arise at chunk boundaries remain poorly understood. In a frozen pretrained action-chunked policy, we identify chunk-boundary artifact as a noise-sensitive failure mechanism. First, artifact is strongly associated with task failure (p < 1e-4, permutation test) and emerges during the rollout rather than only as a post-hoc symptom. Second, under a fixed observation context, changing only latent noise systematically modulates artifact magnitude. Third, by identifying artifact-related directions in noise space and applying trajectory-level steering, we reliably alter artifact magnitude across all evaluated tasks. In hard-task settings with remaining outcome headroom, the success/failure distribution shifts accordingly; on near-ceiling tasks, positive gains are compressed by policy saturation, while the negative causal effect remains visible. Overall, we recast boundary discontinuity from an unavoidable execution nuisance into an analyzable, noise-dominated, and intervenable failure mechanism.

</details>


### [43] [Concurrent Prehensile and Nonprehensile Manipulation: A Practical Approach to Multi-Stage Dexterous Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11655)
*Hao Jiang,Yue Wu,Yue Wang,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexMulti：一种样本高效的灵巧多任务操作方法，通过将演示分解为面向对象的技能，采用检索-对齐-执行范式，在少量演示下实现多阶段操作任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手能够同时进行抓握和非抓握操作（如持有一个物体同时与另一个物体交互），这是日常任务的关键能力，但在机器人领域尚未充分探索。学习这种长时程、接触丰富的多阶段行为具有挑战性，因为演示数据收集成本高，端到端策略需要大量数据才能泛化到不同的物体几何形状和放置位置。

Method: DexMulti方法将演示分解为面向对象的技能，并定义明确的时间边界。该方法不学习单一策略，而是基于当前物体几何形状检索演示技能，使用不确定性感知估计器（跟踪质心和偏航角）将技能对齐到观察到的物体状态，通过检索-对齐-执行范式执行技能。

Result: 在三个需要并发操作的多阶段任务（抓握+拉动、抓握+打开、抓握+抓握）上，使用两个灵巧手（Allegro和LEAP）进行了超过1000次真实世界试验。该方法在训练物体上平均成功率66%，每个物体仅需3-4个演示，性能比扩散策略基线提高2-3倍，同时需要更少的演示。结果展示了良好的泛化能力，能够适应未见过的物体和高达±25厘米的空间变化。

Conclusion: DexMulti提供了一种样本高效的灵巧多任务操作方法，通过技能分解和检索-对齐-执行范式，在少量演示下实现了多阶段并发操作的鲁棒泛化，为解决长时程、接触丰富的灵巧操作任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Dexterous hands enable concurrent prehensile and nonprehensile manipulation, such as holding one object while interacting with another, a capability essential for everyday tasks yet underexplored in robotics. Learning such long-horizon, contact-rich multi-stage behaviors is challenging because demonstrations are expensive to collect and end-to-end policies require substantial data to generalize across varied object geometries and placements. We present DexMulti, a sample-efficient approach for real-world dexterous multi-task manipulation that decomposes demonstrations into object-centric skills with well-defined temporal boundaries. Rather than learning monolithic policies, our method retrieves demonstrated skills based on current object geometry, aligns them to the observed object state using an uncertainty-aware estimator that tracks centroid and yaw, and executes them via a retrieve-align-execute paradigm. We evaluate on three multi-stage tasks requiring concurrent manipulation (Grasp + Pull, Grasp + Open, and Grasp + Grasp) across two dexterous hands (Allegro and LEAP) in over 1,000 real-world trials. Our approach achieves an average success rate of 66% on training objects with only 3-4 demonstrations per object, outperforming diffusion policy baselines by 2-3x while requiring far fewer demonstrations. Results demonstrate robust generalization to held-out objects and spatial variations up to +/-25 cm.

</details>


### [44] [Coupling Tensor Trains with Graph of Convex Sets: Effective Compression, Exploration, and Planning in the C-Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11658)
*Gerhard Reinerth,Riddhiman Laha,Marcello Romano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TANGO是一种新颖的运动规划框架，通过张量压缩和结构化图优化实现高效可扩展的轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于优化的规划器（如图凸集）需要预定义高维构型空间的凸表征，这在通用机器人任务中往往难以实现。需要一种能够处理复杂约束和环境、同时保持高效性的运动规划方法。

Method: TANGO结合张量列车分解压缩可行构型空间，快速发现任务相关区域，然后将这些区域嵌入到GCS类结构中，实现几何感知的运动规划。通过张量压缩与结构化图推理的耦合，系统能够同时处理系统约束和环境复杂性。

Result: 在平面和真实机器人上的严格仿真研究表明，TANGO实现了有效压缩并生成了更高质量的轨迹。框架为未来机器人系统中更表达性和可扩展的构型空间表示奠定了基础。

Conclusion: TANGO通过集成张量压缩和结构化图优化，实现了高效、几何感知的运动规划，解决了传统优化规划器在高维构型空间表征方面的局限性，为机器人运动规划提供了新的可扩展解决方案。

Abstract: We present TANGO (Tensor ANd Graph Optimization), a novel motion planning framework that integrates tensor-based compression with structured graph optimization to enable efficient and scalable trajectory generation. While optimization-based planners such as the Graph of Convex Sets (GCS) offer powerful tools for generating smooth, optimal trajectories, they typically rely on a predefined convex characterization of the high-dimensional configuration space-a requirement that is often intractable for general robotic tasks. TANGO builds further by using Tensor Train decomposition to approximate the feasible configuration space in a compressed form, enabling rapid discovery and estimation of task-relevant regions. These regions are then embedded into a GCS-like structure, allowing for geometry-aware motion planning that respects both system constraints and environmental complexity. By coupling tensor-based compression with structured graph reasoning, TANGO enables efficient, geometry-aware motion planning and lays the groundwork for more expressive and scalable representations of configuration space in future robotic systems. Rigorous simulation studies on planar and real robots reinforce our claims of effective compression and higher quality trajectories.

</details>


### [45] [RADAR: Closed-Loop Robotic Data Generation via Semantic Planning and Autonomous Causal Environment Reset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11811)
*Yongzhong Wang,Keyu Zhu,Yong Zhong,Liqiong Wang,Jinyu Yang,Feng Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RADAR是一个完全自主的机器人数据采集系统，通过四模块管道消除人工干预，实现大规模物理交互数据的自动化生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模物理交互数据采集面临人工成本高、可扩展性有限的问题，需要突破人类参与的数据收集范式瓶颈

Method: 采用四模块闭环管道：1)VLM进行场景相关任务生成；2)GNN策略通过上下文模仿学习执行物理动作；3)VLM通过结构化VQA进行自动成功评估；4)有限状态机管理自主环境重置和数据路由

Result: 在仿真中达到90%成功率处理复杂长时域任务，远超传统基线；在真实世界中可靠执行多样接触丰富技能，无需领域特定微调

Conclusion: RADAR为机器人数据采集提供了高度可扩展的范式，通过完全自主的闭环系统将数据收集转化为自持续过程

Abstract: The acquisition of large-scale physical interaction data, a critical prerequisite for modern robot learning, is severely bottlenecked by the prohibitive cost and scalability limits of human-in-the-loop collection paradigms. To break this barrier, we introduce Robust Autonomous Data Acquisition for Robotics (RADAR), a fully autonomous, closed-loop data generation engine that completely removes human intervention from the collection cycle. RADAR elegantly divides the cognitive load into a four-module pipeline. Anchored by 2-5 3D human demonstrations as geometric priors, a Vision-Language Model first orchestrates scene-relevant task generation via precise semantic object grounding and skill retrieval. Next, a Graph Neural Network policy translates these subtasks into physical actions via in-context imitation learning. Following execution, the VLM performs automated success evaluation using a structured Visual Question Answering pipeline. Finally, to shatter the bottleneck of manual resets, a Finite State Machine orchestrates an autonomous environment reset and asymmetric data routing mechanism. Driven by simultaneous forward-reverse planning with a strict Last-In, First-Out causal sequence, the system seamlessly restores unstructured workspaces and robustly recovers from execution failures. This continuous brain-cerebellum synergy transforms data collection into a self-sustaining process. Extensive evaluations highlight RADAR's exceptional versatility. In simulation, our framework achieves up to 90% success rates on complex, long-horizon tasks, effortlessly solving challenges where traditional baselines plummet to near-zero performance. In real-world deployments, the system reliably executes diverse, contact-rich skills (e.g., deformable object manipulation) via few-shot adaptation without domain-specific fine-tuning, providing a highly scalable paradigm for robotic data acquisition.

</details>


### [46] [Energy Prediction on Sloping Ground for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11963)
*Mohamed Ounally,Cyrille Pierre,Johann Laconte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于标准车载传感器的简单能量模型，用于预测四足机器人在不同地形坡度和航向下的能量消耗，支持路径规划评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人在户外环境中的能量管理是重要挑战，续航能力直接影响任务成功率，而高效资源使用能减少生态影响。需要研究地形坡度和航向对四足运动能量消耗的影响。

Method: 引入仅依赖标准车载传感器的简单能量模型，避免专用仪器，适用于未探索环境。模型通过商用四足机器人的实地运行数据进行识别，表达为坡度和航向的紧凑函数。

Result: 自然地形实地验证显示：力等效成本与坡度角呈近似线性趋势；横向成本始终更高；轨迹段具有可加性行为，支持路径级能量预测用于规划导向评估。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了基于标准传感器的能量模型能有效预测四足机器人在不同地形条件下的能量消耗，为路径规划提供实用的能量评估工具。

Abstract: Energy management is a fundamental challenge for legged robots in outdoor environments. Endurance directly constrains mission success, while efficient resource use reduces ecological impact. This paper investigates how terrain slope and heading orientation influence the energetic cost of quadruped locomotion. We introduce a simple energy model that relies solely on standard onboard sensors, avoids specialized instrumentation, and remains applicable in previously unexplored environments. The model is identified from field runs on a commercial quadruped and expressed as a compact function of slope angle and heading. Field validation on natural terrain shows near-linear trends of force-equivalent cost with slope angle, consistently higher lateral costs, and additive behavior across trajectory segments, supporting path-level energy prediction for planning-oriented evaluation.

</details>


### [47] [Learning Visuomotor Policy for Multi-Robot Laser Tag Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11980)
*Kai Li,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端视觉运动策略用于多机器人激光标签任务，通过多智能体强化学习训练教师策略并蒸馏到视觉学生策略，在命中精度和避碰性能上超越传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模块化方法在多机器人激光标签任务中面临有限观测性、依赖深度映射和机器人间通信等挑战，需要更有效的解决方案。

Method: 使用多智能体强化学习训练高性能教师策略，通过知识蒸馏将其转移到端到端视觉运动学生策略；采用置换不变特征提取器和深度热图输入等技术设计提升性能。

Result: 该策略在命中精度上比传统方法提高16.7%，在碰撞避免上提高6%，并成功部署到真实机器人上。

Conclusion: 提出的端到端视觉运动策略有效解决了多机器人激光标签任务中的挑战，性能优于传统方法，并具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study multi robot laser tag, a simplified yet practical shooting-game-style task. Classic modular approaches on these tasks face challenges such as limited observability and reliance on depth mapping and inter robot communication. To overcome these issues, we present an end-to-end visuomotor policy that maps images directly to robot actions. We train a high performing teacher policy with multi agent reinforcement learning and distill its knowledge into a vision-based student policy. Technical designs, including a permutation-invariant feature extractor and depth heatmap input, improve performance over standard architectures. Our policy outperforms classic methods by 16.7% in hitting accuracy and 6% in collision avoidance, and is successfully deployed on real robots. Code will be released publicly.

</details>


### [48] [Sim-to-reality adaptation for Deep Reinforcement Learning applied to an underwater docking application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12020)
*Alaaeddine Chaarani,Narcis Palomeras,Pere Ridao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度强化学习的自主水下对接方法，使用Girona AUV和高保真数字孪生环境，通过多进程RL框架加速训练，实现了超过90%的模拟成功率和物理测试验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习为自主水下对接提供了鲁棒的控制方案，但"仿真到现实"的差距和高训练延迟是实际部署的主要瓶颈。需要开发一种能够加速训练并确保现实适应性的系统方法。

Method: 将Stonefish模拟器适配到多进程强化学习框架中，使用PPO算法训练6自由度控制策略。采用随机起始位置确保泛化性能，奖励函数考虑距离、方向、动作平滑度和自适应碰撞惩罚以促进软对接。

Result: 智能体在模拟环境中实现了超过90%的成功率。物理测试验证了仿真到现实的适应性，DRL控制器表现出涌现行为，如基于俯仰的制动和偏航振荡以辅助机械对准。

Conclusion: 提出的系统方法有效解决了自主水下对接中的仿真到现实差距问题，多进程RL框架显著加速了训练过程，物理测试验证了方法的实际可行性，为DRL在AUV控制中的实际应用提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) offers a robust alternative to traditional control methods for autonomous underwater docking, particularly in adapting to unpredictable environmental conditions. However, bridging the "sim-to-real" gap and managing high training latencies remain significant bottlenecks for practical deployment. This paper presents a systematic approach for autonomous docking using the Girona Autonomous Underwater Vehicle (AUV) by leveraging a high-fidelity digital twin environment. We adapted the Stonefish simulator into a multiprocessing RL framework to significantly accelerate the learning process while incorporating realistic AUV dynamics, collision models, and sensor noise. Using the Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm, we developed a 6-DoF control policy trained in a headless environment with randomized starting positions to ensure generalized performance. Our reward structure accounts for distance, orientation, action smoothness, and adaptive collision penalties to facilitate soft docking. Experimental results demonstrate that the agent achieved a success rate of over 90% in simulation. Furthermore, successful validation in a physical test tank confirmed the efficacy of the sim-to-reality adaptation, with the DRL controller exhibiting emergent behaviors such as pitch-based braking and yaw oscillations to assist in mechanical alignment.

</details>


### [49] [Flight through Narrow Gaps with Morphing-Wing Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12059)
*Julius Wanner,Hoang-Vu Phan,Charbel Toumieh,Dario Floreano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究人员开发了一种可变翼展的固定翼无人机，能够在飞行中收拢机翼以穿越小于翼展宽度的狭窄间隙，通过优化轨迹生成和非线性模型预测控制实现精确控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定翼无人机穿越狭窄间隙的能力受限于其翼展宽度，这限制了其在复杂环境（如森林、城市峡谷）中的自主飞行能力。受鸟类飞行启发，需要开发能够在飞行中临时收拢机翼以穿越亚翼展宽度间隙的无人机系统。

Method: 1. 开发了适用于低飞行速度和失速后攻角的通用翼展变形无人机气动模型；2. 将纵向无人机动力学集成到具有运行时自适应成本和约束的优化参考轨迹生成和非线性模型预测控制框架中；3. 在130克翼展变形无人机上进行验证。

Result: 在5-7 m/s的前向速度下，无人机穿越狭窄间隙时的平均高度误差为5厘米，同时能在可变阈值距离内强制机翼完全收拢。轨迹分析显示，无人机可以通过在间隙前加速和向上俯仰来补偿穿越间隙期间的升力损失，不同优化目标会导致不同的补偿程度。

Conclusion: 该研究成功实现了固定翼无人机穿越亚翼展宽度间隙的能力，通过翼展变形和先进控制策略的结合，为无人机在复杂环境中的自主飞行提供了新的解决方案，同时展示了在硬件上保持恒定前向飞行速度和近恒定高度的情况下也能实现精确间隙穿越。

Abstract: The size of a narrow gap traversable by a fixed-wing drone is limited by its wingspan. Inspired by birds, here, we enable the traversal of a gap of sub-wingspan width and height using a morphing-wing drone capable of temporarily sweeping in its wings mid-flight. This maneuver poses control challenges due to sudden lift loss during gap-passage at low flight speeds and the need for precisely timed wing-sweep actuation ahead of the gap. To address these challenges, we first develop an aerodynamic model for general wing-sweep morphing drone flight including low flight speeds and post-stall angles of attack. We integrate longitudinal drone dynamics into an optimal reference trajectory generation and Nonlinear Model Predictive Control framework with runtime adaptive costs and constraints. Validated on a 130 g wing-sweep-morphing drone, our method achieves an average altitude error of 5 cm during narrow-gap passage at forward speeds between 5 and 7 m/s, whilst enforcing fully swept wings near the gap across variable threshold distances. Trajectory analysis shows that the drone can compensate for lift loss during gap-passage by accelerating and pitching upwards ahead of the gap to an extent that differs between reference trajectory optimization objectives. We show that our strategy also allows for accurate gap passage on hardware whilst maintaining a constant forward flight speed reference and near-constant altitude.

</details>


### [50] [Decentralized Cooperative Localization for Multi-Robot Systems with Asynchronous Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12075)
*Nivand Khosravi,Niusha Khosravi,Mohammad Bozorg,Masoud S. Bahraini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种去中心化协同定位框架，用于GPS拒止环境中的非完整移动机器人，通过扩展卡尔曼滤波、异步传感器处理、自动坐标系对齐和双路标评估实现鲁棒定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GPS拒止、通信受限的环境中，传统定位方法效果有限，需要一种能够处理异步传感器数据、自动对齐坐标系并在特征稀疏环境中保持鲁棒性的去中心化协同定位方案。

Method: 基于扩展卡尔曼滤波的本地化框架，仅在通信可用时共享测量信息，处理异步传感器数据，通过变换矩阵实现自动坐标系对齐，并引入包含静态环境特征和移动机器人的双路标评估框架。

Result: 在Gazebo仿真和真实地下室环境中，DCL相比集中式协同定位减少34%的RMSE，双路标变体进一步提升56%，证明其在封闭空间、水下环境和特征稀疏地形中的适用性。

Conclusion: 提出的去中心化协同定位框架在GPS拒止环境中表现优异，通过自动坐标系对齐、异步数据处理和双路标评估实现了鲁棒定位，适用于传统方法无效的挑战性场景。

Abstract: Decentralized cooperative localization (DCL) is a promising approach for nonholonomic mobile robots operating in GPS-denied environments with limited communication infrastructure. This paper presents a DCL framework in which each robot performs localization locally using an Extended Kalman Filter, while sharing measurement information during update stages only when communication links are available and companion robots are successfully detected by LiDAR. The framework preserves cross-correlation consistency among robot state estimates while handling asynchronous sensor data with heterogeneous sampling rates and accommodating accelerations during dynamic maneuvers. Unlike methods that require pre-aligned coordinate systems, the proposed approach allows robots to initialize with arbitrary reference-frame orientations and achieves automatic alignment through transformation matrices in both the prediction and update stages. To improve robustness in feature-sparse environments, we introduce a dual-landmark evaluation framework that exploits both static environmental features and mobile robots as dynamic landmarks. The proposed framework enables reliable detection and feature extraction during sharp turns, while prediction accuracy is improved through information sharing from mutual observations. Experimental results in both Gazebo simulation and real-world basement environments show that DCL outperforms centralized cooperative localization (CCL), achieving a 34% reduction in RMSE, while the dual-landmark variant yields an improvement of 56%. These results demonstrate the applicability of DCL to challenging domains such as enclosed spaces, underwater environments, and feature-sparse terrains where conventional localization methods are ineffective.

</details>


### [51] [CRAFT: A Tendon-Driven Hand with Hybrid Hard-Soft Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12120)
*Leo Lin,Shivansh Patel,Jay Moon,Svetlana Lazebnik,Unnat Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CRAFT手是一种肌腱驱动的仿人机械手，采用混合刚柔顺应性设计，通过关节处使用软材料、连杆保持刚性的方式优化接触力分布，实现紧凑轻量且成本低廉的抓取系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机械手在接触丰富的操作任务中面临挑战，因为接触力在手上的分布不均匀——冲击集中在关节处，而连杆承担主要负载。需要一种能够优化接触力分布、提高耐用性同时保持紧凑轻量设计的仿人机械手。

Method: 采用混合刚柔顺应性设计：关节处使用软材料吸收冲击，连杆保持刚性以承载负载；使用滚动接触关节表面确保屈曲运动路径的可重复性；通过15个安装在手指上的电机驱动肌腱，实现紧凑的形态因子和轻量化的手指设计。

Result: 结构测试显示CRAFT在保持可比重复性的同时提高了强度和耐久性；遥操作测试中改善了脆弱和低摩擦物品的处理能力，能够覆盖Feix分类法中33/33种抓取类型；全系统成本低于600美元，并将开源发布。

Conclusion: CRAFT手通过创新的混合刚柔设计有效解决了接触力分布问题，在保持紧凑轻量设计的同时显著提升了机械手的耐用性和操作能力，为低成本开源仿人机械手系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce CRAFT hand, a tendon-driven anthropomorphic hand with hybrid hard-soft compliance for contact-rich manipulation. The design is based on a simple idea: contact is not uniform across the hand. Impacts concentrate at joints, while links carry most of the load. CRAFT places soft material at joints and keeps links rigid, and uses rollingcontact joint surfaces to keep flexion on repeatable motion paths. Fifteen motors mounted on the fingers drive the hand through tendons, keeping the form factor compact and the fingers light. In structural tests, CRAFT improves strength and endurance while maintaining comparable repeatability. In teleoperation, CRAFT improves handling of fragile and low-friction items, and the hand covers 33/33 grasps in the Feix taxonomy. The full design costs under $600 and will be released open-source with visionbased teleoperation and simulation integration. Project page: http://craft-hand.github.io/

</details>


### [52] [ComFree-Sim: A GPU-Parallelized Analytical Contact Physics Engine for Scalable Contact-Rich Robotics Simulation and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12185)
*Chetan Borse,Zhixian Xie,Wei-Cheng Huang,Wanxin Jin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ComFree-Sim：基于无互补性接触建模的GPU并行分析物理引擎，通过闭式计算接触冲量实现近线性运行时扩展，在密集接触场景中吞吐量比MuJoCo Warp高2-3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理仿真中接触分辨率是瓶颈，主流引擎通过互补性约束或约束优化强制执行非穿透和库仑摩擦，需要每步迭代求解，其成本随接触密度超线性增长。需要一种能够高效处理密集接触的仿真方法。

Method: 提出ComFree-Sim，基于无互补性接触建模的GPU并行分析物理引擎。采用阻抗式预测-校正更新在库仑摩擦对偶锥中闭式计算接触冲量。接触计算在接触对之间解耦，并在锥面可分离，自然映射到GPU内核。扩展为统一的6D接触模型，捕捉切向、扭转和滚动摩擦，并引入实用的对偶锥阻抗启发式方法。

Result: 与MuJoCo Warp（MJWarp）相比，ComFree-Sim在密集接触场景中展示近线性扩展和2-3倍更高的吞吐量，同时保持相当的物理保真度。在真实世界多指LEAP手的实时MPC中部署，以及在动态感知运动重定向中应用，证明低延迟仿真能提高闭环成功率并实现接触丰富任务中的实用高频控制。

Conclusion: ComFree-Sim通过无互补性接触建模和GPU并行化，解决了接触丰富机器人物理仿真的瓶颈问题，实现了高效的接触分辨率，为实时控制和高频任务提供了实用的仿真解决方案。

Abstract: Physics simulation for contact-rich robotics is often bottlenecked by contact resolution: mainstream engines enforce non-penetration and Coulomb friction via complementarity constraints or constrained optimization, requiring per-step iterative solves whose cost grows superlinearly with contact density. We present ComFree-Sim, a GPU-parallelized analytical contact physics engine built on complementarity-free contact modeling. ComFree-Sim computes contact impulses in closed form via an impedance-style prediction--correction update in the dual cone of Coulomb friction. Contact computation decouples across contact pairs and becomes separable across cone facets, mapping naturally to GPU kernels and yielding near-linear runtime scaling with the number of contacts. We further extend the formulation to a unified 6D contact model capturing tangential, torsional, and rolling friction, and introduce a practical dual-cone impedance heuristic. ComFree-Sim is implemented in Warp and exposed through a MuJoCo-compatible interface as a drop-in backend alternative to MuJoCo Warp (MJWarp). Experiments benchmark penetration, friction behaviors, stability, and simulation runtime scaling against MJWarp, demonstrating near-linear scaling and 2--3 times higher throughput in dense contact scenes with comparable physical fidelity. We deploy ComFree-Sim in real-time MPC for in-hand dexterous manipulation on a real-world multi-fingered LEAP hand and in dynamics-aware motion retargeting, demonstrating that low-latency simulation yields higher closed-loop success rates and enables practical high-frequency control in contact-rich tasks.

</details>


### [53] [SaPaVe: Towards Active Perception and Manipulation in Vision-Language-Action Models for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12193)
*Mengzhen Liu,Enshen Zhou,Cheng Chi,Yi Han,Shanyu Rong,Liming Chen,Pengwei Wang,Zhongyuan Wang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SaPaVe是一个端到端框架，通过解耦相机控制与机械臂操作动作，采用自底向上的训练策略，联合学习语义驱动的主动感知与鲁棒的执行能力，在真实世界任务中相比现有方法取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以统一语义驱动的主动感知与鲁棒、视角不变的执行能力。机器人需要在复杂场景中进行交互，需要同时具备主动感知环境和执行操作的能力，而当前方法在这两方面的协调存在不足。

Method: 1. 解耦相机控制与机械臂操作动作，而非将它们置于共享动作空间；2. 采用自底向上的训练策略：首先在大规模数据集上训练语义相机控制，然后使用混合数据联合优化两种动作类型；3. 引入3D几何感知模块，提高动态视角下的执行鲁棒性；4. 构建ActiveViewPose-200K数据集（20万图像-语言-相机运动对）用于语义相机运动学习。

Result: 1. 在仿真和真实环境中的大量实验表明，SaPaVe优于最近的视觉-语言-动作模型（如GR00T N1和π₀）；2. 在真实世界任务中成功率提高达31.25%；3. 提出了首个超越固定视角设置的主动操作基准ActiveManip-Bench。

Conclusion: 当感知与执行通过解耦但协调的策略进行训练时，紧密耦合的感知与执行能够实现高效且可泛化的主动操作。该方法展示了数据高效的端到端学习框架在机器人主动感知与操作任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Active perception and manipulation are crucial for robots to interact with complex scenes. Existing methods struggle to unify semantic-driven active perception with robust, viewpoint-invariant execution. We propose SaPaVe, an end-to-end framework that jointly learns these capabilities in a data-efficient manner. Our approach decouples camera and manipulation actions rather than placing them in a shared action space, and follows a bottom-up training strategy: we first train semantic camera control on a large-scale dataset, then jointly optimize both action types using hybrid data. To support this framework, we introduce ActiveViewPose-200K, a dataset of 200k image-language-camera movement pairs for semantic camera movement learning, and a 3D geometry-aware module that improves execution robustness under dynamic viewpoints. We also present ActiveManip-Bench, the first benchmark for evaluating active manipulation beyond fixed-view settings. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments show that SaPaVe outperforms recent vision-language-action models such as GR00T N1 and \(π_0\), achieving up to 31.25\% higher success rates in real-world tasks. These results show that tightly coupled perception and execution, when trained with decoupled yet coordinated strategies, enable efficient and generalizable active manipulation. Project page: https://lmzpai.github.io/SaPaVe

</details>


### [54] [HandelBot: Real-World Piano Playing via Fast Adaptation of Dexterous Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12243)
*Amber Xie,Haozhi Qi,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HandelBot框架通过两阶段流程实现精确的双手机器人钢琴演奏：首先通过结构化精炼修正空间对齐，然后通过残差强化学习学习精细校正动作，仅需30分钟物理交互数据即可显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧操作是机器人领域的长期挑战，高质量数据收集困难是主要瓶颈。虽然强化学习和仿真到现实迁移是可行方案，但对于毫米级精度的任务（如双手机器人钢琴演奏），迁移策略往往失败。

Method: 提出HandelBot框架，采用两阶段流程：1) 结构化精炼阶段：基于物理实验调整横向手指关节以修正空间对齐；2) 残差强化学习阶段：自主学习精细校正动作。从仿真训练策略开始，仅需30分钟物理交互数据。

Result: 在五首公认歌曲上进行广泛硬件实验，HandelBot成功实现精确双手机器人钢琴演奏。系统性能比直接仿真部署提升1.8倍，仅需30分钟物理交互数据。

Conclusion: HandelBot框架通过结合仿真策略和快速适应，有效解决了毫米级精度灵巧操作任务的数据收集难题，为高精度机器人操作提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Mastering dexterous manipulation with multi-fingered hands has been a grand challenge in robotics for decades. Despite its potential, the difficulty of collecting high-quality data remains a primary bottleneck for high-precision tasks. While reinforcement learning and simulation-to-real-world transfer offer a promising alternative, the transferred policies often fail for tasks demanding millimeter-scale precision, such as bimanual piano playing. In this work, we introduce HandelBot, a framework that combines a simulation policy and rapid adaptation through a two-stage pipeline. Starting from a simulation-trained policy, we first apply a structured refinement stage to correct spatial alignments by adjusting lateral finger joints based on physical rollouts. Next, we use residual reinforcement learning to autonomously learn fine-grained corrective actions. Through extensive hardware experiments across five recognized songs, we demonstrate that HandelBot can successfully perform precise bimanual piano playing. Our system outperforms direct simulation deployment by a factor of 1.8x and requires only 30 minutes of physical interaction data.

</details>


### [55] [HumDex:Humanoid Dexterous Manipulation Made Easy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12260)
*Liang Heng,Yihe Tang,Jiajun Xu,Henghui Bao,Di Huang,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HumDex是一个用于人形机器人全身灵巧操作的便携式遥操作系统，通过IMU运动跟踪和学习式重定向方法解决现有系统的局限性，并提出了两阶段模仿学习框架来高效收集高质量演示数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决人形机器人全身灵巧操作中高质量演示数据收集的瓶颈问题。现有遥操作系统存在便携性差、遮挡问题、精度不足等限制，难以应用于复杂的全身任务。

Method: 1. 开发HumDex便携式遥操作系统：采用IMU运动跟踪平衡便携性与精度；2. 提出学习式重定向方法生成平滑自然的手部动作；3. 设计两阶段模仿学习框架：先在多样化人类运动数据上预训练学习通用先验，然后在机器人数据上微调以弥合具身差距。

Result: 系统实现了准确的全身跟踪和灵巧手部控制，显著提高了对新配置、新物体和新背景的泛化能力，同时大幅降低了数据采集成本。整个系统完全可复现并已开源。

Conclusion: HumDex系统有效解决了人形机器人全身灵巧操作中演示数据收集的挑战，通过创新的遥操作技术和两阶段学习框架，为实现高效、高质量的机器人模仿学习提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates humanoid whole-body dexterous manipulation, where the efficient collection of high-quality demonstration data remains a central bottleneck.
  Existing teleoperation systems often suffer from limited portability, occlusion, or insufficient precision, which hinders their applicability to complex whole-body tasks. To address these challenges, we introduce HumDex, a portable teleoperation system designed for humanoid whole-body dexterous manipulation. Our system leverages IMU-based motion tracking to address the portability-precision trade-off, enabling accurate full-body tracking while remaining easy to deploy. For dexterous hand control, we further introduce a learning-based retargeting method that generates smooth and natural hand motions without manual parameter tuning. Beyond teleoperation, HumDex enables efficient collection of human motion data. Building on this capability, we propose a two-stage imitation learning framework that first pre-trains on diverse human motion data to learn generalizable priors, and then fine-tunes on robot data to bridge the embodiment gap for precise execution. We demonstrate that this approach significantly improves generalization to new configurations, objects, and backgrounds with minimal data acquisition costs. The entire system is fully reproducible and open-sourced at https://github.com/physical-superintelligence-lab/HumDex.

</details>
