### [56] [Exceptional theta correspondences via Plancherel formulas for rank one symmetric spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11401)
*Jan Frahm,Quentin Labriet*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了简单分裂Jordan代数上共形群的最小表示，确定了其对偶对G×G'的限制分解，建立了G与G'表示的一一对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究简单分裂Jordan代数上共形群的最小表示，探索其对偶对G×G'的表示论结构，建立群表示之间的对应关系。

Method: 考虑实数或复数域上简单分裂Jordan代数的共形群最小表示，分析其对偶对G×G'的限制，其中G是Jordan代数的自同构群，G'是PSL(2,R)、PGL(2,R)或PGL(2,C)。通过直接积分分解确定最小表示在G×G'上的分解。

Result: 明确确定了最小表示在G×G'上的直接积分分解，建立了G的某些表示与G'的有限覆盖表示之间的一一对应关系。这些G的表示出现在G的秩1对称空间的Plancherel测度支撑中。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了简单分裂Jordan代数共形群最小表示的结构特性，建立了对偶群表示之间的精确对应关系，并利用秩1对称空间的Plancherel公式证明了这一对应。

Abstract: We consider the minimal representation of (a finite cover of) the conformal group of a simple split Jordan algebra over $\mathbb{R}$ or $\mathbb{C}$, whenever it exists. The conformal group contains a natural dual pair $G\times G'$, where $G$ is essentially the automorphism group of the Jordan algebra and $G'$ is either $\operatorname{PSL}(2,\mathbb{R})$, $\operatorname{PGL}(2,\mathbb{R})$ or $\operatorname{PGL}(2,\mathbb{C})$. The groups $G$ that arise in this way include the complex exceptional group of type $F_4$ as well as its compact and split real form. We explicitly determine the direct integral decomposition of the minimal representation restricted to the corresponding cover of $G\times G'$. This yields a one-to-one correspondence between certain representations of $G$ and (a finite cover of) $G'$. The representations of $G$ that occur in this correspondence are in the support of the Plancherel measure for a rank one symmetric space for $G$, and the proof makes use of the corresponding Plancherel formula.

</details>


### [57] [Tensor Products and the Stable Green Ring of the Symmetric Group Algebra $F\mathfrak{S}_p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11533)
*Manzu Kua,Kay Jin Lim*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 给出了对称群代数F𝔖_p中任意两个不可分解非投射模的张量积模投射模的分解显式公式，证明了两个单模的张量积模投射是半单的，并计算了所有此类不可分解非投射模的Benson-Symonds不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对称群代数F𝔖_p中不可分解非投射模的张量积结构，特别是模投射模的分解行为，这对于理解模表示论中的张量积运算和分类问题具有重要意义。

Method: 通过显式公式方法，推导出任意两个不可分解非投射模的张量积模投射模的分解公式，并计算相关的Benson-Symonds不变量。

Result: 1. 得到了任意两个不可分解非投射模的张量积模投射模的显式分解公式；2. 证明了两个单模的张量积模投射是半单的；3. 计算了所有不可分解非投射模的Benson-Symonds不变量。

Conclusion: 该工作完全刻画了对称群代数F𝔖_p中不可分解非投射模的张量积模投射模的结构，为模表示论中的张量积运算提供了完整的理论框架和具体计算方法。

Abstract: We give an explicit formula for the decomposition of the tensor product of any two indecomposable non-projective modules for the symmetric group algebra $F \mathfrak{S}_p$ modulo projective modules. In particular, we show that the tensor product of two simple modules is semisimple modulo projectives. We also compute the Benson--Symonds invariants for all such indecomposable non-projective modules.

</details>

*Cheng Meng*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 证明了循环p群表示环可嵌入实函数代数，以及平凡模的syzygies和cosyzygies直和的n次张量积的非投射部分维数渐近行为为Cγ^n n^α，利用非整数指数α否定了Benson和Symonds的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究模表示论中循环群和交换p群的表示渐近行为，特别是平凡模的syzygies和cosyzygies的张量幂的维数增长模式，并回答Benson和Symonds关于Ω-代数模核心维数递归性的问题

Method: 将循环p群的表示环嵌入实函数代数，分析平凡模的syzygies和cosyzygies直和的n次张量积的非投射部分维数，证明其渐近形式为Cγ^n n^α，其中α为非整数指数

Result: 1) 循环p群表示环可嵌入实函数代数；2) 平凡模的syzygies和cosyzygies直和的n次张量积的非投射部分维数渐近于Cγ^n n^α；3) 利用非整数指数α的存在性，证明存在Ω-代数模使得M⊗n的核心维数不是最终递归的，从而否定了Benson和Symonds的问题

Conclusion: 模表示论中某些张量幂的维数增长具有非整数指数的渐近行为，这种行为导致Ω-代数模的核心维数可能不是最终递归的，为表示论中的渐近分析提供了新的视角和反例

Abstract: In this paper, we prove some results on the asymptotic behavior arising in the representation theory of modular representations of cyclic groups and syzygies and cosyzygies of the trivial module over abelian $p$-groups. We show that the representation ring of a cyclic $p$-group can be embedded into a real algebra of functions, and that the dimension of the non-projective part of $n$-th tensor power of a direct sum of syzygies and cosyzygies of the trivial module is approximately $Cγ^nn^α$. Using the existence of a non-integer exponent $α$, we answer a question of Benson and Symonds in negative, that is, there is an $Ω$-algebraic module where the dimension of the core of $M^{\otimes n}$ is not eventually recursive.

</details>


### [59] [A metrically complete and Krull--Schmidt space of multiparameter persistence modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12049)
*Ulrich Bauer,Cameron Gusel,Luis Scoccola*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了q-tame多参数持久性模块的可观测范畴具有良好的度量和代数性质：构成关于交错距离的完备度量空间，满足Krull-Schmidt定理，且度量和代数结构兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为多参数持久性分析建立合适的范畴框架，统一文献中已有的各种范畴，并证明该框架具有良好的数学性质，为多参数持久性理论提供坚实的数学基础。

Method: 研究q-tame多参数持久性模块的可观测范畴，分析其度量结构（交错距离）、代数结构（Krull-Schmidt分解），证明两者的兼容性，并与其他文献中的范畴进行比较。

Result: 1. 可观测范畴关于交错距离构成完备度量空间；2. 满足Krull-Schmidt定理，每个对象有唯一不可分解分解；3. 距离为零当且仅当同构；4. 文献中许多范畴是该范畴的完全子范畴；5. 刻画了预紧集与有限表示型的关系。

Conclusion: q-tame多参数持久性模块的可观测范畴是研究多参数持久性的合适框架，具有良好的度量和代数性质，能够统一现有理论，并为后续分析提供数学保证。

Abstract: We show that the observable category of q-tame multiparameter persistence modules satisfies good metric and algebraic properties: it forms a complete metric space with respect to the interleaving distance, and it is Krull--Schmidt in the sense that every object admits an essentially unique decomposition into indecomposables. Moreover, we show that these metric and algebraic structures are compatible: two objects are at distance zero if and only if they are isomorphic. We argue that the observable category of q-tame multiparameter persistence modules is the right setup for multiparameter persistence by showing that many of the categories already considered in the literature form full subcategory of this category. We also characterize precompact sets in terms of finite representation type of certain discretizations, and show that the image of several of the main constructions in multiparameter persistence is precompact.

</details>


### [60] [Construction of Local Arthur Packets for Metaplectic Groups and the Adams Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11602)
*Jiahe Chen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为特征零的非阿基米德局部域上的覆盖群显式构造了局部Arthur包，推广了Atobe对经典群的构造，证明了这些包是重数自由的，并将Moeglin的Adams猜想推广到覆盖群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 覆盖群（metaplectic groups）的局部Arthur包构造在现有文献中尚未完全解决。本文旨在将经典群上已有的Arthur包构造理论推广到覆盖群，建立覆盖群表示理论中的局部Arthur包理论框架。

Method: 采用显式构造方法，将Atobe为经典群构造局部Arthur包的技术推广到覆盖群。具体包括：1) 定义覆盖群上的局部Arthur参数；2) 构造对应的表示包；3) 证明重数自由性；4) 将Moeglin关于Adams猜想的结果推广到覆盖群。

Result: 成功构造了覆盖群的局部Arthur包，证明了这些包是重数自由的（即包中每个表示最多出现一次）。同时将Moeglin的Adams猜想推广到覆盖群，建立了覆盖群表示与经典群表示之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 本文建立了覆盖群局部Arthur包的系统理论，为覆盖群的表示分类和Langlands对应提供了重要工具。构造的显式性和重数自由性为后续研究覆盖群的表示理论和自守形式奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this article, we explicitly construct local Arthur packets for metaplectic groups over non-Archimedean local fields of characteristic zero. Our construction is a generalization of Atobe's construction of local Arthur packets for classical groups. As a result, we prove that the local Arthur packets are multiplicity free. Moreover, we generalize Moeglin's earlier work about the Adams conjecture to metaplectic groups.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 1. 两个李代数的自同构群均为 $\mathbf{GSp}_{N}(\mathbb{Z})\ltimes (\mathbb{\mathbb{K}}^{\times})^{N}$；2. 哈密顿李代数的所有导子都是内导子；3. 计算了哈密顿李代数导出子代数的完整导子空间

Conclusion: 完全确定了哈密顿李代数及其导出子代数的自同构群和导子代数结构，揭示了这些代数的对称性和导子性质

Abstract: In this paper, we compute the automorphism group and derivation algebra of the Hamiltonian Lie algebra $\mathcal{H}_{N}$ and its derived subalgebra $\mathcal{H}_{N}'$, where $N$ is an even positive integer. The automorphism groups are shown to be $\mathbf{GSp}_{N}(\mathbb{Z})\ltimes (\mathbb{\mathbb{K}}^{\times})^{N}$ for both Lie algebras and we prove that all derivations are inner for the Hamiltonian Lie algebra, also we compute the full derivation space for the derived subalgebra of Hamiltonian Lie algebra.

</details>

Method: 使用Kraft quivers的概念，对有限维K-向量空间上满足FV=VF=0的σ-线性算子F和σ^{-1}-线性算子V进行分类证明

Result: 给出了满足FV=VF=0条件的算子对(F,V)的完整分类，该分类结果最初由Gelfand-Ponomarev和Kraft获得

Conclusion: 通过Kraft quivers的概念重新表述了经典分类结果，提供了更简洁自包含的证明，有助于理解这类算子结构的本质

Abstract: In this expository paper, given a field $K$ and an automorphism $σ\in \mathrm{Aut}(K)$, we give a self-contained proof of the classification of finite dimensional $K$-vector spaces equipped with two operators $F$ and $V$, respectively $σ$-linear and $σ^{-1}$-linear, such that $FV = VF = 0$. This classification was originally due to the combined results of Gelfand and Ponomarev (1968), and of Kraft (1975). Following a recent suggestion of Chai (2025), we reworked their classification in light of the notion of Kraft quivers.

</details>

### [63] [On the energy dissipation rate of ensemble eddy viscosity models of turbulence: Shear flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11124)
*William Layton*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 研究探讨了基于NSE系综的涡粘模型是否会过度扩散，发现该方法相比传统涡粘模型具有更低的扩散性和更高的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统涡粘模型通常需要构建混合长度和湍流动能的近似解，这些近似往往精度未知且容易过度扩散，需要额外的修正措施（如壁面定律或区域划分）。而基于NSE系综的新方法似乎具有更低的复杂度和更高的精度，并能产生正确的近壁渐近行为，因此需要验证该方法是否也存在过度扩散问题。

Method: 通过求解带有扰动数据的NSE系综，计算系综平均值和脉动量，直接计算湍流粘度参数化。该方法避免了传统涡粘模型中需要求解非线性对流-扩散-反应方程和构造混合长度近似的问题。

Result: 研究表明，基于NSE系综的涡粘方法相比传统涡粘模型具有更低的扩散性，不会过度扩散解，同时保持了更高的精度和正确的近壁渐近行为。

Conclusion: 系综涡粘方法是一种有前景的替代方案，它避免了传统涡粘模型的过度扩散问题，具有更低的复杂度和更高的精度，能够直接产生正确的湍流粘度参数化。

Abstract: Classical eddy viscosity models add a viscosity term with turbulent viscosity coefficient developed beginning with the Kolmogorov-Prandtl parameterization. Approximations of unknown accuracy of the unknown mixing lengths and turbulent kinetic energy are typically constructed by solving associated systems of nonlinear convection-diffusion-reaction equations with nonlinear boundary conditions. Often these over-diffuse so additional fixes are added such as wall laws or using different approximations in different regions (which must also be specified). Alternately, one can solve an ensemble of NSE's with perturbed data, compute the ensemble mean and fluctuation and simply compute directly the turbulent viscosity parameterization. This idea is recent, seems to be of lower complexity and greater accuracy and produces parameterizations with the correct near wall asymptotic behavior. The question then arises: Does this ensemble eddy viscosity approach over-diffuse solutions? This question is addressed herein.

</details>


### [64] [Explicit Discrete Solution for Some Optimization Problems and Estimations with Respect to the Exact Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11313)
*Julieta Bollati,Mariela C. Olguin,Domingo A. Tarzia*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该研究通过有限差分法离散化稳态热传导系统，建立了离散优化问题并证明了收敛性，特别指出使用三点差分格式可将Neumann或Robin边界条件的收敛阶从O(h)提升到O(h²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多维有界域中稳态热传导系统的优化问题，针对两种边界条件系统（混合边界条件和包含对流边界条件的系统），建立离散化模型以解决连续优化问题在复杂域中的计算困难。

Method: 采用有限差分法离散化Poisson方程及其边界条件，建立离散系统(S^h)和(S^h_α)，对应三个优化问题(P^h_i)和(P^h_{iα})。特别使用三点有限差分近似处理Neumann或Robin边界条件。

Result: 获得了离散系统的显式解，证明了当h→0和α→∞时的收敛性和误差估计。数值模拟验证了理论结果，并证明三点差分格式将全局收敛阶从O(h)提升到O(h²)。

Conclusion: 有限差分离散化方法能有效解决热传导系统的优化问题，三点差分格式显著提高了边界条件处理的精度，为复杂域中的热传导优化提供了有效的数值计算方法。

Abstract: We consider two steady-state heat conduction systems called, $S$ and $S_α$, in a multidimensional bounded domain $D$ for the Poisson equation with source energy $g$. In one system, we impose mixed boundary conditions (temperature $b$ on the boundary $Γ_1$, heat flux $q$ on $Γ_2$ and an adiabatic condition on $Γ_3$). In the other system, the condition on $Γ_1$ is replaced by a convective heat flux condition with coefficient $α$. For each of these systems, we consider three associated optimization problems $(P_{i})$ and $(P_{iα})$, $i=1,2,3$, where the variable is the source energy $g$, the heat flux $q$ and the environmental temperature $b$, respectively. In the particular case where $D$ is a rectangle, the explicit continuous optimization variables and the corresponding state of the systems are known. In the present work, by using a finite difference scheme, we obtain the discrete systems $({S^h})$ and ${(S^h_α)}$ and discrete optimization problems ${(P^h_i)}$ and ${(P^h_{i α})}$, $i=1,2,3$, where $h$ is the space step in the discretization. Explicit discrete solutions are found, and convergence and estimation errors results are proved when $h$ goes to zero and when $α$ goes to infinity. Moreover, some numerical simulations are provided in order to test theoretical results. Finally, we note that the use of a three-point finite-difference approximation for the Neumann or Robin boundary condition at the boundary improves the global order of convergence from $O(h)$ to $O(h^2)$.

</details>


### [65] [Physics-based Approximation and Prediction of Speedlines in Compressor Performance Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11317)
*Abdul-Malik Akiev,Danyal Ergür,Alexander Schirger,Matthias Müller,Alexander Hinterleitner,Thomas Bartz-Beielstein*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于物理的压缩机性能图重建方法，通过超椭圆拟合速度线，将每条速度线编码为紧凑可解释的向量参数，实现从稀疏测量数据中重建完整性能图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩机性能图中的速度线对于理解和预测压缩机在不同工况下的行为至关重要。传统方法需要大量测量数据，而实际应用中往往只能获得稀疏测量数据，因此需要开发能够从有限数据中重建完整性能图的物理基础方法。

Method: 基于Llamas等人的公式，开发了一个鲁棒的两阶段拟合流程：第一阶段进行全局搜索，第二阶段进行局部细化。使用超椭圆函数拟合每条速度线，并将其编码为包含喘振、阻塞、曲率和形状参数的紧凑向量表示。

Result: 该方法在不同类型的涡轮增压器工业数据集上进行了验证。讨论了内插和外推的预测质量、度量敏感性，并展示了该方法在从有限数据重建完整压缩机性能图方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为压缩机性能图重建提供了有效的物理基础框架。未来可通过物理信息约束、替代函数族以及物理-机器学习混合映射来改进边界行为，最终实现从有限数据中完全重建压缩机性能图。

Abstract: Speedlines in compressor performance maps (CPMs) are critical for understanding and predicting compressor behavior under various operating conditions. We investigate a physics-based method for reconstructing compressor performance maps from sparse measurements by fitting each speedline with a superellipse and encoding it as a compact, interpretable vector (surge, choke, curvature, and shape parameters). Building on the formulation of Llamas et al., we develop a robust two-stage fitting pipeline that couples global search with local refinement. The approach is validated on industrial data-sets for different turbocharger types. We discuss prediction quality for inter- and extrapolation, metric sensitivities and outline opportunities for physics-informed constraints, alternative function families, and hybrid physics-ML mappings to improve boundary behavior and, ultimately, enable full CPM reconstruction from limited data.

</details>

Main category: math.NA

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: This work presents a new design for broadband absorption of low-frequency acoustic waves using a thin coating made of subwavelength acoustic resonators arranged periodically on a reflective surface. We first study the associated scattering problem and the corresponding subwavelength resonance problem, and then derive analytical approximations for the resonant frequencies and the reflection coefficient in terms of the periodic capacitance matrix in a half-space with a Dirichlet boundary condition. These approximations yield an effective macroscopic description of the coating via an impedance boundary condition and clarify the mechanism of superabsorption through an approximate coupling condition. Moreover, they lead to a reduced order model that enables efficient evaluation of the scattered waves over a frequency band and accelerates broadband absorption design. Building on this reduced order model, we develop a gradient based shape optimization method using shape derivatives of the resonant quantities to achieve broadband absorption. Numerical experiments demonstrate the broadband performance and the effectiveness of the proposed design procedure.

Abstract: The starting point of this paper is that a spectral method is essentially a combination of an orthonormal basis of the underlying Hilbert space with Galerkin conditions. The choice of an orthonormal basis depends on a number of desirable features which we explore in the context of spectral methods for time-dependent partial differential equations in a single space dimension.
  A central role in ensuring many of the above features is played by the differentiation matrix of the underlying orthonormal system. In particular, it is beneficial if this matrix is skew-symmetric and tridiagonal. While orthonormal systems with this feature have been characterised in A. Iserles & M. Webb, ``Orthogonal systems with a skew-symmetric differentiation matrix'', Found. Comput. Maths, 19 (2019), 1191--1221, employing Fourier transforms, in this paper we provide an alternative characterisation using the differential Lanczos algorithm, which can be implemented constructively. It is valid for inner products that obey an `integration-by-parts condition', inclusive of $L_2$ and Sobolev norms on the real line.
  Motivated by quest for integration methods that conserve Hamiltonian energy, we conclude the paper replacing inner products by more general sesquilinear forms and presenting preliminary results. Here the Fourier transform characterisation generalises to spectral theory of Schrödinger operators and the differential Lanczos algorithm generalises to the differential Arnoldi algorithm.

</details>

Abstract: Meshless methods are often used in numerical simulations of systems of partial differential equations (PDEs), particularly those which involve complex geometries or free surfaces. Here we present a novel compact scheme based on the local anisotropic basis function method (LABFM), a meshless method which provides approximations to spatial operators to arbitrary polynomial consistency. Our approach mimics compact finite-differences by using implicit stencils to optimise the resolving power of each operator, whilst retaining diagonal dominance of the resulting global sparse linear system. The new method is demonstrated to provide improved approximations by a series of convergence tests and resolving power analysis, before solutions to canonical PDEs are computed. Significant gains in accuracy are observed, in particular for solutions containing high wavenumber components. Our compact meshfree method provides a pathway to high-order simulations of PDEs in complex geometries with spectral-like resolving power, and has the potential to lead to a step-change in the accuracy of numerical solutions to such problems.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate a mixed finite element method for the spatial discretization of a time-fractional Allen--Cahn equation defined on a convex polyhedral domain, combined with a nonuniform Alikhanov scheme for the temporal approximation. Under suitable regularity assumptions on the initial data that are weaker than those typically imposed in the literature, we establish regularity results for the solution and its flux. We then derive optimal $L^2$-error estimates, up to a logarithmic factor, for both the solution and the flux. The estimates are robust with respect to the fractional order $α$, in the sense that the associated constants remain bounded as $α\to 1^{-}$. Numerical experiments are presented to confirm the theoretical findings.

### [70] [Cayley Commutator-free Methods for Krotov-Type Algorithms in Quantum Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11697)
*Boris Wembe,Usman Ali,Torsten Meier,Sina Ober-Blöbaum*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出基于无交换子Cayley积分器的量子最优控制结构保持数值方法，避免矩阵指数和交换子计算，显著降低计算成本同时保持高阶精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子最优控制问题中，传统基于矩阵指数的传播方法计算成本高昂，特别是对于长时间或高度振荡动力学。需要开发既能保持量子系统结构特性（如幺正性、对称性）又计算高效的方法。

Method: 基于Krotov框架，使用Cayley型方案重新表述前向和后向传播步骤，保持离散层面的幺正性和对称性。采用无交换子Cayley积分器，避免矩阵指数和交换子计算。对于非线性薛定谔方程和Gross-Pitaevskii方程，使用Cayley多项式插值策略扩展方法。

Result: CF-Cayley方法在状态转移问题中达到与高阶指数或Cayley-Magnus方案相同的精度，但计算成本显著降低。在非线性体系中，方法的结构保持特性确保了稳定性和范数守恒，适用于大规模量子控制模拟。

Conclusion: 该方法桥接了几何积分和最优控制，为现有基于指数的传播器提供了高效可靠的替代方案，特别适合长时间或高度振荡动力学的量子控制问题。

Abstract: This paper presents a class of structure-preserving numerical methods for quantum optimal control problems, based on commutator-free Cayley integrators. Starting from the Krotov framework, we reformulate the forward and backward propagation steps using Cayley-type schemes that preserve unitarity and symmetry at the discrete level. This approach eliminates the need for matrix exponentials and commutators, leading to significant computational savings while maintaining higher-order accuracy. We first recall the standard linear setting and then extend the formulation to nonlinear Schrödinger and Gross-Pitaevskii equations using a Cayley-polynomial interpolation strategy. Numerical experiments on state-transfer problems illustrate that the CF-Cayley method achieves the same accuracy as high-order exponential or Cayley-Magnus schemes at substantially lower cost, especially for longtime or highly oscillatory dynamics. In the nonlinear regime, the structure-preserving properties of the method ensure stability and norm conservation, making it a robust tool for large-scale quantum control simulations. The proposed framework thus bridges geometric integration and optimal control, offering an efficient and reliable alternative to existing exponential-based propagators.

</details>


### [71] [Debye Relaxation in Model-Based Multi-Dimensional Magnetic Particle Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11708)
*Vladyslav Gapyak,Thomas März,Andreas Weinmann*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该研究将Debye模型引入磁粒子成像，实现了包含弛豫效应的多维FFP扫描数据全模型重建，无需模型传递函数预处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Langevin模型的MPI重建方法需要模型传递函数进行数据预处理，且包含弛豫效应的模型要么限于1D扫描，要么仅适用于模拟数据，缺乏适用于真实多维FFP扫描数据的全模型重建方法。

Method: 采用多维Debye模型描述弛豫效应，证明Debye模型信号是线性时不变系统对Langevin模型信号的指数记忆响应。提出包含弛豫适应步骤的三阶段重建算法，将弛豫适应信号表达为Langevin理论导出的MPI核心算子。

Result: 实现了真实2D MPI数据的全模型重建，无需特定模型传递函数，算法计算成本低，弛豫适应步骤与输入数据呈线性关系。

Conclusion: 成功将Debye模型应用于多维FFP扫描MPI重建，首次实现了包含弛豫效应的全模型重建，为真实数据应用提供了更准确的模型基础。

Abstract: Model-based reconstruction approaches for the medical imaging modality Magnetic Particle Imaging (MPI) are typically based on the Langevin model, which assumes instantaneous alignment of the particles magnetic momenta with the applied field. Regarding the application to real data, Langevin model-based reconstruction methods require model transfer functions (MTF) obtained from calibrations to preprocess the data. There are also model-based reconstruction approaches that include relaxation effects and other particle-level dynamics. However, they are limited either to 1D or 1D-like scanning scenarios when considering real data, or are limited to simulated data in the case of multi-dimensional field-free point (FFP) MPI. Thus, fully model-based reconstructions from multi-dimensional FFP scanning data that incorporate relaxation effects without using an MTF have not yet been demonstrated. In this work, we incorporate relaxation effects by considering a multi-dimensional Debye model and provide reconstruction formulae. In particular, we show that the Debye model-based signal is the response of a linear time-invariant system with exponential memory applied to a Langevin model-based signal. We provide a reconstruction algorithm for the introduced multi-dimensional Debye model. To this end, we devise a relaxation adaption step. For the resulting relaxation-adapted Debye signal, we show that it can be expressed by the well-studied MPI core operator derived from the Langevin theory. This results in a three-stage algorithm with low additional cost over the Langevin model, as the relaxation adaption scales linearly in the input data. We provide numerical results for the proposed algorithmic approach. In particular, we obtain fully model-based reconstructions from real 2D MPI data without involving any specific MTF analogous to the Langevin model case.

</details>

*Falko Ruppenthal,Dmitri Kuzmin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于浅水方程反演的水深地形重建方法，利用自由表面观测数据通过最优控制框架和稀疏正则化技术稳定地重建不连续或陡变的水深地形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的水深地形数据对浅水流预测至关重要，但直接测量成本高且耗时。遥感平台可提供准确的自由表面观测数据，这促使开发数据驱动的重建策略：通过反演浅水方程来估计与观测数据最匹配的水深地形。

Method: 提出一种直接重建技术，将水深地形重建问题表述为最优控制问题。成本函数惩罚模拟与测量自由表面高程之间的偏差，同时强制执行流动动力学的状态方程。为抑制噪声并保留尖锐深度变化，框架增加了L1正则化和全变分去噪，这些稀疏促进项鼓励分段平滑解。

Result: 在合成噪声数据和不连续水深地形上的数值实验表明，该方法在重建未知水深地形方面表现出稳健性能，能够有效处理数据扰动和不稳定性问题。

Conclusion: 该方法通过结合最优控制框架和稀疏正则化技术，成功解决了水深地形反演中的不适定问题，为利用遥感观测数据重建复杂水深地形提供了一种有效且稳定的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of shallow water flows relies on precise bottom topography data, yet direct bathymetric surveys are expensive and time-consuming. In contrast, remote sensing platforms such as radar or satellite altimetry provide accurate free surface observations. This disparity motivates a data-driven reconstruction strategy: invert the shallow water equations to estimate the bathymetry that yields the best fit to the governing dynamics. We introduce a new direct reconstruction technique that extracts bathymetric features from widely available free surface measurements. The underlying inverse problem of determining an unknown bathymetry profile from observed wave elevations is inherently ill-posed. Small perturbations in the data may lead to large deviations in the reconstructed topography, and discontinuities or sharp gradients further exacerbate instability. To stabilize the inversion, we formulate an optimal-control problem, wherein a cost functional penalizes deviations between simulated and measured free surface elevation while enforcing a state equation for the flow dynamics. To suppress noise and preserve sharp depth variations, the framework is augmented with $L^1$ regularization and total variation denoising. These sparsity-promoting terms encourage piecewise-smooth solutions, allowing changes in the bathymetry to be captured without excessive smoothing. Numerical experiments on synthetic noisy data and discontinuous bathymetry demonstrate robust performance in reconstructing unknown bathymetry.

</details>

*Fuminori Tatsuoka,Yuto Miyatake,Tomohiro Sogabe*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于相似变换矩阵数值范围的方法，用于计算具有特定结构矩阵的指数函数作用，克服了传统方法中数值范围计算困难的问题。

Motivation: 当矩阵具有结构 A = τM⁻¹K 时，传统基于数值范围的有理逼近方法面临挑战：数值范围的边界框难以计算，且数值范围可能过大导致有理逼近构造困难。

Abstract: Several methods for computing the action of the matrix exponential $\mathrm{e}^{\boldsymbol{A}} \boldsymbol{b}$ are expressed by substituting $\boldsymbol{A}$ into a rational approximation of the scalar exponential function. The error of such methods can be estimated using the numerical range of $\boldsymbol{A}$, which enables the computation of $\mathrm{e}^{\boldsymbol{A}}\boldsymbol{b}$ with a prescribed accuracy. However, when the input matrix has the structure $\boldsymbol{A} = τ\boldsymbol{M}^{-1} \boldsymbol{K}$, this approach is challenging because computing the bounding box of numerical range is difficult and the numerical range may be too large to construct rational approximations on it. In this paper, focusing on the case where $\boldsymbol{M}$ is a well-conditioned symmetric positive definite matrix, we propose considering the numerical range of a similarity transformed matrix of $\boldsymbol{A}$. The numerical range of transformed matrix is not only numerically computable but can also be theoretically bounded depending on properties of $\boldsymbol{K}$. Numerical experiments confirm that the computations can be performed within the prescribed error tolerance.

</details>

Abstract: In this work, we present a mixed precision algorithm that leverages the Gram matrix and Jacobi methods to compute the singular value decomposition (SVD) of tall-and-skinny matrices. By constructing the Gram matrix in higher precision and coupling it with a Jacobi algorithm, our theoretical analysis and numerical experiments both indicate that the singular values computed by this mixed precision thin SVD algorithm attain high relative accuracy. In practice, our mixed precision thin SVD algorithm yields speedups of over 10x on a single CPU and about 2x on distributed memory systems when compared with traditional thin SVD methods.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究螺旋度守恒在磁弛豫过程中的重要性，以及不同数值方法中螺旋度守恒水平对计算结果的影响。理想MHD中螺旋度局部守恒，而泰勒弛豫等电阻理论仅保持全局螺旋度，这对结构保持数值方法有重要影响。

Method: 比较三种有限元方法：1）不守恒螺旋度的无约束方案；2）基于有限元外微积分、保持所有局部螺旋度的混合方法；3）仅强制全局螺旋度守恒的拉格朗日乘子法。在编织和打结磁场上进行数值实验。

Result: 局部螺旋度保持方法防止了虚假重联，在理想MHD或磁摩擦中保持了非平凡拓扑结构；而仅强制全局螺旋度守恒的方法允许通过局部重联进一步弛豫。数值结果展示了磁结和编织结构的演化。

Conclusion: 不同离散螺旋度约束水平显著影响磁弛豫和平衡结构。局部螺旋度保持对防止虚假重联和维持拓扑结构至关重要，而仅全局守恒允许更多弛豫途径。这为结构保持数值方法设计提供了指导。

Abstract: Magnetic relaxation drives plasma toward lower-energy equilibria under helicity constraints. In ideal magnetohydrodynamics (MHD), helicity is locally conserved, while resistive theories such as Taylor relaxation preserve only global helicity. This distinction has important implications for structure-preserving numerical methods. We compare three finite element formulations: an unconstrained scheme that does not conserve helicity, a mixed method based on finite element exterior calculus that preserves all local helicities, and a Lagrange multiplier approach that enforces only global helicity conservation. Numerical experiments on braided and knotted magnetic fields show that local helicity preservation prevents spurious reconnection and maintains nontrivial topology in ideal MHD or magneto-friction, whereas enforcing only global helicity allows further relaxation through local reconnection. Numerical results on magnetic knots and braids are provided. These results clarify how different levels of discrete helicity constraints influence magnetic relaxation and equilibrium structure in numerical computation.

</details>


### [76] [Parameter unbounded Uzawa and penalty-splitted accelerated algorithms for frictionless contact problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12205)
*Daria Koliesnikova,Isabelle Ramière*

Main category: math.NA

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统接触问题求解方法需要处理鞍点问题或病态惩罚矩阵，计算效率低且收敛性受参数限制，需要一种仅使用标准刚度矩阵且参数鲁棒的高效求解框架

Method: 提出两阶段不动点算法：1) 给定接触力计算位移；2) 基于位移更新接触力。采用位移-力分裂策略，拉格朗日乘子法对应Uzawa迭代，惩罚法对应算子分裂策略。应用Crossed-Secant不动点加速策略提升收敛速度

Result: 框架仅需标准刚度矩阵，无需处理鞍点或病态惩罚矩阵，可重复使用矩阵分解。Crossed-Secant加速策略显著提升收敛速度，使迭代方案对参数无约束，首次实现此类接触公式的高效参数无界收敛

Conclusion: 提出的统一迭代框架结合Crossed-Secant加速策略，为无摩擦接触问题提供了高效、参数鲁棒的求解方法，仅需标准刚度系统，具有显著的实际计算优势

Abstract: We propose a unified iterative framework for the solution of frictionless mechanical contact problems, which relies exclusively on the solution of standard stiffness systems. The framework is built upon a two-step fixed-point algorithm: first, the displacement is computed for given contact forces; second, the contact forces are updated based on the displacement solution. The choice of the dual update scheme depends on the numerical contact formulation under consideration. Specifically, the Uzawa iterative scheme is obtained for the Lagrange multiplier formulation, while a penalty-based operator-splitting strategy is proposed for the penalty contact formulation. The main interest of such displacement-force splitting strategy is to involve only standard rigidity matrices in the solving step: no saddle-point or penalized ill-conditionned coefficient matrices have to be handled. Moreover only the right-hand side of the system is updated throughout the iterations, which enables matrix factorization reuse or efficient iterative solvers initialization. The main limitation of such splitting iterative strategies lies in the inherently slow convergence of the underlying fixed-point iterations. Moreover, convergence is guaranteed only within a narrow range of numerical parameter values (i.e., the augmentation or penalty parameter). This work addresses both issues by applying the Crossed-Secant fixed-point acceleration strategy, which substantially improves the convergence rate and renders the iterative schemes effectively parameter-unconstrained. To the best of our knowledge, this contribution provides the first computational demonstration of efficient, parameter-unbounded convergence for such contact formulations. The substantial practical benefits of the proposed approach are illustrated through representative three-dimensional academic and industrial frictionless contact problems.

</details>

### [77] [Moiré in $Γ$-valley square lattice: Copper- and iron-based superconductor simulation in a single device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11153)
*Toshikaze Kariyado,Yusuf Wicaksono,Ashvin Vishwanath,Pavel Volkov,Zhu-Xi Luo*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温超导体（铜基、铁基和镍酸盐）都基于方晶格结构，表现出丰富的强关联现象，但传统莫尔异质结构主要基于六角晶格。研究旨在探索利用方晶格莫尔系统模拟这些高温超导体物理的可能性。

Method: 开发了普适理论框架，结合连续哈密顿量建模、第一性原理计算和哈特里-福克平均场理论，对候选材料ZnF₂进行详细分析，重点关注双轨道模型的四分之一填充区域。

Result: 发现第一莫尔带实现了单轨道方晶格哈伯德模型（模拟铜基超导体），第二和第三莫尔带映射到p_x,p_y双轨道方晶格哈伯德模型（模拟铁基超导体）。在双轨道模型的四分之一填充区域发现了稳定的反铁磁轨道-铁磁绝缘相。

Conclusion: Γ-能谷方晶格莫尔系统是新一代范德华异质结构，能够实现有趣的强关联物相，为模拟和理解高温超导体物理提供了重要平台。

Abstract: Novel superconducting phases have been found in various moiré heterostructures based on hexagonal lattices. However, the archetypal high-temperature superconductors (cuprates, iron-based and nickelate families) all share a square lattice foundation. These materials host a rich landscape of correlated phenomena, such as charge and spin stripes, pseudogap behavior, and unconventional metallicity, which continue to challenge our fundamental understanding of strongly correlated electrons. In this work, we investigate the possibility of simulating the effective models governing these high-$T_c$ superconductors using twisted homobilayers of $Γ$-valley square-lattice systems. We develop a universal theoretical framework and carry out a detailed analysis of a promising candidate material ZnF$_2$. We find that the first moiré band realizes a single-orbital square-lattice Hubbard model, widely believed to capture cuprate physics, while the second and third moiré bands map to a $p_x,p_y$ two-orbital square-lattice Hubbard model, which shares common physics to the minimal $d_{xz}, d_{yz}$ models proposed for iron pnictides. Our study combines continuum Hamiltonian modeling, first-principle calculations, and Hartree-Fock mean field theory. The latter focuses on the quarter-filling regime of the two-orbital model and in particular leads to, among others, a stable antiferro-orbital, ferromagnetic insulating phase. These results highlight $Γ$-valley square-lattice moiré systems as a new and important generation of van der Waals heterostructures to realize interesting strongly correlated phases of matter.

</details>


### [78] [Can electronic quantum criticality drive phonon-induced linear-in-temperature resistivity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11176)
*Haoyu Guo,Debanjan Chowdhury*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究量子临界点附近软化的光学声子能否在低温下产生线性T电阻率，分析了声子软化机制及其对电子输运的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学声子在高温等分区自然产生线性T电阻率，但其有限能隙阻碍了该机制在低温下的持续存在。研究目的是探索接近电子量子临界点能否通过强烈软化光学声子来消除这一障碍。

Method: 首先推导了软化声子控制低温输运的模型无关判据：除了降低重整化光学能隙外，Landau阻尼声子必须获得动力学指数z_p>d。然后分析了一个具体机制，其中声子非线性耦合到量子临界点附近的长波长电子集体模式，并应用于Ising-nematic情况。采用大N场论公式化处理。

Result: 在清洁理论中，声子软化在临界附近增强，但由此产生的动力学处于或接近渐进线性T散射的边际边界。包含软化声子对电子临界扇区的反馈进一步削弱了鲁棒低温线性T输运的趋势。

Conclusion: 研究结果既阐明了基于声子解释电子临界附近奇异金属输运的潜力，也揭示了其局限性。声子软化机制在量子临界点附近确实能增强低温线性电阻率，但存在理论限制。

Abstract: Optical phonons naturally generate linear-in-$T$ resistivity in the high-temperature equipartition regime, but their finite gap prevents this mechanism from surviving to asymptotically low temperatures. Here we analyze whether proximity to an electronic quantum critical point can remove this obstruction by strongly softening an optical phonon. We first derive a model-independent criterion for such softened phonons to control low-temperature transport: besides reducing the renormalized optical gap, the Landau-damped phonon must acquire a dynamical exponent $z_p>d$, where $d$ is the spatial dimension of the phonon, so that a sufficiently large thermally occupied phase space survives as $T\to 0$. We then analyze a concrete mechanism in which the phonon couples nonlinearly to long-wavelength electronic collective modes near a $\vec{Q}=0$ quantum critical point, and apply it to the Ising-nematic case. Within a large-$N$ field theoretic formulation, the phonon softening is enhanced near criticality, but in the clean theory the resulting dynamics lies at or near the marginal boundary for asymptotic $T$-linear scattering. Including feedback from the softened phonon back onto the electronic critical sector further weakens the tendency toward robust low-temperature $T$-linear transport. Our results sharpen both the promise and the limitations of phonon-based explanations of strange-metal transport near electronic criticality.

</details>


### [79] [Crossover to Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev criticality in an infinite-range quantum Heisenberg spin glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11263)
*Hossein Hosseinabadi,Subir Sachdev,Jamir Marino*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究无限范围量子海森堡模型在随机耦合下的平衡动力学，通过大N展开分析量子涨落对自旋玻璃相变温度的影响，揭示了SYK临界性与自旋玻璃有序之间的动力学交叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立理解SYK临界性与自旋玻璃有序之间动力学交叉的最小框架，研究量子涨落如何影响随机耦合量子海森堡模型的相变行为。

Method: 采用大N展开方法，其中N_f表示自旋费米子的味道数，控制量子涨落的强度；自洽地包含Luttinger-Ward泛函的1/N_f修正；分析无限范围随机耦合量子海森堡模型。

Result: 在大N_f极限下，高温顺磁相和低温自旋玻璃相之间存在相变，相变温度与N_f无关；对于小N_f，量子涨落显著抑制有序温度；系统接近SYK相时，费米子和自旋谱密度在宽频率范围内显示临界行为；最低能标下自旋玻璃动力学主导，产生普适的亚欧姆动态自旋磁化率χ''(ω)∼sgn(ω)√|ω|。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解SYK临界性与自旋玻璃有序之间的动力学交叉建立了最小框架，揭示了量子涨落对相变温度的重要影响以及不同能标下的普适动力学行为。

Abstract: We study the equilibrium dynamics of an infinite-range quantum Heisenberg model with random couplings, in which local magnetic moments arise from $\mathcal{N}_f$ flavors of spinful fermions. We employ an expansion in $\mathcal{N}_f$, which controls the strength of quantum fluctuations, and self-consistently include $1/\mathcal{N}_f$ corrections to the Luttinger-Ward functional. In the large-$\mathcal{N}_f$ limit, where quantum fluctuations are weak, the high- and low-temperature phases are respectively paramagnetic and spin glass ordered, with a transition temperature independent of $\mathcal{N}_f$. For small numbers of fermionic flavors, however, quantum fluctuations substantially suppress the ordering temperature. We show that this behavior reflects the proximity of the system to a Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) phase, where both fermionic and spin spectral densities display critical behavior over a broad range of finite frequencies, with the latter exhibiting the scale-invariant form $χ''(ω)\sim \operatorname{sgn}(ω)$. At the lowest energies and temperatures, spin-glass dynamics ultimately take over, producing a universal sub-Ohmic dynamical spin susceptibility $χ''(ω)\sim \operatorname{sgn}(ω)\sqrt{|ω|}$. Our results establish a minimal framework for understanding dynamical crossovers between SYK criticality and spin-glass ordering.

</details>


### [80] [Dimensionality tuning of heavy-fermion states in ultrathin CeSi2 films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11289)
*Yi Wu,Weifan Zhu,Teng Hua,Yuan Fang,Yanan Zhang,Jiawen Zhang,Yanen Huang,Hao Zheng,Shanyin Fu,Xinying Zheng,Zhengtai Liu,Mao Ye,Ye Chen,Tulai Sun,Michael Smidman,Johann Kroha,Chao Cao,Huiqiu Yuan,Frank Steglich,Hai-Qing Lin,Yang Liu*

<details>

Method: 结合分子束外延(MBE)生长不同厚度的CeSi2薄膜，采用原位角分辨光电子能谱(ARPES)和输运测量，系统研究薄膜厚度对电子态的影响。

Result: 三维厚膜中观察到费米能级附近的色散近藤峰和晶体电场激发卫星峰；二维超薄膜中晶体电场激发显著抑制，基态近藤峰保持但出现在更低温度；磁电阻最大温度从厚膜的~100K降至超薄膜的~35K。

Conclusion: 维度驱动的晶体电场激发抑制是近藤过程中电子态变化的主要原因，这为理解强关联4f电子系统的量子限制效应提供了直接见解，并为探索二维重费米子材料中的新现象开辟了机会。

Abstract: Dimensionality tuning is an important method to modify the electronic states of quantum materials. However, the mechanism of such tuning in heavy fermion systems and its connection with transport properties remain largely unexplored. Here by combining molecular beam epitaxy (MBE), in-situ angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) and transport measurements, we study the electronic states of the heavy-fermion compound CeSi2 as a function of film thickness. In three dimensional thick films, our measurements reveal a dispersive Kondo peak at the Fermi level (EF) and satellite peaks originating from crystal electric field (CEF) excitations, characteristic of heavy fermion systems. For two-dimensional ultrathin films, the CEF satellites are largely suppressed while the ground-state Kondo peak at EF remains strong, although it develops at lower temperatures. Simultaneously, the maximum temperature Tmax of the magnetic resistivity, \r{ho}m(T), changes from ~100 K in thick films to ~35 K in ultrathin films. This can be attributed to the dimensionality driven reduction of CEF excitations during the Kondo process, in good agreement with spectroscopic results. Our work provides direct insight to understand the quantum confinement effects on strongly correlated 4f-electron systems and opens up new opportunities to explore emergent phenomena in two-dimensional heavy-fermion materials.

</details>


### [81] [Enhanced carrier binding and bond correlations in the Hubbard-Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model with dispersive optical phonons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11373)
*Debshikha Banerjee,Alberto Nocera,Steven Johnston*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过密度矩阵重整化群方法，分析了色散光学声子对轻掺杂一维光学Hubbard-SSH模型中电子配对的影响，发现声子色散显著增强了单重态结合，但并未转化为增强的超导关联，而是形成了稳健的键关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSH模型中原子运动调制电子跃迁，对高温超导有重要意义。先前研究主要关注稀掺杂或半填充模型中的无色散（爱因斯坦）声子，而实际材料中的声子具有色散特性，因此需要研究色散声子对电子配对的影响。

Method: 使用密度矩阵重整化群方法研究轻掺杂一维光学Hubbard-SSH模型，计算各种关联函数来分析色散光学声子的影响。

Result: 发现声子色散显著增强了单重态结合，但增强的结合并未转化为增强的超导关联，而是形成了稳健的键关联。这表明在研究的参数范围内，声子色散虽然影响配对强度，但不促进超导相的形成。

Conclusion: 声子色散对电子关联有显著影响，强调了在模拟实际量子材料时需要超越爱因斯坦声子极限的重要性。虽然声子色散增强了电子配对，但这种增强并未导致超导关联的增强，而是表现为键关联的增强。

Abstract: Electron-phonon (e-ph) interactions play a crucial role in determining many properties of materials. In this context, the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) model, where atomic motion modulates the electronic hopping, has gained significant attention due to its potential for strong electron pairing in relation to high-Tc superconductivity. Previous studies of the SSH models have addressed many aspects of this problem, but have focused heavily on either dilute or half-filled models with dispersionless (Einstein) phonons. Here, we study the effects of dispersive optical phonons on the lightly doped one-dimensional optical Hubbard-SSH model using the density matrix renormalization group. We observe a significant enhancement in singlet binding driven by phonon dispersion; however, by calculating various correlation functions, we find that the enhanced binding does not translate to increased superconducting correlations but rather robust bond correlations in the studied parameter regime. Nevertheless, the significant impact of phonon dispersion on these correlations highlights the need to go beyond the Einstein phonon limit while modeling realistic quantum materials.

</details>

Method: 采用温度依赖角分辨光电子能谱技术，追踪1T-TaS2在近公度-非公度电荷密度波相变过程中的电子结构演化，重点关注布里渊区中心附近的准粒子谱重变化和费米面重构。

Result: 在布里渊区中心观察到准粒子谱重抑制，与输运异常同时发生，但未发现完整带隙打开的证据。相变涉及动量依赖的谱重再分布，表现为相干性丧失导致费米面重构，而导带色散基本保持不变。

Conclusion: 近公度-非公度相变不符合传统金属-绝缘体转变图像，而是由相干性丧失驱动的电子重构过程。该研究为室温附近的电阻率异常提供了微观解释，并为过渡金属二硫化物中集体电子开关的设计提供了指导原则。

Abstract: Transition metal dichalcogenides host a variety of charge density wave phases that couple lattice, charge, and correlation effects. In 1T-TaS2, the commensurate and nearly commensurate states are well characterized, yet the transition near 350 K into the incommensurate phase has lacked direct momentum resolved insight. Here we use temperature dependent angle resolved photoemission spectroscopy to track the electronic structure across this transition. We observe a suppression of quasiparticle spectral weight at the Brillouin zone center, coincident with the transport anomaly, but without clear evidence of a full band gap opening. The transition appears to involve momentum dependent redistribution of spectral weight, consistent with a loss of coherence that reshapes the Fermi surface while leaving conduction dispersions largely intact. These results suggest that the nearly commensurate incommensurate transition may not align with a conventional metal insulator transition picture, but rather as an electronic reconstruction driven by loss of coherence. Our work provides new microscopic insight into the resistivity anomaly near room temperature and may guide design principles for collective electronic switching in Transition metal dichalcogenides.

</details>


### [83] [Bootstrap Embedding for Interacting Electrons in Phonon Coherent-state Mean Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11463)

Method: 结合自洽嵌入方法处理相关电子，采用相干态平均场处理声子，将电子-声子相互作用问题建模为电子在自洽确定的势场中运动的系统。

Result: 在一维Hubbard-Holstein模型中验证了方法对片段尺寸和总系统尺寸的收敛性（可达350个格点），通过有限尺寸标度外推到无限系统尺寸。与密度矩阵重整化群在8格点系统的比较显示计算速度有数量级优势。

Conclusion: 该方法在局域化主导的区域（如Mott绝缘相和强耦合小极化子区域）表现最佳，但在弱耦合离域区域和Peierls相变点存在局限性，主要源于声子的平均场处理和量子涨落效应。

Abstract: We develop a fermi-bose bootstrap embedding (fb-BE) framework for the ground state of interacting elec- trons coupled to phonon mean field. The method combines bootstrap embedding for correlated electrons with a self-consistent coherent-state mean-field treatment for phonons. This method models the interacting electron-phonon problem as a system of correlated electrons traveling in a self-consistently specified potential landscape, allowing for efficient treatment of large lattice systems. Convergence of the methods for frag- ment size and total system size are demonstrated for one-dimensional Hubbard-Holstein model for up to 350 sites. Finite-size scaling is performed to extrapolate to infinite system size. Benchmarking against density matrix renormalization group for small 8-site system at half- and quarter-filling shows orders-of-magnitude runtime advantage. The comparison further reveals that the method performs best in regimes dominated by localization, such as the Mott insulating phase and the strong-coupling tiny polaron regime, where the local embedding ansatz is still valid. However, due to the mean-field treatment for phonons, we find limitations of our methods in the weakly coupled delocalized region and at the Peierls transition, where quantum phonon fluctuations and long-range kinetic correlations become substantial.

*A. A. Katanin,A. N. Rudenko,D. I. Badrtdinov,M. I. Katsnelson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 采用DFT+DMFT方法研究单层Fe₃GeTe₂的电子和磁性性质，发现该材料远离局域磁矩极限，具有非线性温度依赖的磁化率，不同位置的铁原子磁矩分布不均，RKKY交换相互作用对稳定铁磁序至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单层Fe₃GeTe₂的电子和磁性性质，特别是其在顺磁相中的行为，以理解其部分巡游磁性的本质，并解释实验观测到的磁性质。

Method: 采用DFT+DMFT（密度泛函理论+动力学平均场理论）方法，在顺磁相中研究单层Fe₃GeTe₂。考虑了中等库仑相互作用（U=3-4 eV），分析不同对称性不等价铁原子的磁矩分布和交换相互作用。

Result: 1. Fe₃GeTe₂远离局域磁矩极限，在宽温度范围内表现出非线性温度依赖的局域和均匀磁化率；2. 位于Ge平面上方和下方的铁原子具有显著局域磁矩（μ≳4.5 μ_B），而位于Ge平面内的铁原子无明显磁矩；3. 这两类原子间的RKKY型交换相互作用对稳定长程铁磁序至关重要；4. 计算得到的自旋波刚度和居里温度与实验数据吻合良好。

Conclusion: Fe₃GeTe₂表现出部分巡游磁性行为，电子关联效应的动力学处理对其正确描述至关重要。不同位置铁原子的磁矩差异和RKKY交换相互作用共同决定了材料的铁磁性质，计算结果与实验观测一致。

Abstract: We study the electronic and magnetic properties of monolayer Fe$_3$GeTe$_2$ within the DFT+DMFT approach in the paramagnetic phase. We argue that this compound is sufficiently far from the local magnetic moment limit, demonstrating non-linear temperature dependencies of the partial inverse local and uniform magnetic susceptibilities in a broad temperature range. We find that in the regime of moderate Coulomb interactions ($U=3-4$ eV), the iron atoms located above and below the Ge plane carry a substantial local magnetic moment ($μ\gtrsim 4.5 μ_B$), while the iron atom located within the Ge plane does not exhibit any pronounced magnetic moment. At the same time, the RKKY-type exchange interactions between these two symmetry-nonequivalent types of atoms turn out to be crucial for stabilizing long-range ferromagnetic order in Fe$_3$GeTe$_2$. The estimated spin-wave stiffness and Curie temperature are in good agreement with the experimental data, indicating that a dynamical treatment of electron correlations in Fe$_3$GeTe$_2$ is essential to properly describe its partially itinerant magnetic behavior.

</details>


### [85] [Foliated-Exotic Duality and Anomaly Inflow in Fracton Quantum Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11681)
*Shutaro Shimamura*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文扩展了分形子量子场论中的叶状-奇异对偶性，结合子系统对称性的't Hooft异常的反常流入机制，在2+1维的奇异和叶状BF理论以及奇异φ理论中建立了场对应关系，并构造了相应的子系统对称性保护拓扑相描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分形子相是具有子系统全局对称性的新型物质相，其连续低能有效描述有两种形式：奇异张量规范场的奇异量子场论和基于叶状结构的叶状量子场论。某些分形子QFT中这两种描述等价（叶状-奇异对偶性）。本文旨在通过结合子系统对称性的't Hooft异常的反常流入机制来扩展这一对偶性。

Method: 1. 研究2+1维奇异和叶状BF理论中的ℤ_N × ℤ_N子系统对称性混合't Hooft异常，通过反常流入机制用高一维的子系统对称性保护拓扑相捕获该异常；2. 研究2+1维奇异φ理论中的U(1)×U(1)子系统对称性't Hooft异常，通过反常流入机制用3+1维SSPT相捕获该异常；3. 将叶状-奇异对偶性扩展到这些理论中，建立场对应关系并构造相应的叶状描述。

Result: 1. 在BF理论中建立了SSPT相的场对应关系，并构造了SSPT相的叶状描述；2. 在φ理论中建立了场对应关系，并构造了与奇异φ理论等价的叶状φ理论，这是叶状-奇异对偶性在无能隙理论中的首个实例。

Conclusion: 通过结合反常流入机制，成功将叶状-奇异对偶性扩展到包含子系统对称性't Hooft异常的分形子理论中，为奇异和叶状描述之间的等价性提供了更全面的理解，特别是在无能隙理论中首次实现了这种对偶性。

Abstract: Fracton phases are new types of phases of matter characterized by subsystem global symmetry, which is a generalized global symmetry whose symmetry operator is partially topological. Their continuum low-energy effective descriptions admit two different formulations: an exotic quantum field theory (QFT) using exotic tensor gauge fields, and a foliated QFT constructed from a foliation structure and foliated gauge fields. For certain fracton QFTs, these two descriptions are equivalent, which is called the foliated-exotic duality. In this dissertation, we extend the foliated-exotic duality by combining it with the anomaly inflow mechanism for 't Hooft anomalies of subsystem symmetries. This dissertation has two main results. First, we discuss the exotic and foliated $BF$ theories in 2+1 dimensions, which exhibit the mixed 't Hooft anomaly of $\mathbb{Z}_N \times \mathbb{Z}_N$ subsystem symmetry. This anomaly is captured by a subsystem symmetry-protected topological (SSPT) phase for $\mathbb{Z}_N \times \mathbb{Z}_N$ subsystem symmetry in one dimension higher. By extending the foliated-exotic duality in the fractonic $BF$ theory to the SSPT phase, we establish the field correspondences in the SSPT phase and construct the foliated description of the SSPT phase. Second, we discuss the exotic $φ$-theory in 2+1 dimensions -- a fractonic gapless scalar field theory, which has the 't Hooft anomaly of $U(1) \times U(1)$ subsystem symmetry. The anomaly is captured by an SSPT phase for $U(1) \times U(1)$ subsystem symmetry in 3+1 dimensions via the anomaly inflow mechanism. Extending the foliated-exotic duality to the $φ$-theory, we establish field correspondences in the $φ$-theory and construct the foliated $φ$-theory that is equivalent to the exotic $φ$-theory. This provides the first example of the foliated-exotic duality in gapless theories.

</details>


### [86] [A Single-Particle Diagnosis of an Interacting Topological Insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11879)
*Theo N. Dionne,Maia G. Vergniory*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出基于单粒子格林函数构建有效单粒子描述来表征相互作用拓扑相的方法，通过SSH模型与Hatsugai-Kohmoto相互作用示例，利用单粒子约化密度矩阵定义有效绕数和量子体积，可从单粒子可观测量区分包括Mott态在内的三种绝缘相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强关联系统中拓扑性质如何保持是一个核心挑战，因为大多数拓扑诊断方法依赖于非相互作用的能带结构。需要开发能够表征相互作用拓扑相的有效框架。

Method: 从单粒子格林函数构建单粒子约化密度矩阵，利用该矩阵定义有效绕数和量子体积（量子态几何的度量）。以Su-Schrieffer-Heeger模型与Hatsugai-Kohmoto相互作用作为解析可处理的示例，通过单粒子可观测量来诊断拓扑相。

Result: 该方法能够区分三种绝缘相，包括关联Mott态。结果表明相互作用拓扑可以通过单粒子激发的谱权重分布来解释，为诊断关联系统中的拓扑相提供了直观且计算上可实现的途径。

Conclusion: 提出的框架与现有多体模拟技术兼容，为识别相互作用拓扑材料开辟了新途径。相互作用拓扑可以在有效单粒子描述下通过谱权重分布来理解，这提供了诊断关联系统拓扑相的可计算方法。

Abstract: Understanding how topology survives in strongly correlated systems remains a central challenge, as most topological diagnostics rely on non-interacting band structures. Here we present a framework to characterize interacting topological phases within an effective single-particle description derived from the single-particle Green's function. Using the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model with Hatsugai-Kohmoto interactions as an analytically tractable example, we construct the one-body reduced density matrix from the Green's function and use it to define an effective winding number together with quantum volume, a measurement of state geometry. These quantities allow us to distinguish three insulating phases including correlated Mott states directly from single-particle observables. Our results show that interacting topology can be interpreted in terms of the spectral weight distribution of single-particle excitations, providing an intuitive and computationally accessible route to diagnose topological phases in correlated systems. This approach is compatible with modern many-body simulation techniques and opens a pathway toward the identification of interacting topological materials.

</details>


### [87] [Engineering altermagnetic orders on the square-kagome lattice through sublattice interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12022)
*Jonas Issing,Jannis Seufert,Michael Klett,Sarbajit Mazumdar,Yasir Iqbal,Ronny Thomale,Atanu Maity*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在方晶格-笼目晶格上研究交变磁性相的出现，发现子晶格干涉效应使不同子晶格上的磁有序倾向解耦，从而产生两种不同类型的交变磁性相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究方晶格-笼目晶格上交变磁性相的出现机制，探索子晶格选择性磁不稳定性如何调控交变磁性序的性质。

Method: 使用Kotliar-Ruckenstein slave boson形式体系，分析子晶格权重分解导致的矩阵元效应（子晶格干涉），研究不同子晶格磁不稳定性产生的交变磁性相。

Result: 识别出两种交变磁性相：d_{xy}型和d_{x^2-y^2}型，分别源于不同的子晶格极化模式。通过相互作用强度函数探索了这些交变磁性相的稳定性。

Conclusion: 子晶格选择性磁不稳定性为调控交变磁性序的性质提供了一种通用途径，展示了子晶格干涉在交变磁性相形成中的关键作用。

Abstract: We investigate the emergence of altermagnetic (AM) phases on the square-kagome lattice. Our analysis reveals that matrix element effects due to an orthogonal sublattice weight decomposition of Fermi level eigenstates known as sublattice interference enable decoupled magnetic ordering tendencies on distinct sublattices. Depending on which sublattice undergoes a magnetic instability, we identify a $d_{xy}$-type AM phase and a $d_{x^{2}-y^{2}}$-type AM phase originating from different sublattice polarization patterns. Using the Kotliar-Ruckenstein slave boson formalism we explore the stability of these AM phases as a function of interaction strength. Our findings demonstrate that sublattice-selective magnetic instabilities provide a versatile route to engineer the nature of AM order.

</details>


### [88] [Directional Manipulation of a Staggered Charge Density Wave and Kondo Resonance in UTe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12097)
*Nileema Sharma,Fangjun Cheng,Hyeok Jun Yang,Matthew Toole,James McKenzie,Mitchell M. Bordelon,Sean M. Thomas,Priscila F. S. Rosa,Yi-Ting Hsu,Xiaolong Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: UTe2中发现新的交错电荷密度波(CDW)，其对外加磁场表现出强烈各向异性响应，与Kondo效应存在紧密关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UTe2是一种罕见的量子材料，其中非常规密度波序、Kondo物理、自旋三重态配对和再入超导性共存于同一电子系统中。理解其超导态如何从强关联正常相中产生，需要识别和控制竞争或交织的正常态序。

Method: 使用扫描隧道显微镜(STM)在矢量磁场中研究高质量UTe2晶体，通过磁场方向调控来观察交错CDW的响应行为。

Result: 发现之前未报道的交错电荷密度波(CDW)，其对外加磁场表现出强烈各向异性：沿准一维铀链方向(a轴)的1.7 T磁场完全淬灭CDW，而沿其他晶向的磁场则无此效应。同时，相同磁场方向和强度改变杂化能隙并抑制5f Kondo共振。

Conclusion: 交错CDW与Kondo效应之间存在紧密关联，磁场调控导致主导杂化通道从Te 5p-U 5f转变为U 6d-U 5f耦合。该工作为UTe2中交织序提供了有效调控手段，并揭示了轨道选择性Kondo杂化的证据。

Abstract: UTe2 is a rare example of a correlated quantum material in which unconventional density wave orders, Kondo physics, spin-triplet pairing, and reentrant superconductivity coexist within the same electronic system. Its superconducting state develops out of a strongly correlated normal phase. The identification and control of competing or intertwined normal-state orders are thus central to elucidating the electronic landscape from which its superconductivity arises. Here, using scanning tunnelling microscopy (STM) in a vector magnetic field, we uncover a previously unreported staggered charge-density-wave (CDW) in high-quality UTe2 crystals and demonstrate its strong directional response to an external magnetic field: the staggered CDW is completely quenched by a modest 1.7 T field aligned with the quasi-one-dimensional uranium chain direction (a-axis), while remaining robust against fields along other crystallographic directions. This pronounced anisotropy is consistent with an orbital-driven mechanism that leads to a field-tuned quantum phase transition. Strikingly and counterintuitively, the same field orientation and strength concomitantly alter the hybridization gap and suppress the 5f Kondo resonance. Modelling indicates that this correlated evolution arises from a switch of the dominant hybridization channel from Te 5p- U 5f to U 6d- U 5f coupling, suggesting an intimate interplay between CDW and the Kondo effect. Our work establishes an effective tuning knob for the intertwined orders in UTe2 and provides evidence for orbital-selective Kondo hybridization, shedding light on its correlated normal state.

</details>

*Matt Boswell,Antonio M. dos Santos,Mingyu Xu,Madalynn Marshall,Su-Yang Xu,Weiwei Xie*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究压力诱导的电子离域转变附近出现的奇异量子现象，特别是层状锰基磷化物CaMn₂Sb₂中结构、电荷和磁性的耦合机制。

Method: 采用单晶X射线衍射分析结构相变，残余电子密度分析探测电荷局域化，键合分析研究轨道重排，中子散射确定磁结构演化。

Result: 在5.4 GPa附近观察到从三角晶系P-3m1到单斜晶系P2₁/m的一级相变，伴随约7%体积塌缩；中间压力下发现沿Mn-Sb链的电荷局域化；压力诱导形成非共线磁序，单斜相中锯齿形Mn链在ac平面内呈现反铁磁耦合。

Conclusion: CaMn₂Sb₂是研究压力下层状锰基磷化物中结构畸变、电荷重分布和磁序耦合的理想模型体系，揭示了轨道重排驱动的几何畸变在电子离域转变中的关键作用。

Abstract: Exotic quantum phenomena often emerge near an electronic delocalization transition (EDT) from an antiferromagnetic insulating phase to a strongly correlated metallic state under pressure. We report the pressure-induced structural and magnetic evolution of the antiferromagnetic insulator CaMn$_2$Sb$_2$. Single-crystal X-ray diffraction reveals a first-order phase transition near 5.4 GPa from a trigonal P-3m1 structure to a monoclinic P2$_1$/m phase, accompanied by a ~7% volume collapse. Residual electron density analysis at intermediate pressures reveals charge localization along Mn-Sb chains, signaling electronic instability preceding the structural transition. Bonding analysis indicates anisotropic Mn-Sb orbital reconfiguration under pressure, driving a distorted square-pyramidal geometry. Neutron scattering confirms the transition and identifies a pressure-induced incommensurate magnetic order, distinct from the ambient antiferromagnetic state. In the monoclinic phase, zigzag Mn chains exhibit antiferromagnetic coupling along the ac-plane, enabled by enhanced orbital overlap. These results establish CaMn$_2$Sb$_2$ as a model system for studying the coupling of structural distortion, charge redistribution, and magnetic order in layered Mn pnictides under pressure.

</details>


### [90] [Kinetic obstruction to pairing in the doped Kitaev-Heisenberg ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12198)
*Bradraj Pandey,Bo Xiao,Satoshi Okamoto,Gonzalo Alvarez,Gábor B. Halász,Elbio Dagotto,Pontus Laurell*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究掺杂Kitaev自旋液体的物理性质，特别是理解掺杂空穴的动能如何影响配对行为和超导相关性，探索不同相互作用参数下的相变行为。

Method: 使用密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）方法研究空穴掺杂的Kitaev-Heisenberg模型在两腿梯子几何结构中的行为，分析不同掺杂水平和跳跃强度下的配对倾向、电荷密度分布和相关函数。

Result: 发现配对倾向仅在t/K≲0.65时出现，与三腿梯子结果一致；揭示了plaquette算符空间分布与电荷密度的紧密联系；构建了掺杂依赖相图，显示rung-singlet区域在掺杂后发展出主导的超导相关性，弱掺杂时电荷密度波相关性占主导，AFK和铁磁Kitaev极限以及stripy相中表现出自旋密度波行为。

Abstract: We investigate the hole-doped Kitaev-Heisenberg ($t$-$J$-$K$) model on a two-leg ladder geometry using the density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG). We first consider the behavior of the antiferromagnetic Kitaev (AFK) spin-liquid phase as a function of hopping strength $t$ and doping level. This reveals intriguing pairing tendencies only for $\frac{t}{K} \lesssim 0.65$, consistent with prior results on three-leg ladders, and firmly supports the emerging picture that the physics of doped Kitaev spin liquids strongly depends on the kinetic energy of the doped holes. Analysis of one- and two-hole doping uncovers close links between the spatial profiles of the plaquette operator and the charge density. We construct a doping-dependent phase diagram for antiferromagnetic Heisenberg interactions and intermediate hopping $t=1$. Upon doping, the rung-singlet region develops dominant superconducting correlations. Charge-density-wave correlations dominate at weak doping near the transition to the stripy phase. Spin-density wave-like behavior is found in the AFK and ferromagnetic Kitaev limits, and in the stripy phase.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究截断Brown-Peterson谱的拓扑Hochschild同调性质，特别是它们在p=2时的代数结构特征，以及它们是否能够表示为Thom谱的问题

Method: 使用新的计算工具——Brun谱序列的变体来计算截断Brown-Peterson谱的拓扑Hochschild同调；在p=2时计算二阶截断Brown-Peterson谱的拓扑Hochschild同调，并在条件允许下推广到任意素数

Result: 成功计算了p=2时二阶截断Brown-Peterson谱的拓扑Hochschild同调；证明了在p=2时，对于任意n≥2，截断Brown-Peterson谱的𝔼₃-MU-代数形式都不是Thom谱BP⟨n⟩

Conclusion: 该研究提供了计算截断Brown-Peterson谱拓扑Hochschild同调的新方法，并揭示了这些谱在p=2时的特殊代数性质，特别是它们不能表示为Thom谱的重要结果

Abstract: We compute topological Hochschild homology of $\mathbb{E}_3$-MU-algebra forms of the second truncated Brown-Peterson spectrum with Adams summand coefficients at $p=2$ and conditionally at arbitrary primes. We also provide a new computational tool, a variant of the Brun spectral sequence, for computing topological Hochschild homology of truncated Brown-Peterson spectra with certain coefficients. As a consequence, we show that $\mathbb{E}_3$-MU-algebra forms of truncated Brown-Peterson spectra are not Thom spectra $\mathrm{BP}\langle n\rangle$ at the prime $p=2$ for any $n\ge 2$.

</details>


### [92] [Homological algebra over non-unital rings and algebras, with applications to $(\infty, 1)$-categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11937)
*Eric Goubault,Eliot Médioni*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该文在非幺环和代数上的模中发展同调代数，主要应用是定义和研究(∞,1)-范畴及有向空间的（有向）同调，包括相对同调及其正合序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统同调代数主要基于幺环上的模理论，但许多数学结构（如某些范畴论构造和几何对象）自然对应非幺代数。为研究(∞,1)-范畴和有向空间的同调理论，需要建立非幺环上模的同调代数框架。

Method: 在非幺环和代数上的模范畴中发展同调代数理论，包括链复形、同调群等基本概念。特别地，定义(∞,1)-范畴和有向空间的有向同调，并建立相对同调理论及其长正合序列。

Result: 成功构建了非幺环上模的同调代数理论，定义了(∞,1)-范畴和有向空间的有向同调群，建立了相对同调理论并证明了相应的长正合序列存在性，为这些结构的同调研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We report the first measurement of the underground cosmic muon flux at the Stawell Underground Physics Laboratory. Abstract: Some gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) exhibit precursor emission episodes preceding the main emission, with a quiescent period in between. The properties of the precursor emission and the duration of the quiescent interval are related to the central engine activity and jet formation processes, thus providing insights into the physics of GRBs.
  We present a comprehensive analysis of the prompt emission and multi-wavelength afterglow of GRB 250916A. Using detailed afterglow modeling, we find that the broadband data are best described by a powerlaw structured jet with a relatively narrow core ($θ_c \approx 0.8^\circ$), viewed moderately off-axis at a viewing angle $θ_v \approx 2.7^\circ$. The isotropic-equivalent kinetic energy of the jet ($E_{k,iso} \approx 2.4 \times 10^{54}$ erg) is on the higher side for typical GRBs. The precursor emission is well described by a blackbody spectrum with a temperature of kT $\approx$ 13.2 keV and is separated from the main emission by a long quiescent interval of 150 s.
  Put together, our results indicate that the precursor is likely to be a shock breakout from a cocoon formed by the interaction of the relativistic jet with the progenitor star. The resulting cocoon pressure and shock collimation naturally lead to the launch of a narrowly collimated jet, consistent with the jet geometry inferred from afterglow observations. The long quiescent interval may imply the central engine turn-off in addition to the effect of the off-axis geometry.

</details>


### [96] [Neutrino Spectral Pinching in 3D Core-Collapse Supernovae: Late-Time Convergence, Failed-Explosion Signatures, and Viewing-Angle Dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11272)

TL;DR: 对25个3D核心坍缩超新星模拟中微子谱夹紧参数α_p的系统调查，发现3D PNS对流导致ν̄_e夹紧下限比1D预测低0.2-0.4，黑洞形成模型在坍缩前显示反夹紧，晚期出现能谱反转，为3D中微子谱收敛提供了首个特征描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究3D核心坍缩超新星模拟中的中微子谱夹紧参数α_p，旨在理解3D效应（特别是PNS对流）如何影响中微子能谱形状，并与传统1D预测进行比较，为观测提供理论依据。

Method: 使用Princeton Fornax集合的25个3D核心坍缩超新星模拟，涵盖8.1-100 M_sun的前身星质量，模拟持续时间达8.47秒反弹后。采用Fornax代码和SFHo状态方程，通过128x256天空网格上的12-bin谱矩计算夹紧参数α_p = (2^2 - E_rms^2)/(E_rms^2 - ^2)，对三种中微子物种进行时间和角度分辨的谱表征。

Result: 1) ν̄_e夹紧下限为α_p = 1.92 ± 0.10，比1D预测低0.2-0.4，归因于3D PNS对流；2) 黑洞形成模型在坍缩前显示反夹紧(α_p < 0.9)，从t=0.5秒可见Δα_p ~ 0.65的缺失；3) 6个长期运行模型中有2个在t>5秒后出现能谱反转，轻子味携带(40±3)%的辐射能量；4) LESA偶极子在黑洞形成模型中被抑制>3倍，视角散布Δα_p(68%) ~ 0.8-1.5主导谱反演不确定性。

Conclusion: 晚期ν̄_e夹紧下限代表了开尔文-亥姆霍兹冷却期间谱收敛的首个3D特征描述，比1D预测低0.2-0.4，这是3D PNS对流输运的直接特征。在Hyper-Kamiokande、DUNE、JUNO和IceCube的探测率显示，在开尔文-亥姆霍兹冷却期间可实现8-12%的中微子质量顺序区分。

Abstract: We present a systematic survey of the neutrino spectral pinching parameter alpha_p(t, M, n-hat) across the Princeton Fornax ensemble of 3D core-collapse supernova simulations. We analyze 25 simulations spanning progenitor masses 8.1-100 M_sun with
  durations up to 8.47 s post-bounce, computed with the Fornax code and the SFHo equation of state. The pinching parameter alpha_p = (2^2 - E_rms^2)/(E_rms^2 - ^2) is derived from 12-bin spectral moments on a 128x256 sky grid for three neutrino
  species, enabling time- and angle-resolved spectral characterization.
  Four results emerge. (1) The nu-bar_e pinching floor is alpha_p = 1.92 +/- 0.10 (N=13 long-running models), lying 0.2-0.4 below 1D predictions due to 3D PNS convection. (2) Both BH-forming models (12.25, 14 M_sun) show anti-pinching (alpha_p <
  0.9) before collapse, with deficit Delta alpha_p ~ 0.65 visible from t = 0.5 s. (3) Two of six long-running models exhibit a hierarchy reversal ( > ) after t = 5 s; leptonic flavors carry (40 +/- 3)% of radiated energy. (4) The LESA dipole is
  suppressed by >3x in BH-forming models; viewing-angle spread Delta alpha_p(68%) ~ 0.8-1.5 dominates spectral-inversion uncertainty. Mollweide sky maps reveal coherent angular structures with alpha_p anticorrelated with luminosity and correlated
  with mean energy.
  Detection rates at Hyper-Kamiokande, DUNE, JUNO, and IceCube yield 8-12% NMO/IMO discrimination during Kelvin-Helmholtz cooling. The late-time nu-bar_e pinching floor represents the first 3D characterization of spectral convergence during
  Kelvin-Helmholtz cooling, lying 0.2-0.4 below 1D predictions, a direct signature of 3D PNS convective transport

</details>

Cox,Martin Elvis,Roberto Gilli,Fiona A. Harrison,Ross Silver,Daniel Stern,Nuria Torres-Albà,Qian Yang,Lizhong Zhang*

TL;DR: 高红移AGN的X射线谱分析显示，其日冕截止能量和温度显著低于本地AGN，而光学深度更高，表明高光度AGN中日冕存在更强的康普顿冷却或非热电子成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地活动星系核（AGN）的高能截止通常超出NuSTAR探测范围，难以直接约束日冕性质。高红移、高光度类星体使系统约束高能截止成为可能，因为宇宙学红移将光谱截止移至可观测的硬X射线波段。

Method: 采用PACHA项目，对13个z>1的射电宁静AGN进行NuSTAR和XMM-Newton准同时观测，在90%置信水平下约束高能截止和日冕温度，并将高红移样本与本地AGN结合分析。

Result: 样本平均截止能量E_cut=80.8±8.1 keV，平均日冕温度kT_e=18.4±1.6 keV，均显著低于本地Swift-BAT AGN；平均光学深度τ=4.8±0.3则显著更高。发现截止能量与X射线光度、黑洞质量存在潜在反相关，但与爱丁顿比无显著依赖。

Conclusion: 在混合日冕框架下，推断的温度远低于纯热日冕的电子对产生极限，表明存在显著的康普顿冷却效率和/或非热电子成分。高光度AGN中低日冕温度的探测与近期辐射MHD模拟预测一致，暗示非热电子可能不是这些系统中观测到的日冕性质的主要驱动因素。

Abstract: The X-ray emission of active galactic nuclei (AGN) is generally attributed to inverse Compton scattering of accretion-disk photons by hot electrons in a compact corona. In local AGN, directly constraining coronal properties is challenging because the high-energy cutoff often lies beyond the NuSTAR bandpass. High-redshift, luminous quasars enable systematic constraints on the high-energy cutoff, as cosmological redshift shifts the spectal cutoff into the observable hard X-ray band. We present first results from the ``Probing the AGN Coronae with High-redshift AGN'' (PACHA) project, based on quasi-simultaneous NuSTAR and XMM-Newton observations of 13 radio-quiet AGN at $z>1$. We constrain the high-energy cutoff and coronal temperature at 90\% confidence level for 10 and 9 sources, respectively. The sample exhibits a mean cutoff energy of $E_{\rm cut}=80.8\pm8.1$ keV and a mean coronal temperature of $kT_{\rm e}=18.4\pm1.6$ keV, both significantly lower than those measured in local {\it Swift}-BAT AGN, while the mean optical depth ($τ=4.8\pm0.3$) is significantly higher. The uncertainties are at 1~$σ$. Combining our high-redshift sample with local AGN, we find a potential anti-correlation between cutoff energy and both X-ray luminosity and black hole mass, with no significant dependence on Eddington ratio. Within a hybrid coronal framework, the inferred temperatures lie well below the pair-production limits for purely thermal coronae, indicating a substantial efficient Compton cooling and/or non-thermal electron component. The detection of low coronal temperatures in high-luminosity AGN is broadly consistent with predictions from recent radiation MHD simulations that consider purely thermal electron populations, implying that non-thermal electrons may not be the primary drivers of the observed coronal properties in these systems.

</details>


### [98] [Imploding Remnants: detection bias against AGNs in massive clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11361)
*Ross J. Turner,Georgia S. C. Stewart*

TL;DR: 论文提出，观测到的致密星系团中缺乏遗留的瓣状AGN是由于喷流停止活动后瓣的快速"内爆"造成的，这导致大质量星系团中的遗留AGN被低估至少5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为什么在致密星系团中观测到的遗留瓣状活动星系核（AGN）数量稀少，这可能是由于喷流停止活动后瓣的动力学特性导致的快速内爆现象。

Method: 通过分析RAiSE动力学模型的渐近行为，并将预测结果与完整模型和流体动力学模拟进行比较，研究弱喷流驱动的遗留瓣在大质量星系团中的稳定性。

Result: 发现大质量星系团（M_halo ∼ 10^14.5 M_⊙）中由弱喷流驱动的遗留瓣在最多几百万年内就会内爆不稳定，导致这些遗留AGN的数量比贫乏星系团（M_halo ∼ 10^12 M_⊙）至少低估5倍。

Conclusion: 遗留AGN的缺乏可能导致对低功率喷流提供的AGN反馈显著低估，特别是在反馈最有效的星系团核心区域。稳定磁场鞘可能影响内爆的性质，决定瓣是干净内爆还是与周围介质湍流混合。

Abstract: We propose that an observed scarcity of remnant lobed AGNs in dense clusters results from a peculiarity in their dynamics upon the cessation of jet activity: a rapid `implosion' of lobes that, in their active phase, were primarily supported by the momentum flux of the jet. We investigate this behaviour by analysing the asymptotic behaviour of the RAiSE dynamical model and comparing our predictions both to the full model and hydrodynamic simulations. We find that remnant lobes powered by weak jets in massive clusters are unstable to implosion on the order of at most a few Myr. Consequently, remnant AGNs in massive clusters ($M_\text{halo} \sim 10^{14.5}$~M$_\odot$) will be under-counted by a factor of at least five compared to those in poorer groups ($M_\text{halo} \sim 10^{12}$~M$_\odot$). The lack of such remnants in observed populations may lead to a significant underestimate of the AGN feedback provided by low-powered jets, especially given their prevalence towards cluster cores where feedback is most effective. We discuss the influence of a stabilising magnetic field sheath on the nature of the implosion: does the lobe cleanly implode in on itself, or do fluid instabilities turbulently mix the lobe and ambient medium?

</details>


### [99] [Marginally stable nuclear burning triggered at different depths of the neutron star surface in Low-mass X-ray binary 4U 1608-52](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11424)
*Lyu Ming,Zhang Guobao,Mendez Mariano,Xiao Huaping*

Conclusion: 研究支持边缘稳定核燃烧随温度降低在更深层点燃并释放较少能量的物理图景，为理解中子星表面核燃烧过程与观测特征的关系提供了重要观测证据，验证了相关理论预测。

Abstract: We investigated the timing and spectral properties of the millihertz quasi-periodic oscillations (mHz QPOs) in the neutron-star low-mass X-ray binary 4U 1608-52 using NICER observations. Our analysis reveals a correlation between the QPO frequency and its absolute amplitude, as well as between the frequency and the temperature of the burning layer. Intensity-resolved spectral analysis indicates that the flux modulation of the mHz QPOs is primarily caused by the variations in the blackbody temperature in most observations. Furthermore, for the first time, we report that as the source evolves from the soft spectral state toward the transitional state, the marginally stable burning responsible for the mHz QPOs ignites at deeper layers of the neutron-star surface. The radiation flux associated with the mHz QPOs shows a decreasing trend as the source moves into the transitional state. These two findings support a scenario in which the marginally stable nuclear burning ignites at deeper layers as the temperature decreases, releasing less energy from the nuclear reaction. Finally, we determine that the energy release rate of the marginally stable burning is around 10$^{35}$ erg/s, consistent with the theoretical predictions.

</details>
