  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: III族恒星（第一代恒星）的探测对理解早期宇宙至关重要。潮汐瓦解事件释放巨大能量，为探测III族恒星提供了有前景的途径。然而，此前对III族恒星TDE辐射特性的研究仅限于解析分析，依赖许多假设，需要更精确的数值模拟来预测其观测特征。

Method: 采用辐射流体动力学模拟，追踪300M☉的III族恒星被10⁶M☉黑洞潮汐瓦解后吸积系统的演化过程。计算了静止坐标系中的发射特性，并考虑了红移（z~10）和消光效应。同时计算了风与核周介质相互作用产生的射电辐射。

Result: 1. 静止坐标系中光谱峰值位于光学/紫外波段；2. 考虑红移和消光后，观测光谱峰值移至红外波段，通量超过10²nJy，可被JWST和Roman望远镜探测；3. 观测光谱对视角的依赖性因尘埃消光而减弱；4. 射电辐射可产生异常持久的连续增强耀发，持续时间超过10⁴天。

Conclusion: III族恒星潮汐瓦解事件在红外波段具有可探测性，且能产生持久射电信号，为探测早期宇宙中的第一代恒星提供了新的观测途径。模拟结果为JWST和Roman望远镜的观测策略提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Tidal Disruption Events (TDEs) release enormous amounts of energy, offering a promising avenue for detecting Population III (Pop III) stars. However, the radiative properties of TDEs of Pop III stars have so far been studied only analytically, relying on many assumptions. Based on our radiative hydrodynamic simulations that follow the evolution of the accretion system for Pop III star TDEs where a $300\ M_{\odot}$ ($M_{\odot}$ is the solar mass) star is disrupted by a $10^{6}\ M_{\odot}$ black hole (BH), we compute the emission properties of the event in rest frame and find that the spectrum peaks in the optical/UV waveband. After accounting for redshift ($z \sim 10$) and extinction effects, we find the observed spectral peak shifts to the infrared, with fluxes exceeding $10^{2}\mathrm{nJy}$-making such events detectable with both the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) and the Nancy Grace Roman Space Telescope (Roman). The dependence of the observed spectrum on viewing angle is suppressed due to dust extinction. Using our simulation results, we also calculate the radio emission generated by the interaction between the wind and the circumnuclear medium (CNM) and find that a Pop III star TDE can produce an unusually long-lasting, continuously increasing radio flare with a duration greater than $10^4$ days and thus has the potential to be detected in radio wavebands. These results may be helpful to the detection of Pop III stars.

</details>


### [101] [Three-dimensional Global Relativistic Radiation Magnetohydrodynamics of Magnetically Arrested Disk Accretion Flows in AGNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11452)
*Ramiz Aktar,Kuo-Chuan Pan,Toru Okuda*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究通过三维辐射-相对论磁流体动力学模拟，分析了不同自旋黑洞周围磁滞吸积盘的动力学特性，发现黑洞自旋对吸积流动力学、辐射特性及光谱能量分布影响甚微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探究黑洞自旋对磁滞吸积盘状态下吸积流动力学和辐射特性的影响，特别是在三维辐射-相对论磁流体动力学框架下，理解自旋参数如何影响吸积盘结构、辐射过程及光谱特征。

Method: 采用三维辐射-相对论磁流体动力学模拟，研究磁滞吸积盘状态，使用单温度模型（仅包含韧致辐射不透明度），黑洞自旋参数从非自旋到快速自旋变化。后处理采用双温度模型计算同步辐射和韧致辐射光度。

Result: 磁滞吸积盘状态在所有自旋值下均保持稳定；黑洞自旋对吸积流动力学影响极小；总辐射光度显著高于同步辐射和韧致辐射光度；喷流区域电子温度始终较高；辐射和同步辐射光度的时间演化在所有自旋值下表现相似；黑洞自旋对光谱能量分布影响甚微。

Conclusion: 在磁滞吸积盘状态下，黑洞自旋对吸积流动力学、辐射特性及光谱能量分布的影响可以忽略不计，辐射过程对吸积流动力学具有显著影响，这一发现对理解活动星系核的观测特性具有重要意义。

Abstract: We perform three-dimensional radiation-relativistic magnetohydrodynamic (3D Rad-RMHD) simulations of accretion flows around spinning active galactic nuclei (AGNs). Our study focuses on the magnetically arrested disk (MAD) state, adopting a single-temperature model that includes bremsstrahlung opacity as the sole radiation process while varying the black hole spin from non-spinning to rapidly spinning cases. We find that the MAD state persists across all spin values, as demonstrated by the normalized magnetic flux at the horizon and the physically motivated spatially averaged plasma beta. The overall flow dynamics remain qualitatively similar for all spin models in 3D flow, suggesting that black hole spin has minimal influence on the accretion dynamics. In addition, we conduct post-processing using a two-temperature model to calculate the luminosities from synchrotron and bremsstrahlung radiation. We find that the total radiation luminosity is significantly higher than the luminosities from synchrotron and bremsstrahlung. This finding highlights the influence of radiation on the dynamics of the accretion flow. Our analysis shows that the electron temperature is significantly high in the jet region, regardless of spin. We further find that the temporal evolution of both radiative and synchrotron luminosities exhibits qualitatively similar behavior across all spin values. Finally, our results indicate that black hole spin has minimal impact on the spectral energy distribution (SED) in MAD state accretion flows.

</details>


### [102] [SN 2023axu: A Type IIP Supernova Interacted with a Low-Density Stellar Wind1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11646)
*Zeyi Wang,Jujia Zhang,Qian Zhai,Liping Li,G. Valerin,A. Reguitti,A. Pastorello,Zhenyu Wang,Zeyi Zhao,Tengfei Song,Yongzhi Cai*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: SN 2023axu是一颗典型的IIP型超新星，具有早期光谱中4600Å附近的宽"平台"特征，这被归因于抛射物与低密度星风弱相互作用激发的C、N、He谱线混合。晚期演化无相互作用迹象，支持低密度星风情景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究IIP型超新星SN 2023axu的光变曲线和光谱特征，特别是早期光谱中独特的4600Å"平台"特征，以理解其抛射物与星周物质的相互作用机制，探索II型超新星前身星的星风质量损失历史和星周环境多样性。

Method: 对SN 2023axu进行了约400天的测光和光谱观测，包括V波段光变曲线测量和光谱分析。通过光谱建模和比较，识别早期光谱中4600Å附近的宽"平台"特征。结合晚期光变演化分析，评估星周物质相互作用的强度。通过建模估算56Ni产量，并通过星云光谱分析推断前身星质量。

Result: 1. 光变曲线为典型正常IIP型超新星，V波段峰值-17.25±0.06 mag，无早期过量辐射表明强星周相互作用缺失；2. 早期光谱显示4600Å附近独特宽"平台"，归因于弱抛射物-星风相互作用激发的C、N、He谱线混合；3. 晚期光变演化无相互作用贡献，支持低密度星风情景；4. 合成约0.055 M⊙的56Ni；5. 星云光谱分析表明前身星质量约15 M⊙。

Conclusion: SN 2023axu代表了弱抛射物-星风相互作用的典型案例，其早期光谱特征由低密度星风相互作用产生，晚期无强相互作用迹象。该研究突出了II型超新星前身星质量损失历史和星周环境的多样性，为理解大质量恒星演化末期质量损失机制提供了重要观测约束。

Abstract: We present photometric and spectroscopic observations of Type IIP supernova SN 2023axu, spanning $\sim$400 d after the explosion. Its light curve is typical of normal SNe IIP, with a V-band peak of $-17.25 \pm 0.06$ mag and no early-time excess indicative of strong circumstellar interaction. The early spectra exhibit a distinctive broad "ledge" near 4600 Å. Through spectral modeling and comparison, we attribute this feature to a blend of C, N, and He lines excited by weak interaction between the ejecta and a low-density stellar wind. The late-time photometric evolution shows no discernible contribution from interaction, arguing against strong late-time circumstellar material engagement and supporting the low-density wind scenario. From modeling, this SN synthesized $\sim 0.055\,M_\odot$ of $^{56}$Ni, and nebular spectrum analysis indicates a progenitor mass near $15\,M_\odot$. SN 2023axu thus exemplifies weak ejecta-wind interaction and highlights the diversity of mass-loss histories and circumstellar environments of SNe II progenitors.

</details>


### [103] [Revisiting early afterglows of gamma-ray bursts with finite-thickness ejecta: Implications from XRF 080330 and GRB 080710](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11718)
*Kaori Obayashi,Ryo Yamazaki,Yo Kusafuka,Katsuaki Asano*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究通过数值余辉模型和贝叶斯推断，重新解释了XRF 080330和GRB 080710早期余辉中的消色差峰值和拐折特征，发现这些特征主要源于有限厚度的喷流动力学演化而非离轴观测效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新解释XRF 080330和GRB 080710多波段余辉中观测到的早期（数千秒后）消色差峰值和拐折的物理起源。传统薄壳近似可能无法准确描述早期余辉动力学。

Method: 使用包含有限喷流厚度和广义外部密度剖面的数值余辉模型，对两个事件进行贝叶斯推断以估计模型参数。通过贝叶斯模型比较评估不同外部密度剖面的适用性。

Result: 1. 两个事件的逐渐上升和消色差时间特征更自然地由有限壳层厚度的喷流动力学演化解释，而非离轴观测效应。
2. 推断的抛射物初始径向宽度约为10^13 cm，表明中心引擎活动时间尺度显著长于仅由伽马射线暴持续时间所暗示的时间。
3. 贝叶斯模型比较支持广义的环暴密度剖面优于经典的均匀或稳态风模型。

Conclusion: 早期余辉光变曲线在适当考虑有限抛射物厚度时，强烈受到过渡动力学的影响，这为瞬时辐射和余辉阶段提供了物理联系。简单地应用薄壳近似来解释早期余辉存在局限性，而固定外部密度结构为理想化剖面可能会掩盖前身星暴前活动的重要信息。

Abstract: We revisit the physical origin of the achromatic peaks and breaks observed several thousand seconds after the burst in the multi-wavelength afterglows of XRF 080330 and GRB 080710. Using a numerical afterglow model that consistently incorporates finite ejecta thickness and a generalized external density profile, we perform Bayesian inference to estimate model parameters describing these events. Our analysis shows that the gradual rise and achromatic temporal features in both events are more naturally explained by jet dynamical evolution with finite shell thickness rather than by off-axis viewing effects. The inferred initial radial width of the ejecta is of order $10^{13}$ cm for both bursts, implying a central engine activity timescale significantly longer than that suggested by the prompt gamma-ray duration alone. Taken together, these results demonstrate that early afterglow light curves are strongly influenced by transition dynamics when finite ejecta thickness is properly taken into account, thereby providing a physical link between the prompt and afterglow phases and highlighting limitations of simply applying the thin-shell approximation when interpreting early-time afterglows. Furthermore, Bayesian model comparison favors a generalized circumburst density profile over the canonical uniform or steady-wind models, suggesting that fixing the external density structure to idealized profiles a priori may obscure crucial information about the progenitor's pre-burst activity.

</details>


### [104] [Unveiling the biconical geometry of the outflow in the ultraluminous X-ray source NGC 5204 X-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11922)
*S. Caserta,C. Pinto,T. Di Salvo,F. Pintore,P. Kosec,F. Barra,D. J. Walton,A. D'Aì,M. Del Santo,A. Gúrpide,A. Fabian,A. Wolter*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 对NGC 5204 X-1超亮X射线源进行高分辨率X射线光谱分析，发现约0.3c蓝移和红移的碰撞电离成分，支持双锥形外流结构，并推断出发射线等离子体的物理性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超亮X射线源NGC 5204 X-1的吸积盘几何结构和相对论性外流的物理性质，探索影响ULX光谱转变的因素。

Method: 使用XMM-Newton卫星的RGS仪器进行高分辨率X射线光谱分析，包括档案数据，利用碰撞电离和光电离平衡的等离子体物理模型表征外流特征。

Result: 识别出约0.3c蓝移和红移的碰撞电离成分，具有高统计显著性，支持双锥形外流结构；通过O VII线三重态分析推断低速度线发射等离子体的电子密度（ne ∼ 10¹⁰ cm⁻³）和温度（Te ≥ 1.5×10⁵ K），支持碰撞和辐射共同影响的混合等离子体模型。

Conclusion: NGC 5204 X-1的外流呈现双锥形结构，其等离子体状态受碰撞和辐射共同影响，这些发现为理解ULX的超爱丁顿吸积过程和外流机制提供了重要线索。

Abstract: Ultraluminous X-ray sources (ULXs) are non-nuclear X-ray binary systems that exceed the Eddington luminosity for a 10 Msun black hole. The majority of these sources are thought to be stellar-mass compact objects accreting at super-Eddington rates, exhibiting powerful relativistic winds. These winds have been identified through the detection of absorption lines with a blueshift as high as 0.3c and emission lines typically found at their laboratory wavelengths. In this work, we analysed the XMM-Newton data of the ULX NGC 5204 X-1, which has been observed to exhibit emission lines with a blueshift of about 0.3c. The aim of this study is to examine the geometry and physical properties of the accretion disc and the relativistic outflows. In addition, we aim to explore the factors that influence the ULX spectral transitions. We undertook an observing campaign with XMM-Newton to explore the source behaviour at different luminosities. In this first paper of the series, we performed high-resolution X-ray spectroscopy, including archival data, with the RGS instrument which allowed us to resolve both emission and absorption lines. The outflows features were characterised using physical models of plasma in collisional-ionisation and photoionisation equilibrium. We identify collisionally-ionised blueshifted and redshifted components at about 0.3c. These findings have high statistical significance and suggest a biconical structure for the outflow. Additionally, the analysis of the O VII line triplet observed in the spectrum enables us to infer physical properties of the low-velocity line-emitting plasma, e.g. electron density (ne $\sim 10^{10}$ cm$^{-3}$) and temperature (Te $ \geq 1.5 \times 10^5$ K). A hybrid plasma whose ionisation balance is affected by both collisions and radiation is favoured.

</details>


### [105] [The twin-jet system in the FRII radio galaxy 3C 452: A sub-parsec scale VLBI study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12003)
*Eftychia Madika,Bia Boccardi,Luca Ricci,Paola Grandi,Eleonora Torresi,Gabriele Giovannini,Matthias Kadler,J. Anton Zensus*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 首次对FRII型高激发射电星系3C 452进行多频率VLBI分析，揭示其亚秒差距尺度的双喷流结构，发现对称抛物线膨胀、低多普勒因子和大视角特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次在VLBI尺度上详细研究FRII型高激发射电星系3C 452的双喷流结构，特别是其亚秒差距尺度的喷流特性，填补了这类罕见源在毫米波观测上的空白。

Method: 使用高灵敏度阵列(HSA)在4.9、8.4、15.4、23.6和43.2 GHz进行多频率VLBI观测，采用可见度平面和图像平面的拟合方法追踪喷流膨胀，分析核心亮度温度、多普勒因子和谱指数。

Result: 首次在数千到近10^5史瓦西半径尺度上解析了3C 452的双喷流结构，发现对称抛物线膨胀（喷流k≈0.66，反喷流k≈0.47），低多普勒因子（δ∼0.03-0.83）表明大视角（θ≈70°）或磁主导喷流基，谱指数分析显示强反转核心谱和急剧陡变。

Conclusion: 3C 452是罕见的在毫米波探测到喷流的FRII源，窄线高激发射电星系（如3C 452和天鹅座A）在≤10^5 R_S完成准直，而宽线源在10^6-10^7 R_S发生形态转变，表明观测视角在喷流准直尺度中起重要作用。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive multifrequency VLBI analysis of the FRII, high-excitation radio galaxy 3C 452, aiming to resolve and analyze for the first time its twin-jet structure on sub-parsec scales. Our data set comprises High Sensitivity Array (HSA) observations at 4.9, 8.4, 15.4, 23.6, and 43.2 GHz. Through fitting methods performed in both the visibility and the image plane, we trace the jet expansion from scales of a few thousand to nearly $10^5$ Schwarzschild radii ($R_S$) on both the approaching and receding jets. Additionally, we derive the core brightness temperatures and Doppler factors to constrain the jet's orientation and intrinsic speed. Our study provides the first detailed description of the twin-jet system in 3C 452 on VLBI scales, confirming it as a rare FRII source with jets detected down to millimeter wavelengths. We resolve both jet and counter-jet down to scales of a few thousand $R_S$, revealing a symmetric, parabolically expanding structure with power-law indices $k \approx 0.66$ (jet) and $k \approx 0.47$ (counter-jet). The brightness temperature analysis yields low Doppler factors ($δ\sim 0.03$-$0.83$), indicative of Doppler de-boosting due to the large viewing angle ($θ\approx 70^\circ$) and/or a magnetically dominated jet base. A spectral index analysis reveals a strongly inverted core spectrum ($α> 2$) with additional absorption at the highest frequencies, followed by a sharp steepening ($α\sim -2.5$) to optically thin values in the innermost jet. Finally, a comparison between broad- and narrow-line high-excitation radio galaxies shows that jets in narrow-line sources such as 3C 452 and Cygnus A complete collimation at $\leq 10^5 R_S$, whereas broad-line sources exhibit shape transitions at $10^6$-$10^7 R_S$, suggesting that orientation plays an important role in the observed collimation scales.

</details>


### [106] [Sensitivity to Axion-like Particle dark matter with very-high-energy gamma-ray observations of Active Galactic Nuclei located behind Galaxy Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12009)
*Cervane Grimaud,Denys Malyshev,Emmanuel Moulin*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究通过堆叠分析位于星系团后方的活动星系核，预测了利用伽马射线望远镜探测轴子类粒子-光子耦合的灵敏度，可达6×10⁻¹³ GeV⁻¹，探索了10⁻⁸-10⁻⁷ eV质量范围内未探测的轴子类粒子暗物质参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子类粒子是假想的赝标量粒子，被认为是轻暗物质候选者。其与光子的耦合会在天体物理源的伽马射线谱中产生特征性谱特征。星系团磁场中的轴子类粒子-光子转换会在活动星系核光谱中产生吸收状特征，但单个源的预测存在较大方差，因此需要堆叠分析多个源对来获得更可控的预测。

Method: 采用堆叠分析方法，对位于星系团后方的多个活动星系核进行模拟观测。使用费米大面积望远镜选定的活动星系核，通过成像大气切伦科夫望远镜（如H.E.S.S.、MAGIC和VERITAS）进行真实模拟观测。评估多个活动星系核-星系团对的联合统计分析预期灵敏度，并考虑建模和仪器不确定性的影响。

Result: 对于3×10⁻⁸ eV质量的轴子类粒子，灵敏度可达轴子类粒子-光子耦合常数6×10⁻¹³ GeV⁻¹。当前灵敏度主要受统计不确定性主导，表明更多观测可进一步改进。堆叠分析方法能够探索10⁻⁸-10⁻⁷ eV质量范围内尚未探测的轴子类粒子暗物质参数空间。

Conclusion: 堆叠分析多个位于星系团后方的活动星系核是探测轴子类粒子-光子相互作用的有效方法，能够提供对轴子类粒子暗物质参数空间的新约束，特别是在10⁻⁸-10⁻⁷ eV质量范围内。该方法目前受统计不确定性主导，未来更多观测数据可进一步提高灵敏度。

Abstract: Axion-Like-Particles (ALPs) are hypothetical pseudo-scalar particles actively searched as light dark matter candidates. The coupling of ALPs to photons can give rise to distinctive spectral features in the observed gamma-ray spectrum of astrophysical sources.
  We perform a forecast study on the sensitivity to ALP-photon interactions using stacked mock observations of selected active galactic nuclei (AGNs) located behind galaxy clusters (GC). The ALP-photon conversion in the magnetic fields of galaxy clusters give rise to absorption-like features in AGN spectra that are subject to large variance in their prediction for individual sources. We consider here a stacking analysis of multiple AGN-cluster pairs, which yields a more controlled prediction of the expected ALP-induced spectral patterns in the observed gamma-ray spectra.
  Using realistic mock observations of selected Fermi-LAT AGNs by ongoing Imaging Atmospheric Cherenkov Telescopes such as H.E.S.S., MAGIC and VERITAS, we provide a careful assessment of the expected sensitivity of a combined statistical analysis of many AGN-GC pairs, together with the impact of modelling and instrumental uncertainties. The sensitivity reaches ALP-photon couplings down to 6$\times$10$^{-13}$ GeV$^{-1}$ for an ALP mass of 3$\times$10$^{-8}$ eV, and is currently statistically dominated indicating further improvements from more observations. Such a stacking analysis approach enables exploration of the yet-uncharted ALP dark matter parameter space in the 10$^{-8}$ - 10$^{-7}$ eV mass range.

</details>


### [107] [X-ray evidence from NuSTAR for a Mach 3 shock in Merging Galaxy Cluster ZWCL 1856.8](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12207)
*Ayşegül Tümer,Christian T. Norseth,Daniel R. Wik*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过更深的NuSTAR观测，在ZWCL1856.8+6616星系团合并系统中发现了北侧遗迹处存在迄今最强的X射线激波（M=3.90），远强于其对应的射电激波（M=2.5），表明粒子加速在激波后区域更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究星系团合并系统中射电遗迹与X射线激波特征之间的关系，特别是通过更深的NuSTAR观测来探测激波强度和逆康普顿辐射。

Method: 使用270 ks的NuSTAR深度观测数据，分析温度结构；采用nucrossarf生成名义和交叉ARF来消除点扩展函数引起的区域间光子交叉污染；在射电遗迹位置测量X射线激波强度。

Result: 在北侧遗迹处检测到M=3.90(+1.64,-0.85)的极强X射线激波，远超射电激波的M=2.5±0.2；南侧激波强度为M=2.36(+0.58,-0.46)；未在射电遗迹位置检测到逆康普顿辐射。

Conclusion: 北侧射电遗迹区域激波强度差异表明粒子加速在激波后区域更高效；X射线激波强度显著高于射电激波，为星系团合并物理提供了新见解；未检测到逆康普顿辐射限制了相对论电子种群。

Abstract: We present spectral analysis results of deeper (270 ks) NuSTAR observations of the merging galaxy cluster system, ZWCL1856.8+6616, at redshift z=0.304, following a pilot study using shallower (30 ks) NuSTAR data (Tumer et al. 2024). The cluster hosts a double radio relic, pointing to a similar mass head-on collision at/near the plane of sky. We aim to find the relation between radio and X-ray shock features. Using data from both focal plane modules of NuSTAR, we study the temperature structure across the field of view and report on the X-ray detected shock strength at the relic sites. We generate nominal and cross-ARFs with nucrossarf to disentangle photon cross-contamination within regions of interest due to the moderate point spread function of NuSTAR. Here we report one of the strongest X-ray detected shocks in a galaxy cluster merger with M=3.90(+1.64,-0.85) at the Northern relic site, that is unprecedentedly larger than the radio counterpart; M=2.5+/-0.2 (Jones et al. 2021a), and we report Southern shock strength as M=2.36(+0.58,-0.46). We argue that since the Northern relic (or radio shock), is confined in a very small region in the sky, particle acceleration is more efficient and is likely to grow in the post-shock regions. In addition, we search for inverse Compton (IC) emission at the radio relic sites; however, an IC component was not detected.

</details>


<div id='cs.CG'></div>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Approximate Dynamic Nearest Neighbor Searching in a Polygonal Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11775)
*Joost van der Laan,Frank Staals,Lorenzo Theunissen*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 提出在二维多边形域中支持动态点集插入删除的近似最近邻搜索和近似两点最短路径查询的高效数据结构


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二维多边形域中，计算任意两点间距离需要大量空间，因此需要设计支持动态更新且能高效近似计算距离和最近邻查询的数据结构

Method: 构建基于ε-近似距离计算的数据结构：首先构建O(n/ε log n)空间的数据结构，能在O(1/ε² log n)时间内计算两点间(1+ε)-近似距离；然后扩展为支持动态点集的O((n+m)/ε log n + m/ε log m)空间数据结构

Result: 实现了(1+ε)-近似最近邻查询，查询时间为O(1/ε² log n + 1/ε log n log m + 1/ε log² m)，更新时间为O(1/ε² log n + 1/ε log n log m + 1/ε log² m)（摊销时间）

Conclusion: 提出的数据结构在二维多边形域中实现了高效的动态近似最近邻搜索，在空间和时间复杂度上取得了良好的平衡，支持插入和删除操作

Abstract: We present efficient data structures for approximate nearest neighbor searching and approximate 2-point shortest path queries in a two-dimensional polygonal domain $P$ with $n$ vertices. Our goal is to store a dynamic set of $m$ point sites $S$ in $P$ so that we can efficiently find a site $s \in S$ closest to an arbitrary query point $q$. We will allow both insertions and deletions in the set of sites $S$. However, as even just computing the distance between an arbitrary pair of points $q,s \in P$ requires a substantial amount of space, we allow for approximating the distances. Given a parameter $\varepsilon > 0$, we build an $O(\frac{n}{\varepsilon}\log n)$ space data structure that can compute a $1+\varepsilon$-approximation of the distance between $q$ and $s$ in $O(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^2}\log n)$ time. Building on this, we then obtain an $O(\frac{n+m}{\varepsilon}\log n + \frac{m}{\varepsilon}\log m)$ space data structure that allows us to report a site $s \in S$ so that the distance between query point $q$ and $s$ is at most $(1+\varepsilon)$-times the distance between $q$ and its true nearest neighbor in $O(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^2}\log n + \frac{1}{\varepsilon}\log n \log m + \frac{1}{\varepsilon}\log^2 m)$ time. Our data structure supports updates in $O(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^2}\log n + \frac{1}{\varepsilon}\log n \log m + \frac{1}{\varepsilon}\log^2 m)$ amortized time.

</details>


### [109] [Fast and exact visibility on digitized shapes and application to saliency-aware normal estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11851)
*Romain Negro,Jacques-Olivier Lachaud*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 利用数字集合的积分区间列表表示高效计算数字形状格点间的完整可见性图，并应用于准确估计数字形状的法向量场


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何对象上的可见性计算通常需要大量计算，因为需要识别相互可见的点对（即存在连接两点且保持在对象边界附近的直线段）。本文旨在利用数字集合的特定表示来高效计算可见性图。

Method: 提出利用基于积分区间列表的数字集合表示方法，高效计算数字形状格点间的完整可见性图。该方法能够处理数字形状的边界附近可见性关系。

Result: 开发了基于积分区间列表表示的高效可见性图计算算法，能够准确识别数字形状中格点间的可见性关系。该方法为法向量场估计提供了基础。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法不仅能够高效计算数字形状的可见性图，还能应用于准确且收敛地估计数字形状的法向量场，同时保持对形状显著和尖锐特征的感知。

Abstract: Computing visibility on a geometric object requires heavy computations since it requires to identify pairs of points that are visible to each other, i.e. there is a straight segment joining them that stays in the close vicinity of the object boundary. We propose to exploit a specic representation of digital sets based on lists of integral intervals in order to compute eciently the complete visibility graph between lattice points of the digital shape. As a quite direct application, we show then how we can use visibility to estimate the normal vector eld of a digital shape in an accurate and convergent manner while staying aware of the salient and sharp features of the shape.

</details>


### [110] [On strictly output sensitive color frequency reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11898)
*Erwin Glazenburg,Frank Staals*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究了颜色频率报告问题，针对轴对齐框或支配范围查询，提出了空间-时间权衡的数据结构，证明了其接近最优性，并扩展到高维和空间优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颜色频率报告问题在计算几何中具有重要意义，需要高效存储彩色点集以支持查询区域内颜色及其频率的报告。现有方法通常关注存在性查询，而本文关注频率信息的精确报告，旨在实现严格输出敏感的查询时间。

Method: 1. 对于二维点集，构建了O(ns log_s n)大小的数据结构，支持O(log n + k log_s n)查询时间；2. 在算术计算模型中证明了加权问题的下界；3. 通过变换将空间复杂度降至O(n(sφ)^ε log_s n)；4. 提出了处理多个支配查询的算法，使用线性工作空间。

Result: 1. 提出的数据结构在二维情况下实现了空间-时间权衡；2. 下界证明表明该数据结构接近最优；3. 空间优化方法有效减少了存储需求；4. 多查询算法在保持线性工作空间的同时实现了高效处理。

Conclusion: 本文为颜色频率报告问题提供了接近最优的解决方案，通过创新的数据结构和算法设计，在空间复杂度、查询时间和工作空间之间实现了良好的平衡，并将结果扩展到高维情况。

Abstract: Given a set of $n$ colored points $P \subset \mathbb{R}^d$ we wish to store $P$ such that, given some query region $Q$, we can efficiently report the colors of the points appearing in the query region, along with their frequencies. This is the \emph{color frequency reporting} problem. We study the case where query regions $Q$ are axis-aligned boxes or dominance ranges. If $Q$ contains $k$ colors, the main goal is to achieve ``strictly output sensitive'' query time $O(f(n) + k)$. Firstly, we show that, for every $s \in \{ 2, \dots, n \}$, there exists a simple $O(ns\log_s n)$ size data structure for points in $\mathbb{R}^2$ that allows frequency reporting queries in $O(\log n + k\log_s n)$ time. Secondly, we give a lower bound for the weighted version of the problem in the arithmetic model of computation, proving that with $O(m)$ space one can not achieve query times better than $Ω\left(φ\frac{\log (n / φ)}{\log (m / n)}\right)$, where $φ$ is the number of possible colors. This means that our data structure is near-optimal. We extend these results to higher dimensions as well. Thirdly, we present a transformation that allows us to reduce the space usage of the aforementioned datastructure to $O(n(s φ)^\varepsilon \log_s n)$. Finally, we give an $O(n^{1+\varepsilon} + m \log n + K)$-time algorithm that can answer $m$ dominance queries $\mathbb{R}^2$ with total output complexity $K$, while using only linear working space.

</details>


### [111] [Space-Efficient Approximate Spherical Range Counting in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12106)
*Andreas Kalavas,Ioannis Psarros*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了高维欧几里得空间中的近似范围搜索问题，构建了具有近线性空间复杂度和亚线性查询时间的数据结构，突破了维度灾难的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维欧几里得空间中，精确的范围搜索需要指数级的空间复杂度（维度灾难）。因此需要设计近似解决方案，在可接受的空间和查询时间下提供近似结果。

Method: 构建近似数据结构，允许查询时考虑距离在(1+ε)r范围内的点。采用基于查询分布的预处理算法，使数据结构适应查询分布特征。

Result: 实现了具有近线性空间复杂度的数据结构，查询时间为n^{1-Θ(ε⁴/log(1/ε))}+t_q^ρ·n^{1-ρ}，其中ρ=Θ(ε²)，t_q是模糊区域中的点数。这是首个在任意ε>0时具有亚二次或近线性空间复杂度，且对任意亚线性t_q保持亚线性查询时间的数据结构。

Conclusion: 该研究成功克服了高维范围搜索中的维度灾难问题，通过近似方法实现了高效的空间和时间复杂度平衡，并提供了适应查询分布的预处理算法。

Abstract: We study the following range searching problem in high-dimensional Euclidean spaces: given a finite set $P\subset \mathbb{R}^d$, where each $p\in P$ is assigned a weight $w_p$, and radius $r>0$, we need to preprocess $P$ into a data structure such that when a new query point $q\in \mathbb{R}^d$ arrives, the data structure reports the cumulative weight of points of $P$ within Euclidean distance $r$ from $q$. Solving the problem exactly seems to require space usage that is exponential to the dimension, a phenomenon known as the curse of dimensionality. Thus, we focus on approximate solutions where points up to $(1+\varepsilon)r$ away from $q$ may be taken into account, where $\varepsilon>0$ is an input parameter known during preprocessing. We build a data structure with near-linear space usage, and query time in $n^{1-Θ(\varepsilon^4/\log(1/\varepsilon))}+t_q^{\varrho}\cdot n^{1-\varrho}$, for some $\varrho=Θ(\varepsilon^2)$, where $t_q$ is the number of points of $P$ in the ambiguity zone, i.e., at distance between $r$ and $(1+\varepsilon)r$ from the query $q$. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first data structure with efficient space usage (subquadratic or near-linear for any $\varepsilon>0$) and query time that remains sublinear for any sublinear $t_q$. We supplement our worst-case bounds with a query-driven preprocessing algorithm to build data structures that are well-adapted to the query distribution.

</details>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [From Computational Certification to Exact Coordinates: Heilbronn's Triangle Problem on the Unit Square Using Mixed-Integer Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11107)
*Nathan Sudermann-Merx*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出优化-精化框架解决Heilbronn三角形问题，结合混合整数非线性规划和精确符号计算，显著提升计算效率，证明n=9配置的全局最优性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决经典Heilbronn三角形问题的计算挑战，特别是n=9情况需要约一天计算时间，寻求更高效的全局优化方法

Method: 开发优化-精化框架，结合全局混合整数非线性规划和精确符号计算；采用新颖的对称性破缺策略，利用行列式结构特性构建更强优化模型

Result: n=9问题在15分钟内求解到全局最优，比之前一天计算提升一个数量级；首次证明Comellas和Yebra 2002年发现的n=9配置是全局最优；为n=5-9提供精确坐标配置

Conclusion: 新框架显著提升Heilbronn三角形问题的求解效率，发现非关键三角形面积聚集于少量不同值的结构模式，为极值点集的组合几何研究提供新方向

Abstract: We develop an optimize-then-refine framework for the classical Heilbronn triangle problem that integrates global mixed-integer nonlinear programming with exact symbolic computation. A novel symmetry-breaking strategy, together with the exploitation of structural properties of determinants, yields a substantially stronger optimization model: for $n=9$, the problem can be solved to certified global optimality in 15 minutes on a standard desktop computer, improving upon the previously reported effort of about one day by more than an order of magnitude. Combining the numerical certificate with exact symbolic computation, we provide the first proof that the configuration discovered by Comellas and Yebra in 2002 for $n=9$ is globally optimal, and derive exact coordinates for all optimal configurations with $n=5,\dots,9$, confirming earlier best-known results and sometimes simplifying their presentation. An analysis of these configurations reveals structural patterns-notably the clustering of noncritical triangle areas around a small number of distinct values-which give rise to new research questions about the combinatorial geometry of extremal point sets. All configurations and code are publicly available to provide a reproducible foundation for further research.

</details>


### [113] [First and second-order optimality conditions for a bilinear controlled wave equation on an infinite horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11271)
*Redouane El Mezegueldy,Zakarya Dardour*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了无限时间域上双线性阻尼波方程的最优控制问题，建立了系统适定性、最优控制存在性，并推导了一阶和二阶最优性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无限时间域上双线性阻尼波方程的最优控制问题，这类系统在工程和物理中有广泛应用，但无限时间域上的最优控制理论分析相对复杂，需要建立完整的数学框架。

Method: 首先建立控制系统的适定性和一致性能量估计；通过构造极小化序列证明最优控制存在性；证明控制到状态映射的二阶Fréchet可微性；推导一阶必要条件（变分不等式和逐点投影公式）；建立二阶必要和充分条件（Hessian非负性和强制性）。

Result: 证明了双线性阻尼波方程在无限时间域上的适定性；建立了最优控制的存在性；推导了一阶最优性条件（变分不等式和逐点投影公式）；建立了二阶最优性条件（Hessian非负性为必要条件，强制性为充分条件）。

Conclusion: 该研究为有界空间域上无限时间域的双线性双曲控制系统提供了完整的局部最优性刻画，建立了完整的数学理论框架，包括适定性、最优控制存在性和一阶、二阶最优性条件。

Abstract: This paper investigates the optimal control of a bilinear damped wave equation over an infinite time horizon. We establish the well-posedness of the controlled system and derive uniform energy estimates. The existence of optimal controls is proven by constructing a minimizing sequence. We prove that the control-to-state mapping is twice continuously Fréchet differentiable, which enables the derivation of first-order necessary optimality conditions in the form of a variational inequality and a pointwise projection formula. Furthermore, we establish second-order necessary and sufficient conditions: the nonnegativity of the Hessian of the cost functional is shown to be a necessary condition for local optimality, while the coercivity of this Hessian constitutes a sufficient condition. These results provide a complete characterization of local optimality for bilinear hyperbolic control systems over infinite time horizons on bounded spatial domains.

</details>


### [114] [Minimizers that are not Impulsive Minimizers and Higher Order Abnormality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11302)
*Monica Motta,Michele Palladino,Franco Rampazzo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究最优控制中的两个相关问题：1) 分离集方法与惩罚技术之间的兼容性问题，通过QDQ逼近锥与Clarke切锥的关系解决；2) 将兼容性结果应用于无界控制和脉冲扩展问题中的下确界间隙现象，建立严格意义极小化器的异常性与高阶极大值原理的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决最优控制中两个经典方法（分离集方法和惩罚技术）之间的兼容性问题，这些方法由于在目标处使用不同的切性概念而导致非等价条件。同时研究无界控制和脉冲扩展问题中的下确界间隙现象，特别是严格意义极小化器的异常性与高阶极大值原理之间的关系。

Method: 第一部分：使用拟微分商(QDQ)逼近锥（适用于分离集方法）并确定Clarke切锥（惩罚技术的典型工具）也是QDQ逼近锥的条件，特别是在局部不变性假设或目标与r-邻近正则集局部重合的情况下。第二部分：将兼容性结果应用于无界控制和脉冲扩展问题，通过拓扑论证和前述兼容性问题，建立严格意义极小化器的下确界间隙与涉及李括号的高阶极大值原理异常性之间的对应关系。

Result: 证明了在适当的局部不变性假设下或当目标局部与r-邻近正则集重合时，Clarke切锥也是QDQ逼近锥。建立了严格意义极小化器的下确界间隙与涉及李括号的高阶极大值原理异常性之间的对应关系，这一关系先前仅对扩展意义（脉冲）极小化器建立，现扩展到严格意义极小化器。

Conclusion: 论文成功解决了最优控制中分离集方法与惩罚技术之间的兼容性问题，为两种经典方法提供了统一框架。同时，将兼容性结果应用于下确界间隙现象，建立了严格意义极小化器的异常性与高阶极大值原理之间的对应关系，扩展了先前仅适用于扩展意义极小化器的结果。

Abstract: This paper addresses two related problems in optimal control. The first investigation consists of compatibility issues between two classical approaches to deriving necessary conditions for optimal control problems with a final target: the set-separation approach and penalization techniques. These methods generally lead to non-equivalent conditions, mainly due to their reliance on different notions of tangency at the target.
  We address this issue by considering Quasi Differential Quotient (QDQ) approximating cones (which are fit for the set-separation approach) and identifying conditions under which the Clarke tangent cone (which is a typical tool within penalization techniques) is also a QDQ approximating cone. In particular, we show that this property holds under suitable local invariance assumptions or when the target coincides locally with an $r$-prox regular set.
  In the second part of the paper we apply this compatibility result to the study of infimum-gap phenomena in optimal control problems with unbounded controls and impulsive extensions. In particular, we establish a connection between the occurrence of infimum gaps for strict-sense minimizers and abnormality in a higher-order Maximum Principle involving Lie brackets. While the abnormality-gap correspondence beyond first-order conditions has been already established for extended-sense --i.e. impulsive-- minimizers, a topological argument involving the former and the utilization of the above compatibility issues allow us to extend this correspondence to strict-sense minimizers.

</details>


### [115] [Blind Hyperspectral and Multispectral Images Fusion: A Unified Tensor Fusion Framework from Coupled Inverse Problem Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11530)
*Ying Gao,Michael K. Ng,Chunfeng cui*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种盲融合框架，将高光谱与多光谱图像融合中的空间模糊算子和光谱响应算子作为未知参数进行联合估计，无需预训练即可实现实时融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高光谱与多光谱图像融合方法假设空间模糊算子和光谱响应算子已知，这在实践中限制性太强。需要克服这一限制，开发无需先验知识的盲融合方法。

Method: 将盲融合建模为耦合逆问题，结合空间域的盲去卷积和光谱域的盲解混。提出统一张量融合框架，构建联合估计目标高分辨率高光谱图像、空间点扩散函数和光谱响应函数的优化模型，采用带Moreau包络平滑的部分线性化ADMM算法求解，并设计针对融合问题特性的初始化估计器。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上与最先进方法的数值比较表明，所提方法具有优越性能，能够有效实现盲融合任务。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种新颖的盲融合框架，通过联合估计未知参数解决了传统方法对先验知识的依赖问题，实现了灵活自调整和实时融合，为高光谱图像处理提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Hyperspectral and multispectral images fusion aims at integrating a low-resolution hyperspectral image (LR-HSI) and a high-resolution multispectral image (HR-MSI) to construct a high-resolution hyperspectral image (HR-HSI). It is generally assumed that spatial blurring operator and spectral response operator are prior-known. However, such an assumption is extremely restrictive in practice. To overcome this limitation, this paper formulates blind fusion as a coupled inverse problem, integrating blind deconvolution in the spatial domain with blind unmixing in the spectral domain. From this novel perspective, we propose a unified tensor fusion framework capable of flexible self-adjustment and real-time fusion without pre-training. We further introduce an optimization model for the joint estimation of the target HR-HSI, the spatial point spread function, and the spectral response function. To solve this model, we devise a partially linearized alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) algorithm with Moreau envelope smoothing, accompanied by the rigorous convergence analysis. An initialization estimator tailored to the specific characteristics of the fusion problem is proposed. Numerical comparisons with state-of-the-art methods on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate the compelling performance of the proposed method.

</details>


### [116] [Simultaneous estimation of multiple discrete unimodal distributions under stochastic order constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11532)
*Yasuhiro Yoshida,Noriyoshi Sukegawa,Jiro Iwanaga*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种估计多个离散单峰分布的方法，通过施加随机顺序约束来整合先验知识，将问题建模为混合整数凸二次优化问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受现实世界平台搜索行为分析驱动，需要估计多个离散单峰分布，同时考虑分布间的优先关系先验知识

Method: 施加随机顺序约束，将估计任务建模为混合整数凸二次优化问题

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，当样本量较小时，Jensen-Shannon散度平均降低2.2%（最高达6.3%）；当数据充足时，性能与现有方法相当

Conclusion: 提出的方法在小样本情况下能有效利用先验知识提升估计精度，在数据充足时保持竞争力

Abstract: We study the problem of estimating multiple discrete unimodal distributions, motivated by search behavior analysis on a real-world platform. To incorporate prior knowledge of precedence relations among distributions, we impose stochastic order constraints and formulate the estimation task as a mixed-integer convex quadratic optimization problem. Experiments on both synthetic and real datasets show that the proposed method reduces the Jensen-Shannon divergence by 2.2% on average (up to 6.3%) when the sample size is small, while performing comparably to existing methods when sufficient data are available.

</details>


### [117] [Families of Two-Impulse Optimal Rendezvous Transfers Between Elliptic Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11538)
*Beom Park,Kathleen C. Howell,Jaewoo Kim,Jaemyung Ahn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该研究从族系角度重新审视了开普勒轨道间的燃料最优双脉冲交会问题，揭示了孤立最优解之间的连续连接关系，通过数值延拓方法追踪解族，并分析了最优分支在轨道几何变化下的演化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通常只能得到孤立的最优解，这些解之间的相互关系不明确。作者希望通过族系视角揭示看似无关的最优解实际上是连续解族的成员，从而提供对解空间全局结构的理解。

Method: 提出一个框架，强制执行一阶必要最优性条件的子集，通过数值延拓方法追踪由此产生的一参数解族。使用基于Hessian的准则和Primer Vector理论对解族进行分类，并将解族投影到porkchop图上以连接角度域和时间域。

Result: 代表性案例研究表明，在轨道几何变化下，局部最优分支会出现、合并和消失，提供了解空间的全局映射。这一互补视角阐明了最优解的鲁棒性，并识别了标称轨迹附近的替代近最优转移方案。

Conclusion: 该研究为燃料最优双脉冲交会问题提供了新的族系视角，揭示了最优解之间的连续连接关系，有助于理解解空间的全局结构，并为轨迹优化提供了更全面的解决方案。

Abstract: The classical fuel-optimal two-impulse rendezvous problem between Keplerian orbits is revisited from a family-based perspective. Conventional approaches often yield isolated optimal solutions whose mutual relationships remain unclear; yet, when re-parameterized appropriately, seemingly unrelated optima are revealed to be connected members of continuous solution families. To expose this structure, the proposed framework enforces a subset of first-order necessary optimality conditions and traces the resulting one-parameter families via numerical continuation. The families are classified using Hessian-based criteria and Primer Vector Theory, and are projected onto porkchop plots to connect the angular and temporal domains. Representative case studies reveal the emergence, merging, and disappearance of locally optimal branches under variations in orbital geometry, supplying a global map of the solution landscape. This complementary perspective clarifies the robustness of optimal solutions and identifies alternative near-optimal transfers in the vicinity of a nominal trajectory.

</details>


### [118] [Exploiting Parallelism in a QPALM-based Solver for Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11723)
*Pieter Pas,Kristoffer Fink Løwenstein,Daniele Bernardini,Panagiotis Patrinos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: QPALM-OCP算法在最优控制二次规划问题中的并行化机会分析及优化实现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用最优控制问题中不同阶段可独立计算的结构特点，探索QPALM-OCP算法的并行化潜力，以提高计算效率

Method: 分析QPALM-OCP算法的计算结构，识别可并行化的部分，采用并行化和向量化技术，在C++中实现优化版本

Result: 提供了最优控制基准问题的测试结果，并与原始QPALM方法进行了性能比较

Conclusion: 最优控制问题的特定结构为QPALM-OCP算法提供了显著的并行化机会，通过并行化和向量化技术可有效提升计算性能

Abstract: We discuss the opportunities for parallelization in the recently proposed QPALM-OCP algorithm, a solver tailored to quadratic programs arising in optimal control. A significant part of the computational work can be carried out independently for the different stages in the optimal control problem. We exploit this specific structure to apply parallelization and vectorization techniques in an optimized C++ implementation of the method. Results for optimal control benchmark problems and comparisons to the original QPALM method are provided.

</details>


### [119] [An Eikonal Approach for Globally Optimal Free Flight Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11830)
*Ralf Borndörfer,Arturas Jocas,Martin Weiser*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于程函方程的方法，用于在静止风场中寻找飞机的连续全局最优轨迹，以最小化排放和燃料消耗。通过有限元误差估计构建割迹周围的信任区域，保证远离割迹的目的地轨迹唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当目的地靠近Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程的割迹时，小的数值离散化误差可能导致选择局部最优而非全局最优轨迹。需要解决数值误差对轨迹选择的影响，确保找到全局最优解。

Method: 采用基于程函方程的方法，结合有限元误差估计，在割迹周围构建信任区域。该方法能够保证对于足够远离割迹的目的地，轨迹具有唯一性。

Result: 该方法能够找到连续全局最优轨迹，最小化飞机的排放和燃料消耗。通过信任区域机制，避免了因数值误差而选择局部最优解的问题。

Conclusion: 提出的eikonal-based方法结合信任区域机制，能够可靠地找到全局最优轨迹，解决了靠近割迹时数值误差导致的轨迹选择问题，为飞机在风场中的节能路径规划提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We present an eikonal-based approach that is capable of finding a continuous globally optimal trajectory for an aircraft in a stationary wind field. This minimizes emissions and fuel consumption. If the destination is close to a cut locus of the associated Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation, small numerical discretization errors can lead to selecting a merely locally optimal trajectory and missing the globally optimal one. Based on finite element error estimates, we construct a trust region around the cut loci in order to guarantee uniqueness of trajectories for destinations sufficiently far from cut loci.

</details>


### [120] [Generalisation of Farkas' lemma beyond closedness: a constructive approach via Fenchel-Rockafellar duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11859)
*Camille Pouchol,Emmanuel Trélat,Christophe Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于优化对偶理论和Fenchel-Rockafellar对偶的Farkas引理新推广方法，放宽了传统要求A(P)闭的条件，仅需P由闭有界凸集生成，适用于非闭锥情形


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Farkas引理要求A(P)闭，但这一条件难以验证且不总是成立。现有推广大多仍需A(P)闭或至少P闭，限制了应用范围。需要更弱的假设条件来扩展Farkas引理的应用场景

Method: 基于原始-对偶优化问题对和Fenchel-Rockafellar对偶理论，在仅要求P由闭有界凸集生成的弱假设下，构建新的Farkas引理推广框架。通过最优性条件构造性地刻画满足‖Ax-b‖≤ε的x∈P的存在性

Result: 获得了b∈A(P)和b∈A(P)闭包的必要充分条件。当ε=0时，结果强烈依赖于对偶问题是否有解，并讨论了确保对偶解存在的条件。方法还可通过松弛应用于非凸锥

Conclusion: 提出了一种在更弱假设下推广Farkas引理的新方法，仅要求P由闭有界凸集生成，不要求A(P)或P闭。该方法提供了构造性刻画，扩展了Farkas引理在优化理论中的应用范围

Abstract: Farkas' lemma is an ubiquitous tool in optimisation, as it provides necessary and sufficient conditions to have $b \in A(P)$, where $P$ is a closed convex cone, $A$ is a (continuous) linear mapping and $b$ is a fixed vector. The standard underlying hypothesis is the closedness of $A(P)$, which is not always satisfied and can be difficult to check. We devise a new method to generalise Farkas' lemma, based on a primal-dual pair of optimisation problems and Fenchel-Rockafellar duality theory. We work under the sole hypothesis that $P$ be generated by a closed bounded convex set. This hypothesis is weaker than in previous generalisations of Farkas' lemma, which almost all require that $A(P)$ be closed, or, in few cases, that only $P$ be closed. In our case, $P$ (and a fortiori $A(P)$) is not necessarily closed; we uncover necessary and sufficient conditions both for $b \in A(P)$ and $b \in \overline{A(P)}$. For a given $\e \geq 0$, we exhibit constructive characterisations of $x \in P$ such that $\|Ax-b\| \leq \e$ when it exists, by means of optimality conditions. For $\e = 0$, these strongly rely on whether the dual problem admits a solution, and we discuss conditions under which it does. Finally, we also explain how, upon relaxation, we may apply our method to a nonconvex cone.

</details>


### [121] [Asymptotically Efficient Recursive Identification Under One-Bit Communications Achieving Original CRLB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11964)
*Xingrui Liu,Jieming Ke,Mingjie Shao,Yanlong Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种针对一比特通信下ARX系统的渐近高效递归辨识算法，通过融合当前和历史输出输入信息，使量化损失相对于原始CRLB可忽略，达到基于原始数据的CRLB性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一比特量化辨识方法通常只量化当前系统输出，导致信息损失不可忽略，只能达到量化观测的CRLB。需要开发能够达到原始数据CRLB的渐近高效算法。

Method: 提出新颖的量化方法，融合当前和历史系统输出与输入信息，在一比特数据中提供更丰富的参数信息；基于此技术设计相应的远程估计算法；建立新框架分析量化前组合数据的尾概率，避免传统独立性假设。

Result: 证明远程估计具有渐近正态性，误差协方差矩阵收敛于原始CRLB，确认其渐近效率；相比现有一比特数据辨识算法，渐近均方误差至少降低36%；数值仿真验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提算法通过融合历史信息的量化策略，在一比特通信下实现了ARX系统的渐近高效辨识，突破了现有方法只能达到量化CRLB的限制，显著提升了估计精度。

Abstract: This paper develops an asymptotically efficient recursive identification algorithm for autoregressive systems with exogenous inputs under one-bit communications. In particular, the proposed method asymptotically achieves the Cramer-Rao lower bound (CRLB) based on the original data before quantization (original CRLB), whereas existing approaches typically attain only the CRLB corresponding to the quantized observations. The primary reason is that the existing methods quantize only the current system output, resulting in non-negligible information loss under one-bit quantization. To overcome this challenge, we present a novel quantization method that integrates both current and historical system outputs and inputs to provide richer parameter information in one-bit data, allowing the information loss caused by quantization to become a minor term relative to the original CRLB. Based on this technique, a corresponding remote estimation algorithm is further proposed. To address the convergence analysis challenge posed by the non-independence of the one-bit data, we establish a new framework that analyzes the tail probability of integrated data formed by combining current and historical system outputs and inputs before quantization, thereby eliminating the need for the traditional independence assumption on the quantized data. It is proven that the remote estimate achieves asymptotic normality, and the error covariance matrix converges to the original CRLB, confirming its asymptotic efficiency. Compared to existing identification algorithms under one-bit data, this method reduces the asymptotic mean squared error by at least 36%. Several numerical examples are simulated to show the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [122] [Gradient-robustness in optimization subject to stationary Navier-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12028)
*Constanze Neutsch,Winnifried Wollner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 讨论非线性不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程模拟及其最优控制问题的梯度鲁棒离散化方法，比较不同等价连续问题表述对离散化和伴随方程的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非线性不可压缩Navier-Stokes问题模拟及其最优控制中梯度鲁棒离散化的重要性，探讨不同连续问题表述如何影响离散化方案和伴随方程的计算

Method: 考虑非线性不可压缩Navier-Stokes问题的多种等价连续表述，比较它们的梯度鲁棒离散化方法，分析不同表述对最优控制问题中伴随方程梯度计算的影响

Result: 展示了不同连续问题表述对梯度鲁棒离散化方案的影响，并分析了这些表述如何影响最优控制问题中伴随方程的构造和梯度计算

Conclusion: 非线性不可压缩Navier-Stokes问题的最优控制中，连续问题的表述选择对梯度鲁棒离散化和伴随方程的构造具有重要影响，需要根据具体应用场景选择合适的表述形式

Abstract: In this article, we discuss gradient robust discretizations for the simulation of non-linear incompressible Navier-Stokes problem and the optimal control of such flow. We consider several formulations of the flow problem that are equivalent for the continuous non-linear forward problem and compare their gradient robust discretization. We will then discuss the influence of the chosen formulation on the adjoint equations needed for gradient computation in the solution of the optimal control problem.

</details>


### [123] [Well-posedness of boundary control systems and application to ISS for coupled heat equations with boundary disturbances and delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12029)
*Yassine El Gantouh,Jun Zheng,Guchuan Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究边界控制系统的解存在性和适定性，建立了保证解对初始数据和L^p输入连续依赖的显式可验证条件，并应用于边界耦合热方程的指数输入到状态稳定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究边界控制系统的适定性问题，特别是解对初始数据和输入的连续依赖性，这对于控制系统分析和设计至关重要。现有方法在处理无界控制/观测算子和L^p输入时存在局限性。

Method: 提出了一种新的有界性估计技术，用于线性时不变无限维系统（具有无界控制和观测算子）的输入/输出映射。该证明基于这一核心估计，并将其应用于边界耦合热方程的具体分析。

Result: 建立了保证解存在性和适定性的显式可验证条件，特别是连续依赖初始数据和L^p输入的条件。将该技术应用于边界耦合热方程，推导出具有边界扰动和时滞的系统的指数输入到状态稳定性具体条件。

Conclusion: 论文发展了一种处理无界控制/观测算子的新估计技术，为边界控制系统提供了实用的适定性判据，并在热方程应用中展示了该方法的有效性，为更广泛的控制系统分析提供了工具。

Abstract: This paper studies the existence of solutions and, in particular, the well-posedness of a class of boundary control systems. Our main result provides explicit and verifiable conditions on the system data that guarantee continuous dependence of solutions on the initial data and $L^p$-inputs. The proof relies on a new boundedness estimate for the input/output maps of linear time-invariant infinite-dimensional systems with unbounded control and observation operators. The developed technique is applied to derive specific conditions for the exponential input-to-state stability of boundary-coupled heat equations with boundary disturbances and time-delays.

</details>


### [124] [Controlled Swarm Gradient Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12039)
*Louison Aubert*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将受控模拟退火框架扩展到群体梯度动力学，证明其不变测度收敛到全局极小值，构建速度场实现精确退火曲线跟踪，理论上可实现任意快速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展受控模拟退火框架到群体梯度动力学，这类朗之万型平均场扩散的噪声强度依赖于过程的边际密度。旨在构建能够精确跟踪退火曲线的受控过程，实现向全局极小值的快速收敛。

Method: 首先分析群体梯度动力学的不变概率密度，证明当逆温度参数趋于无穷时，不变测度弱收敛到全局极小值集上的概率测度。然后给定任意非降冷却计划，证明存在速度场求解与不变密度曲线相关的连续性方程。将该速度场叠加到群体梯度动力学上，得到边际定律精确遵循预定退火曲线的受控过程。

Result: 证明了受控群体动力学向全局极小值收敛，理论上收敛速率可以任意快，完全由冷却计划的选择决定。讨论了受控动力学的算法实现，并与受控模拟退火进行了性能比较，指出了某些数值局限性。

Conclusion: 成功将受控模拟退火框架扩展到群体梯度动力学，构建了能够精确跟踪退火曲线的受控过程，理论上可实现任意快速收敛到全局极小值，为全局优化问题提供了新的理论框架和算法思路。

Abstract: We consider the global optimization of a non-convex potential $U : \mathbb{R}^d \to \mathbb{R}$ and extend the controlled simulated annealing framework introduced by Molin et al. (2026) to the class of swarm gradient dynamics, a family of Langevin-type mean-field diffusions whose noise intensity depends locally on the marginal density of the process. Building on the time-homogeneous model of Huang and Malik (2025), we first analyze its invariant probability density and show that, as the inverse temperature parameter tends to infinity, it converges weakly to a probability measure supported on the set of global minimizers of $U$. This result justifies using this family of invariant measures as an annealing curve in a controlled swarm setting. Given an arbitrary non-decreasing cooling schedule, we then prove the existence of a velocity field solving the continuity equation associated with the curve of invariant densities. Superimposing this field onto the swarm gradient dynamics yields a well-posed controlled process whose marginal law follows exactly the prescribed annealing curve. As a consequence, the controlled swarm dynamics converges toward global minimizers with, in principle, arbitrarily fast convergence rates, entirely dictated by the choice of the cooling schedule. Finally, we discuss an algorithmic implementation of the controlled dynamics and compare its performance with controlled simulated annealing, highlighting some numerical limitations.

</details>


### [125] [Expanding Flow Shop Tasks Based on Recursive Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12077)
*Boris Kupriyanov,Alexander Lazarev,Alexandr Roschin,Frank Werner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出了流水车间调度问题的递归表示扩展，展示了递归函数如何统一描述多种扩展问题，包括机器相关函数、加工时间调整函数和调度可行性控制函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流水车间调度问题存在多种扩展形式，传统方法需要为每种扩展单独建模。作者旨在开发一种统一的递归表示框架，能够简洁地描述多种扩展问题，提高问题建模的灵活性和通用性。

Method: 提出基于递归函数的统一建模框架，考虑六种扩展类型。使用三类递归函数：机器相关函数、加工时间调整函数和调度可行性控制函数。分析这些函数的叠加结构，用递归函数描述目标函数，制定递归函数的一般要求和性质。

Result: 成功建立了流水车间调度问题的递归表示扩展框架，能够统一描述多种扩展问题。通过简单流水车间扩展和分支定界优化的实例，展示了新问题的公式化能力。

Conclusion: 递归函数为流水车间调度问题的扩展提供了统一的建模框架，能够简洁地描述多种复杂扩展，为调度问题的公式化和优化提供了灵活的工具。

Abstract: The paper discusses several extensions of the recursive representation of the flow shop scheduling problem. It is shown that recursive functions make it possible to describe multiple extensions in a single problem. The paper considers altogether six extensions. The examples consider three types of recursive functions: functions associated with the machine, functions that adjust the procession time based on constraints, and functions that control the feasibility of the schedule. The structure of the superpositions of these functions is presented, and also descriptions of several objective functions by recursive functions are presented. Then the general requirements for a recursive function are formulated and its properties are described. Finally, a demonstration of the formulation of new problems is provided using examples of simple flow shop extensions and branch and bound optimization.

</details>


### [126] [Asymptotic Convergence of the Frank-Wolfe Algorithm for Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12104)
*Matthew Hough*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文分析了Frank-Wolfe算法在单调C¹算子下求解变分不等式问题的收敛性，通过连续时间插值和动力系统理论证明了算法的全局收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Frank-Wolfe算法在单调变分不等式问题中的收敛行为，特别是解决Hammond关于广义虚构博弈收敛性的猜想。

Method: 采用连续时间插值方法将离散迭代转化为连续动力系统，利用动力系统理论分析渐近行为，从而推导离散算法的收敛结果。

Result: 证明了在单调C¹算子下：1) 迭代的所有聚点都是变分不等式的解；2) 迭代到解集的距离收敛到零；3) Frank-Wolfe间隙渐近消失；4) 强单调情况下解唯一且迭代收敛到该解。

Conclusion: Frank-Wolfe算法在单调变分不等式问题中具有全局收敛性，这为广义虚构博弈的收敛性提供了理论证明，解决了Hammond的猜想。

Abstract: We consider the Frank-Wolfe algorithm for solving variational inequalities over compact, convex sets under a monotone $C^1$ operator and vanishing, nonsummable step sizes. We introduce a continuous-time interpolation of the discrete iteration and use tools from dynamical systems theory to analyze its asymptotic behavior. This allows us to derive convergence results for the original discrete algorithm. Consequently, every cluster point of the iterates is a solution of the underlying variational inequality, the distance from the iterates to the solution set converges to zero, and the Frank-Wolfe gap vanishes asymptotically. In the strongly monotone case, the solution is unique and the iterates converge to it. In particular, this proves Hammond's conjecture on the convergence of generalized fictitious play.

</details>


### [127] [Folding Mixed-Integer Linear Programs and Reflection Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12136)
*Rolf van der Hulst*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了DRCR方法，从两方面处理对称性：支持反射对称性（推广置换对称性），并应用于混合整数线性规划的连续变量。通过仿射全幺模分解减少整数变量，实验证明DRCR能有效提升求解效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性对分支定界和线性优化算法性能有负面影响。现有DRCR方法仅处理置换对称性，本文旨在扩展其能力以处理更广泛的对称类型，并应用于混合整数线性规划。

Method: 1. 扩展DRCR支持反射对称性（置换对称性的推广）；2. 将DRCR应用于混合整数线性规划的连续变量；3. 使用仿射全幺模分解重新表述问题以减少整数变量数量。

Result: 在MIPLIB 2017测试集上使用SCIP 10的实验表明：1. 对于线性规划松弛，带反射对称性的DRCR相比原始DRCR有适度的时间减少；2. 对于混合整数线性规划模型，DRCR相比SCIP默认配置显著减少求解时间；3. DRCR检测算法快速且可扩展到大问题实例。

Conclusion: DRCR是处理对称性的有效工具，扩展后的方法能处理更广泛的对称类型，并在混合整数线性规划中显著提升求解效率，检测算法具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: For mixed-integer linear programming and linear programming it is well known that symmetries can have a negative impact on the performance of branch-and-bound and linear optimization algorithms. A common strategy to handle symmetries in linear programs is to reduce the dimension of the linear program by aggregating symmetric variables and solving a linear program of reduced dimension. In their work ``Dimension Reduction via Color Refinement'' (DRCR), Grohe, Kersting, Mladenov and Selman show that it is sufficient to run a fast color refinement algorithm to detect permutation symmetries and reduce the dimension of the linear program. We extend DRCR in two directions. First, we show that DRCR can be extended to reflection symmetries, which generalize permutation symmetries. Second, we show the folklore result that DRCR can be applied to the continuous columns of mixed-integer linear programs. In order to derive additional reductions on the integer variables we use affine totally unimodular decompositions to reformulate mixed-integer linear programs into mixed-integer linear programs with fewer integer variables. Computational experiments on MIPLIB 2017 collection set using SCIP 10 show that DRCR is an effective tool for handling symmetries. For the linear programming relaxations, DRCR with reflection symmetries yields a modest reduction in running time compared to the original DRCR procedure. For mixed-integer linear programming models, DRCR is very effective at reducing the solution time compared to the default configuration of SCIP. Moreover, the developed DRCR detection algorithms are fast and scale well to large problem instances.

</details>


### [128] [Forecasting and Manipulating the Forecasts of Others](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12140)
*Sam Babichenko*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文首次精确刻画了具有内生信号的有限玩家连续时间LQG博弈的均衡，通过将信念层次结构简化为确定性双时间核，无需截断或大群体极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有私人信息的战略环境中，评估政策变化需要预测均衡响应。但当行动重塑对手信号时，每个智能体的最优响应依赖于信念的无限层次结构，这一问题四十年来一直难以精确分析。

Method: 采用基于原始布朗冲击而非物理状态的条件化方法（Harsanyi共同先验构造的动态类比），将信念层次结构坍缩为确定性双时间核，将纳什均衡简化为确定性不动点。

Result: 得到了显式的信息楔子$\mathcal{V}^i_t$（确定性Volterra过程），用于定价转移对手后验的边际价值。该楔子在信号外生于控制时恰好消失，正式划定了战略信念操纵重要的边界。

Conclusion: 提供了从信息原语到均衡结果的闭式映射，首次精确解决了具有内生信号的有限玩家连续时间LQG博弈的均衡分析问题。

Abstract: In strategic environments with private information, evaluating a change in policy requires predicting how the equilibrium responds -- but when actions reshape opponents' signals, each agent's optimal response depends on an infinite hierarchy of beliefs about beliefs that has resisted exact analysis for four decades. We provide the first exact equilibrium characterization of finite-player continuous-time LQG games with endogenous signals. Conditioning on primitive Brownian shocks rather than the physical state -- a dynamic analogue of Harsanyi's common-prior construction -- collapses the belief hierarchy onto deterministic two-time kernels, reducing Nash equilibrium to a deterministic fixed point with no truncation and no large-population limit. The characterization yields an explicit information wedge $\mathcal{V}^i_t$ -- a deterministic Volterra process -- that prices the marginal value of shifting opponents' posteriors. The wedge vanishes precisely when signals are exogenous to controls, formally delineating the boundary where strategic belief manipulation matters, and provides a closed-form mapping from information primitives to equilibrium outcomes.

</details>


### [129] [Operator Splitting, Policy Iteration, and Machine Learning for Stochastic Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12167)
*Alain Bensoussan,Thien P. B. Nguyen,Minh-Binh Tran,Son N. T. Tu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种分裂方法求解二阶Hamilton-Jacobi方程，将其分解为热方程步骤和纯一阶步骤，利用梯度值策略迭代算法实现高效的特征线机器学习方法，并建立了收敛率分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决二阶Hamilton-Jacobi方程的高效数值计算问题，传统方法在处理这类非线性偏微分方程时面临计算复杂度和收敛性挑战。通过分裂方法将问题分解为更易处理的子问题，结合机器学习技术提高计算效率。

Method: 采用分裂方法将二阶Hamilton-Jacobi方程分解为热方程步骤和纯一阶步骤。一阶步骤使用梯度值策略迭代算法实现，通过求解线性特征方程并最小化加权值梯度损失来学习值函数。该方法结合了特征线方法和机器学习技术。

Result: 建立了分裂方法的收敛率：对于Lipschitz初值数据，L∞误差下界为O(h)，上界为O(h^{1/7})；对于半凹数据改进为O(h^{1/5})；对于C²数据改进为O(h^{1/3})。在周期设置下，L¹误差上界为O(h^{1/2})。一阶步骤的加权L²误差分析显示指数收敛。数值结果稳定且准确。

Conclusion: 提出的分裂方法结合梯度值策略迭代算法为二阶Hamilton-Jacobi方程提供了高效稳定的数值解法，建立了严格的收敛理论，并通过机器学习技术实现了特征线方法的有效实现，为非线性偏微分方程的数值求解提供了新思路。

Abstract: We propose a splitting approach to solve the second-order Hamilton--Jacobi equation, reducing it to a heat step and a purely first-order step. The latter is implemented using a gradient value policy iteration algorithm, enabling efficient characteristic-based machine learning methods. We establish convergence rates for the splitting method. In particular, the $L^\infty$ error is bounded below by $\mathcal{O}(h)$ and above by $\mathcal{O}(h^{1/7})$ for Lipschitz initial data; this improves to $\mathcal{O}(h^{1/5})$ for semiconcave data and to $\mathcal{O}(h^{1/3})$ for $C^2$ data. We also prove an upper $L^1$ error estimate of order $\mathcal{O}(h^{1/2})$ in the periodic setting, where $h$ is the splitting step. For the first-order step, we provide a weighted $L^2$ error analysis that shows exponential convergence. Each iteration solves linear characteristic equations and learns the value function by minimizing a weighted value gradient loss. The approach yields stable and accurate numerical results.

</details>
